01 · Science Advances · 11 月 27 日新收录

circSRSF5-FUS-EIF4A3调控回路保护尤文肉瘤细胞免受EWS::FLI1小分子抑制

原始发布日期：2026-11-27

环状RNA（circRNAs）因其高稳定性、多样性以及调控多种生物过程的能力，已成为癌症研究的重点。本研究探讨了YK-4-279对尤文肉瘤（ES）中EWS::FLI1癌基因进行小分子抑制的影响。ES是一种罕见且高度侵袭性的儿童癌症，由破坏正常基因调控并促进肿瘤生长的融合蛋白驱动。当前的治疗依赖高强度化疗，伴有严重的长期副作用，这凸显了对靶向治疗的需求。一种前景良好的新方法利用小分子YK-4-279靶向EWS::FLI1与RNA解旋酶DHX9之间的相互作用；该分子显示出强效抗肿瘤作用，并已进入临床试验。转录组分析揭示，YK-4-279治疗后circRNA表达发生广泛变化，其中circSRSF5显著上调。功能和机制证据表明，circSRSF5通过稳定抗凋亡蛋白真核翻译起始因子4A3（EIF4A3）并促成治疗耐药，在肿瘤细胞存活中发挥关键作用。这些发现揭示了circRNAs，尤其是circSRSF5，在ES发病机制和治疗反应中此前未被识别的作用。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

The circSRSF5 -FUS-EIF4A3 regulatory circuitry protects Ewing sarcoma cells from EWS::FLI1 small-molecule inhibition

Circular RNAs (circRNAs) have become a key focus in cancer research due to their high stability, diversity, and ability to regulate various biological processes. This study explores the impact of small-molecule inhibition of EWS::FLI1 oncogene by YK-4-279 in Ewing sarcoma (ES). ES is a rare and highly aggressive childhood cancer driven by fusion proteins that disrupt normal gene regulation and promote tumor growth. Current treatments rely on intensive chemotherapy with serious long-term side effects, emphasizing the need for targeted therapies. A promising new approach targets the interaction between EWS::FLI1 and the RNA helicase DHX9 through the small molecule YK-4-279, which displays strong antitumor effects and has advanced to clinical trials. Transcriptome profiling revealed widespread changes in circRNA expression after YK-4-279 treatment, with a notable up-regulation of circSRSF5 . Functional and mechanistic evidence identifies a key role for circSRSF5 in tumor cell survival by stabilizing the anti-apoptotic protein eukaryotic translation initiation factor 4A3 (EIF4A3) and contributing to therapy resistance. These findings uncover a previously unidentified role for circRNAs, particularly circSRSF5 , in ES pathogenesis and treatment response.

02 · Science Advances · 11 月 27 日新收录

硒同位素揭示MORB地幔中深部板块构造循环的机制

原始发布日期：2026-11-27

数十亿年来，俯冲作用一直将洋壳循环带入地球内部，但深度俯冲物质如何演化并对地幔源熔体（如大洋中脊玄武岩（MORBs））作出贡献，仍未得到充分阐明。由于俯冲板片衍生的脱水流体通常会使残余榴辉岩中的硒（Se）同位素组成偏轻，而这种特征会在不发生进一步分馏的情况下传递至地幔熔体，因此Se可作为一种强有力的同位素示踪剂，用于示踪进入地幔的再循环组分。在此，我们给出了再循环地壳衍生的石榴辉石岩的高精度Se同位素数据，弥合了原始地幔与同位素组成较轻的MORB之间的差距。通过两阶段热力学建模，我们追踪了榴辉岩衍生熔体在对流地幔内的反应性渗透过程，该过程导致俯冲衍生的石榴辉石岩与其中保存的残余榴辉岩核同时形成，二者均在绝热减压期间对MORBs的地幔源作出贡献。我们的结果建立了一个岩石学上一致且经同位素验证的框架，用以追踪俯冲地壳的归宿及其在整个深部板块构造循环中对MORB地幔的长期印记。

查看原文标题与摘要

Selenium isotopes uncover the mechanism of the deep plate tectonic cycle in the MORB mantle

Subduction has recycled oceanic crust into Earth’s interior for billions of years, yet how deeply subducted material evolves and contributes to mantle-derived melts, such as mid-ocean ridge basalts (MORBs), remains poorly resolved. Because subducting slab-derived dehydration fluids typically produce isotopically light selenium (Se) in residual eclogites, which is transferred to mantle melts without further fractionation, Se serves as a powerful isotope-based tracer of recycled components into the mantle. Here, we present high-precision Se isotope data for recycled crust–derived garnet pyroxenites that bridge the gap between the primitive mantle and isotopically lighter MORB. Through two-stage thermodynamic modeling, we trace the reactive infiltration of eclogite-derived melts within the convective mantle, leading to the concurrent formation of subduction-derived garnet pyroxenites that preserve residual eclogite cores, both of which contribute to the mantle source of MORBs during adiabatic decompression. Our results establish a petrologically consistent and isotope-validated framework to trace the fate of subducted crust and its long-term imprint on the MORB mantle throughout the deep plate tectonic cycle.

03 · arXiv nucl-th · 11 月 27 日新收录

基于BSkG3的大规模裂变性质 I：裂变路径和自发裂变半衰期

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

我们给出了首项基于BSkG3能量密度泛函的大规模核裂变性质分析。基于该模型与基态观测量及锕系区域裂变数据前所未有的一致性，我们考察了近3300个满足$80\leq Z\leq118$的原子核，覆盖预测的质子滴线与中子滴线之间的区域。我们计算了数千个势能面，并明确考虑了三轴和八极形变。我们使用由BSkG3一致导出的微观集体惯性确定相应的最小作用量裂变路径，并在半经典WKB近似下计算自发裂变（SF）半衰期。我们的结果揭示了丰富多样的裂变路径拓扑，远远超出反应程序中通常采用的常规双峰图景；沿这些路径还出现了多种多样的核形状，凸显了仅探索一个集体自由度的局限性。特别是，三轴性不仅影响锕系区域的内势垒，也影响超重核若干区域的外势垒和单势垒。我们预测的SF半衰期所遵循的趋势与先前大规模研究相似，但在中子滴线附近出现了显著差异。所得数据集包括裂变势垒、SF半衰期和鞍点核能级密度，现已被纳入TALYS反应程序和BRUSLIB存储库。它为核反应计算和天体物理应用提供了微观基础，尤其适用于需要远离稳定谷原子核可靠裂变性质的快中子俘获模拟。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Large-scale fission properties with BSkG3 I : fission paths and spontaneous fission half-lives

We present our first large-scale analysis of nuclear fission properties based on the BSkG3 energy density functional. Building on the model's unprecedented agreement with ground state observables and fission data in the actinide region, we survey nearly 3300 nuclei with $80\leq Z\leq118$, spanning the region between the predicted proton and neutron drip lines. We calculate thousands of potential energy surfaces that explicitly account for triaxial and octupole deformations. We determine the corresponding least-action fission paths using microscopic collective inertias derived consistently from BSkG3 and calculate spontaneous fission (SF) half-lives within the semiclassical WKB approximation. Our results reveal a rich diversity of fission path topologies, extending well beyond the conventional two-humped picture commonly adopted in reaction codes, and a wide variety of nuclear shapes along these paths, highlighting the limitations of exploring only one collective degree of freedom. In particular, triaxiality affects not only inner barriers in the actinide region but also outer barriers and single barriers in several regions of the superheavy nuclei. Our predicted SF half-lives follow trends similar to those of previous large-scale studies, although significant differences emerge near the neutron drip line. The resulting dataset, including fission barriers, SF half-lives, and saddle-point nuclear level densities, is available has been incorporated into the TALYS reaction code and the BRUSLIB repository. It provides a microscopic foundation for nuclear reaction calculations and astrophysical applications, particularly rapid neutron-capture simulations, which require reliable fission properties of nuclei far from the valley of stability.

04 · arXiv nucl-th · 11 月 27 日新收录

电磁矩对核力的敏感性

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

核电磁矩可在整个核素图上得到精确测量，但理解它们如何由底层核力产生，仍是核理论面临的一项开放性挑战。利用从头算的全局模拟器，我们表明，与结合能和电荷半径不同，磁矩和四极矩能够探测手征相互作用中随中子数强烈变化的组分。我们通过钙同位素的性质展示了这种敏感性，其中测得的矩揭示了在整体观测量中很大程度上被隐藏的信息。随后，我们展示了一种处理逆问题的方法，即利用选定的实验磁矩约束并重新组织核力的允许参数空间，在保留对其他核性质描述的同时，改进校准集之外的预测。

查看原文标题与摘要

Sensitivity of Electromagnetic Moments to Nuclear Forces

Nuclear electromagnetic moments can be precisely measured across the nuclear chart, yet understanding how they emerge from the underlying nuclear force is an open challenge for nuclear theory. Using a global emulator of ab initio calculations, we show that, unlike binding energies and charge radii, magnetic and quadrupole moments probe components of the chiral interaction that vary strongly with neutron number. We demonstrate this sensitivity with the properties of calcium isotopes, where measured moments reveal information that is largely hidden from bulk observables. We then demonstrate a method to address the inverse problem, using selected experimental magnetic moments to constrain and reorganize the allowed parameter space of the nuclear force, improving predictions beyond the calibration set while preserving descriptions of other nuclear properties.

05 · arXiv nucl-th · 11 月 27 日新收录

中子和核 $\beta$ 衰变中 $\alpha^2$ 阶的 QED 修正

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

我们计算中子和核 β 衰变的双圈 QED 修正，包括完整的电子质量依赖性和实辐射贡献。中子的结果 $\delta_{R,\,\rm static}= ( 29.199 \pm 0.001 )\times 10^{-3}$ 表明，与此前在高能量/小电子质量极限 $m_e/(m_n-m_p) \to 0$ 下获得的结果相比，低能辐射修正的不确定度缩小至原来的约 $1/70 $。对于超允许核衰变，我们发现，与 $Z$ 无关的 $\alpha^2$ 阶修正处于 $(0.1-0.5) \times 10^{-4}$ 范围内，即大致为预期量级，并且与当前的不确定度估计相当。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

QED corrections to neutron and nuclear $\beta$-decay at order $\alpha^2$

We compute two-loop QED corrections to neutron and nuclear beta decay, including full electron mass dependence and real radiation contributions. The result for the neutron, $\delta_{R,\,\rm static}= ( 29.199 \pm 0.001 )\times 10^{-3}$, implies a factor $\sim 70 $ reduction in the uncertainty of the low-energy radiative corrections compared to previous results obtained in the large energy/small electron-mass limit, $m_e/(m_n-m_p) \to 0$. For superallowed nuclear decays we find that the $Z$-independent order $\alpha^2$ corrections range from $(0.1-0.5) \times 10^{-4}$ i.e., of roughly the expected size and comparable to current uncertainty estimates.

06 · arXiv nucl-th · 源文更新（相对 11 月 25 日）

基于 $\textit{从头算}$ 光学势的低能质子和中子在 He 靶上的弹性散射

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

我们构建并讨论用于 $^{3,4,6}$He 靶的低能 $\textit{从头算}$ 核子-原子核光学势。在本工作中，我们采用 $\textit{从头算}$ SA-NCSM/GF 方法，该方法将 $\textit{从头算}$ 对称性适配无芯壳模型与格林函数技术相结合以构建光学势，并将这一表述推广至质子散射和非零自旋靶。我们表明，这些光学势能够非常好地再现实验微分截面以及质子和中子弹性散射的相移。$\textit{从头算}$ SA-NCSM/GF 方法提供非局域、能量依赖且具有色散性的光学势，适用于天体物理学相关的低能区以及实验困难且往往缺乏数据的奇异核。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Proton and neutron elastic scattering at low energies on He targets from $\textit{ab initio}$ optical potentials

We construct and discuss $\textit{ab initio}$ nucleon-nucleus optical potentials at low energies for $^{3,4,6}$He targets. In this work, we use the $\textit{ab initio}$ SA-NCSM/GF approach that combines the $\textit{ab initio}$ symmetry-adapted no-core shell model with the Green's function technique to construct optical potentials, and extend this formulation to proton scattering and targets with nonzero spin. We show that these optical potentials reproduce experimental differential cross sections and phase shifts for proton and neutron elastic scattering remarkably well. The $\textit{ab initio}$ SA-NCSM/GF approach provides nonlocal, energy dependent and dispersive optical potentials, suitable for the astrophysically relevant regime of low energies and for exotic nuclei, where experiments are difficult and data is often unavailable.

07 · arXiv nucl-ex · 11 月 27 日新收录

ALICE 中 pO 和 OO 碰撞里 $\pi^0$ 产生的核修正

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

高动量部分子的能量损失是在重离子碰撞中形成的夸克--胶子等离子体的关键特征之一。通过测量核修正因子 $R_{\rm AA}$，即观测到高-$p_{\rm T}$ 粒子产额受到强烈抑制，已在 LHC 的 Pb--Pb 碰撞中证实了部分子能量损失。然而，在 pp 和 p--Pb 等小碰撞系统中，能量损失尚未得到证实。本文给出了 OO 和 pO 碰撞中 $\pi^0$ $R_{\rm AA}$ 的测量结果，其 $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}$ 分别为 $5.36$ TeV 和 $9.62$ TeV。这些结果基于使用 ALICE 电磁量能器测得的 OO、pO 和 pp 碰撞中的 $\pi^0$ $p_{\rm T}$ 谱。对 $R_{\rm pO}$ 的测量能够对冷核物质效应施加精确约束。结合 $R_{\rm OO}$，这可以分离冷核物质和热核物质的贡献，并能定量确定 OO 碰撞中的部分子能量损失。将这些测量结果与同时包含冷核物质效应和热核物质效应的理论模型计算进行了比较。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Nuclear Modification of $\pi^0$ Production in pO and OO Collisions with ALICE

Energy loss of high-momentum partons is one of the key signatures of the quark--gluon plasma formed in heavy-ion collisions. Through measurements of the nuclear modification factor, $R_{\rm AA}$, where a strong suppression of high-$p_{\rm T}$ particle yields is observed, parton energy loss has been confirmed in Pb--Pb collisions at the LHC. However, in small collision systems, like pp and p--Pb, energy loss has not yet been confirmed. In this contribution, a measurement of the $\pi^0$ $R_{\rm AA}$ in OO and pO collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}} = 5.36$ TeV and $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}} = 9.62$ TeV, respectively, is presented. These results are based on the measured $\pi^0$ $p_{\rm T}$ spectra in OO, pO, and pp collisions using the ALICE electromagnetic calorimeter. The measurement of the $R_{\rm pO}$ allows for precise constraints on cold-nuclear matter effects. In combination with the $R_{\rm OO}$, this allows for a separation of cold and hot nuclear matter contributions and enables a quantitative determination of partonic energy loss in OO collisions. The measurements are compared to theoretical model calculations that include both cold and hot nuclear matter effects.

08 · arXiv nucl-th · 11 月 27 日新收录

自共轭 94Ag 中高自旋同核异能态的直接质量测量

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

二十年前，有研究报道了 $^{94}$Ag 的一个 $(21^+)$ 同核异能态发生双质子发射。该报道至今仍是核素图上具有奇数个质子的原子核中可能存在双质子放射性的唯一实例。由于该同核异能态的质量未知，关于这种衰变模式是否可行的争论一直持续至今。在此，我们采用热腔捕集器中的态选择性激光电离，对 $^{94}$Ag 的长寿命同核异能态及其单质子衰变子体 $^{93}$Pd 进行了多反射飞行时间质谱测量。对 $(7^+)$ 和 $(21^+)$ 同核异能态的首次直接质量测量为独立讨论这些衰变的能量学奠定了基础。我们排除了此前所报道的 $(21^+)$ 态双质子发射，并揭示其单质子衰变能显著更低。最先进的壳模型计算再现了 $(7^+)$ 态与 $(21^+)$ 态之间的能隙以及 $(21^+)$ 态的半衰期，这意味着 $(21^+)$ 态很可能接近球形，与所报道的双质子放射性解释相矛盾。重要的是，所采用的技术是迈向首次对质子发射态开展激光光谱研究的一块基石。

查看原文标题与摘要

Direct mass measurements of the high-spin isomers in self-conjugate 94Ag

Two decades ago, two-proton emission from a $(21^+)$ isomeric state in $^{94}$Ag was reported. This report has remained the sole example of possible two-proton radioactivity in a nucleus with an odd number of protons on the chart of nuclei. A debate regarding the viability of the decay mode has been ongoing due to the unknown mass of the isomer. Here, using state-selective laser ionization in a hot cavity catcher, we present multi-reflection time-of-flight mass spectrometry of the long-lived isomeric states of $^{94}$Ag and its one-proton decay daughter $^{93}$Pd. For the first time, direct mass measurements of the $(7^+)$ and $(21^+)$ isomeric states set the stage for an independent discussion of the energetics of these decays. We rule out the two-proton emission from the $(21^+)$ state as previously reported and reveal the one-proton decay energy to be significantly lower. State-of-the-art shell-model calculations reproduce the energy gap of the $(7^+)$ and the $(21^+)$ states as well as the half-life of the $(21^+)$ state, implying the $(21^+)$ state is likely near-spherical, contradicting the reported explanation for the two-proton radioactivity. Importantly, the techniques employed are a stepping stone towards the first laser spectroscopic investigation of a proton-emitting state

09 · arXiv nucl-th · 11 月 27 日新收录

用于中子诱发裂变总动能分布的概率机器学习

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

发射后中子总动能（TKE）分布对入射中子能量的依赖性，特别是其在多机会裂变区间内的宽度，为裂变计算提供了一项灵敏检验。我们研究了裂变观测量通用描述（GEF）以及基于 GEF 训练的概率机器学习模型如何再现实测 TKE 分布，重点考察其在高入射中子能量下的宽度以及超出训练范围的外推。使用一个共同的计算数据集评估高斯过程回归（GPR）、带白噪声核的 GPR、混合密度网络（MDN）和贝叶斯混合密度网络（BMDN）。扩展的 MDN/BMDN 分析使用了来自 20 个靶原子核的 1,025 个 GEF 能谱和 60 个实测分布。将最高达到 20、40 和约 76 MeV 的训练范围，与延伸至 30 或 40 MeV、同时保持每个靶的能谱数量不变的相同规模对照进行比较。在 40 MeV 以上，相对于实验的 BMDN 宽度平均绝对误差从最高训练至 20 MeV 时的 5.63 MeV，降至两个相同规模的 30-MeV 对照的 2.70 和 2.53 MeV，以及相同规模的 40-MeV 对照的 1.77 MeV。相对于 GEF，在 40 MeV 以上的预测区间覆盖率从 20-MeV 训练范围的 52.3% 上升到完整训练范围的 92.3%。与实测平均 TKE 和宽度的一致性取决于靶，在 GEF、MDN 和 BMDN 之间不存在统一的优劣次序。结果表明，高能宽度对训练能量覆盖范围十分敏感，且在 30 MeV 时改善已很明显。GEF 宽度在高能区变化相对缓慢，限制了这些改善可归因于学习非平凡能量依赖性的程度。充分的训练能量覆盖对于再现计算得到的高能演化至关重要，而与实验的一致性仍受底层裂变模型的制约。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Probabilistic Machine Learning for Total Kinetic Energy Distributions in Neutron-Induced Fission

The incident-neutron-energy dependence of post-neutron total-kinetic-energy (TKE) distributions, particularly their width in the multichance-fission regime, provides a sensitive test of fission calculations. We investigate how the General Description of Fission Observables (GEF) and probabilistic machine-learning models trained on GEF reproduce measured TKE distributions, emphasizing their width at high incident-neutron energies and extrapolation beyond the training range. Gaussian process regression (GPR), GPR with a white-noise kernel, a mixture density network (MDN), and a Bayesian mixture density network (BMDN) are evaluated on a common calculated dataset. An extended MDN/BMDN analysis uses 1,025 GEF spectra from 20 target nuclei and 60 measured distributions. Training ranges of up to 20, 40, and approximately 76 MeV are compared with matched-size controls extending to 30 or 40 MeV while preserving the number of spectra per target. Above 40 MeV, the BMDN width mean absolute error relative to experiment decreases from 5.63 MeV for training up to 20 MeV to 2.70 and 2.53 MeV for two matched-size 30-MeV controls and 1.77 MeV for the matched-size 40-MeV control. Predictive-interval coverage relative to GEF above 40 MeV rises from 52.3% to 92.3% between the 20-MeV and full training ranges. Agreement with measured mean TKE and widths is target dependent, with no uniform ordering among GEF, MDN, and BMDN. The results demonstrate the sensitivity of high-energy widths to training-energy coverage, with improvements already evident at 30 MeV. The comparatively slow variation of GEF widths at high energies limits the extent to which these improvements can be attributed to learning nontrivial energy dependence. Adequate training-energy coverage is critical for reproducing the calculated high-energy evolution, while agreement with experiment remains constrained by the underlying fission model.

采用 Skyrme 和 Fayans 泛函的自洽方法中 Ca 原子核电荷半径的剖析

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

已知 $^{40-48}$Ca 原子核的电荷半径具有反常的 $N$ 依赖性。尽管多数自洽计算难以再现这一现象，Fayans 泛函能够再现 $^{42,44,46}$Ca 中的抛物线行为，但它往往会高估缺中子 $^{38}$Ca 的电荷半径。我们通过将其分解为径向贡献，研究 Fayans 泛函所得 $N$ 依赖性的起源，并将其追溯至单粒子势和中子对密度。将结果与被视为常规泛函代表的 Skyrme 泛函 SkM$^\ast$ 的结果进行比较。研究发现，在 Fayans 泛函的结果中，$^{42,44,46}$Ca 的抛物线行为与 $^{38}$Ca 电荷半径的错误增强具有共同的起源。要彻底解决与 $^{42,44,46}$Ca 抛物线型电荷半径有关的谜题，同时再现缺中子区域至关重要。

查看原文标题与摘要

Anatomy of charge radii of Ca nuclei in self-consistent approaches with Skyrme and Fayans functionals

It has been known that the $^{40-48}$Ca nuclei have anomalous $N$-dependence of their charge radii. While difficult to reproduce in most self-consistent calculations, the parabolic behavior in $^{42,44,46}$Ca is reproduced with the Fayans functional, although it tends to overestimate the charge radii at the neutron-deficient $^{38}$Ca. We investigate the origin of the $N$-dependence with the Fayans functional by decomposing it into radial contributions, and trace it back to the single-particle potentials and neutron pair densities. The results are compared with those of a Skyrme functional SkM$^\ast$, which is considered a representative of the usual functionals. It is found that, in the results of the Fayans functional, the origin is shared between the parabolic behavior in $^{42,44,46}$Ca and the erroneous enhancement of the charge radii at $^{38}$Ca. To fully resolve the puzzle with respect to the parabolic charge radii in $^{42,44,46}$Ca, it is vital to reproduce the neutron-deficient region simultaneously.

$\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 3$-$39$ GeV 下 Au+Au 碰撞中横动量关联的平衡基线：守恒定律、共振衰变、量子统计和集体流

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

STAR 在 Au+Au 碰撞中测得的逐事件横动量关联呈现非单调的能量依赖性，在 $\sqrt{s_{NN}} \approx 5$-$8$ GeV 附近达到最小值，并朝 $3$ GeV 急剧上升。在将这些特征解释为软化的状态方程或 QCD 临界点之前，必须确立平衡强子共振气体所给出的基线。在此，我们针对 0-5% 和 30-40% 碰撞，计算了从 $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 3$ 到 $39$ GeV 热平衡和化学平衡下的双粒子关联量 $\langle \Delta p_{T,i}\Delta p_{T,j} \rangle$，其中纳入了共振衰变、Bose-Einstein 统计、Bjorken 纵向流和横向径向流，以及能量、重子数、电荷、奇异数和横动量的全局守恒。通过微正则系综的鞍点展开施加守恒定律，可将任意可加观测量的方差表示为巨正则协方差的 Schur 补。尽管平衡气体经历了从重子主导物质到介子主导物质的跃迁，但组分涨落抵消了在逐事件固定各粒子种类多重数时产生的较大负“混合”项，仅留下小得多的负守恒残余。在 $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 11.5$-$39$ GeV 范围内，具有强流的平衡基线对 0-5% 碰撞中的数据低估至其 $3$-$4$ 分之一，对 30-40% 碰撞中的数据低估至其 $\approx 6.5$ 分之一；在固定靶能量（$3$-$5.2$ GeV）下，数据最大且为正，而基线转为负值。若通过固定能量下的径向流涨落来解释数据，则中心碰撞需要 $\sigma_\beta/\beta_s \approx 6$-$10\%$，30-40% 对撞机能量碰撞需要 $\approx 22\%$，其随中心度降低而增长的速度快于独立源标度。

查看原文标题与摘要

Equilibrium baseline for transverse-momentum correlations in Au+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 3$-$39$ GeV: Conservation laws, resonance decays, quantum statistics, and collective flow

Event-by-event transverse-momentum correlations measured by STAR in Au+Au collisions exhibit a nonmonotonic energy dependence with a minimum near $\sqrt{s_{NN}} \approx 5$-$8$ GeV and a steep rise toward $3$ GeV. Before interpreting these features in terms of a softened equation of state or a QCD critical point, the baseline from an equilibrated hadron resonance gas must be established. Here we compute the two-particle correlator $\langle \Delta p_{T,i}\Delta p_{T,j} \rangle$ in thermal and chemical equilibrium from $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 3$ to $39$ GeV for 0-5% and 30-40% collisions, incorporating resonance decays, Bose-Einstein statistics, Bjorken longitudinal and transverse radial flow, and global conservation of energy, baryon number, electric charge, strangeness, and transverse momentum. Imposing conservation laws via a saddle-point expansion of the microcanonical ensemble yields the variance of any additive observable as a Schur complement of grand-canonical covariances. Although the equilibrium gas undergoes the transition from baryon- to meson-dominated matter, composition fluctuations cancel the large negative "mixing" term that arises when species multiplicities are fixed event by event, leaving only a much smaller negative conservation residual. Across $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 11.5$-$39$ GeV, the equilibrium baseline with strong flow underpredicts the data by a factor of $3$-$4$ in 0-5% and $\approx 6.5$ in 30-40% collisions; at fixed-target energies ($3$-$5.2$ GeV), where the data are largest and positive, the baseline turns negative. Accounting for the data via radial-flow fluctuations at fixed energy requires $\sigma_\beta/\beta_s \approx 6$-$10\%$ in central and $\approx 22\%$ in 30-40% collider-energy collisions, growing faster with decreasing centrality than independent-source scaling.

RF 驱动负离子源中回流正离子的实验与数值研究

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

摘要 回流正离子（BSPI）是高功率负离子型中性束注入系统中不可避免的副产物，其引起的显著热负荷和材料削弱对离子源的长脉冲运行稳定性构成了严峻挑战。本文对 CRAFT 的 HONOR 测试设施中双射频（RF）驱动器负离子源内 BSPI 的产生、空间分布和热效应进行了全面的实验与数值研究。利用水流量热诊断系统，系统研究了 BSPI 功率份额（ P BSPI / P beam ）对关键运行参数的依赖性。结果表明，P BSPI / P beam 比值随源充气压力单调增大，从 0.3 Pa 时的 ∼2.05% 上升到 0.57 Pa 时的 ∼3.15%。观察到该比值对束流能量具有非单调依赖性，在 161 keV 时达到 ∼2.65% 的峰值。这一趋势归因于两种效应之间的竞争：中等能量下束流发散度减小使 BSPI 的几何传输效率得到提高，而较高能量下碰撞截面则发生内在减小。此外，还发现该比值随引出束流电流的增大而降低，这可由空间电荷引起的束流发散来解释，这种发散降低了束流通过加速栅极的几何传输效率。背板上明显的 BSPI 轰击痕迹呈现出与等离子体栅极孔相对应的阵列图案。结合实测热负荷的热-流体-固体耦合模拟表明，在典型高功率条件（190 kV, 16.2 A）下，背板最高温度仍远低于材料安全极限。本工作阐明了控制 BSPI 产生的关键机制，并验证了背板设计的热可靠性，为高功率负离子型中性束注入（NNBI）系统的研制提供了坚实基础。

Experimental and numerical investigation of backstreaming positive ions in an RF-driven negative ion source

Abstract Backstreaming positive ions (BSPIs) are an unavoidable byproduct in high-power negative ion-based neutral beam injection systems, posing a critical challenge to the long-pulse operational stability of ion sources due to the significant heat loads and material erosion they induce. This paper presents a comprehensive experimental and numerical investigation into the generation, spatial distribution, and thermal effects of BSPIs in the dual-radio-frequency (RF)-driver negative ion source at the HONOR test facility of CRAFT. Using a water-flow calorimetry diagnostic system, the dependence of the BSPI power fraction ( P BSPI / P beam ) on key operational parameters was systematically studied. The results show that the P BSPI / P beam ratio increases monotonically with the source filling pressure, rising from ∼2.05% at 0.3 Pa to ∼3.15% at 0.57 Pa. A non-monotonic dependence on beam energy is observed, with the ratio peaking at ∼2.65% at 161 keV. This trend is attributed to the competition between the improved geometric transmission efficiency of BSPIs—resulting from reduced beam divergence at moderate energies—and the intrinsic decrease in collision cross-sections at higher energies. Furthermore, the ratio is found to decrease with increasing extracted beam current, which is explained by the space-charge-induced beam divergence that reduces the geometric transmission efficiency through the acceleration grids. Distinct BSPI bombardment traces on the backplate exhibit an array pattern corresponding to the plasma grid apertures. Coupled thermal-fluid-solid simulations, incorporating the measured heat loads, indicate that under typical high-power conditions (190 kV, 16.2 A), the maximum backplate temperature remains well below the material safety limit. This work clarifies the key mechanisms governing BSPI generation and validates the thermal reliability of the backplate design, providing a solid basis for the development of high-power negative-ion-based neutral beam injection (NNBI) systems.

JT-60SA首次运行阶段的约束特性

摘要 在JT-60SA中，已评估1 MA氢放电的全局能量约束时间τ E为0.1 – 0.2 s，并与ITERL-96P(th)定标进行了比较。由于首次运行阶段无法获得离子温度T i、电子密度和有效电荷的局部测量结果，并且放电次数及其参数范围有限，因此目前的结果不应被视为定论。随着加热功率增大，τ E趋于按照与该定标相同的趋势缩短。τ E短于该定标的结果，但随密度增大而接近该定标。对于0.4–0.6 MW欧姆加热等离子体，τ E随碰撞性增大而增加并达到饱和。对于0.6–0.8 MW欧姆加热和电子回旋共振加热（ECH）等离子体，τ E持续增加至更高的碰撞性并达到该定标的结果。采用俘获旋转Landau-流体模型并假设线性T i剖面的湍流模拟表明，主导湍流模为俘获电子模。在0.4–0.6 MW欧姆加热等离子体中，线性增长率随碰撞性增大而降低，并在较低水平达到饱和，这表明发生了从线性欧姆约束区到饱和欧姆约束区的跃迁。对于0.6–0.8 MW欧姆加热和ECH等离子体，饱和出现在更高的碰撞性下，表明线性约束区延伸至更高的碰撞性。通过密度反馈控制实验中的频率响应，还评估了不包括再循环贡献的全局粒子约束时间τ p，其值为0.301 ± 0.015 s，约为τ E的两倍。根据τ p和包括再循环的有效粒子约束时间τ p ∗，得到再循环系数为0.865 ± 0.013。小于一的再循环系数表明壁再循环不足以维持等离子体密度，这与连续注气条件下首次氢放电所导致的氢滞留一致。

查看原文标题与摘要

Confinement property in the JT-60SA first operation phase

Abstract In JT-60SA, the global energy confinement time τ E has been evaluated to be 0.1 – 0.2 s for 1 MA hydrogen discharges and compared with the ITERL-96P(th) scaling. Because local measurements of ion temperature T i , electron density and effective charge are not available in the first operation phase and the number of discharges and their parameter range are limited, the present results should not be considered definitive. As heating power increased, τ E tends to shorten in the same trend as the scaling. τ E is shorter than the scaling but approaches the scaling with increasing density. For 0.4–0.6 MW ohmic heating plasmas, τ E increases with increasing collisionality and saturates. For 0.6–0.8 MW ohmic heating and electron cyclotron resonance heating (ECH) plasmas, τ E continues to increase up to higher collisionality and reaches the scaling. The turbulence simulations with the trapped gyroLandau-fluid model assuming a linear T i profile show that the dominant turbulence mode is trapped electron modes. The linear growth rate decreases with increasing collisionality and saturates at a low level in the 0.4–0.6 MW ohmic heating plasma, suggesting the transition from the linear ohmic confinement regime to the saturated ohmic confinement regime. For 0.6–0.8 MW ohmic heating and ECH plasmas, the saturation occurs at higher collisionality, indicating that the linear confinement regime extends to higher collisionality. The global particle confinement time excluding recycling contributions, τ p , has also been evaluated to be 0.301 ± 0.015 s, approximately twice τ E , from the frequency responses in a density feedback control experiment. From τ p and the effective particle confinement time including recycling τ p ∗ , a recycling coefficient is obtained as 0.865 ± 0.013 . The sub-unity recycling coefficient indicates that wall recycling is insufficient to maintain plasma density, consistent with hydrogen retention due to the first hydrogen discharge with continuous gas injection.

区域光学势的贝叶斯校准及复合核反应的量化不确定度预测

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

经过不确定度量化的全局光学势参数化可用于在整个核素图上开展系统研究，并对远离稳定线的区域进行预测。尽管这些参数化通常能很好地描述用于校准它们的反应，但在特定情况下，预测可能出现显著偏差，且外推会带来很大的不确定度。我们提出了一种适用于核素版图特定区域的区域光学势。我们沿同位素链校准该势，从而同时实现充分的数据覆盖并提高对相邻同位素的外推能力。我们对Chapel Hill光学势以及Pruitt等人先前使用的统计模型进行了修改，以利用更完善的数据覆盖，对核子与Zr同位素的弹性散射开展贝叶斯分析。我们将Chapel Hill区域势（CHiRP）的参数分布传播至包括(n, g)和(n, 2n)在内的复合核反应，这些反应与核技术应用相关。将结果与Chapel Hill势的全局校准CHUQ进行了比较。CHiRP与CHUQ的差异尤其体现在其虚部的能量依赖性和径向依赖性上。对于弹性散射，使用CHiRP所得可观测量的不确定度小于使用CHUQ所得的不确定度。在与10 MeV附近及以上能区数据的符合程度方面，CHiRP相较其全局对应模型也有所改进，而区域方法和全局方法与更高能量及更重靶核校准数据的符合程度相近。本文还报告了将CHiRP和CHUQ的不确定度传播通过复合核反应道时，二者的相对表现。对于需要核素图某个子区域内精确信息的应用，区域势方法是全局光学模型参数化的一种可行替代方案。

Bayesian calibration of a regional optical potential and uncertainty-quantified predictions for compound nucleus reactions

Uncertainty-quantified global optical potential parameterizations are useful for making systematic studies across the nuclear chart and making predictions away from stability. While they generally provide a good description of the reactions on which they were calibrated, predictions can deviate strongly in specific cases and extrapolations incur large uncertainties. We propose a regional optical potential suited for a specific region of the nuclear landscape. We calibrate the potential along isotopic chains to enable both strong data coverage and improved extrapolative power to neighboring isotopes. We modify the Chapel Hill optical potential and the statistical model used previously by Pruitt et al. to perform a Bayesian analysis of elastic scattering of nucleons from Zr isotopes with improved data coverage. The parameter distributions of our Chapel Hill Regional Potential (CHiRP) are propagated to compound nucleus reactions including (n, g) and (n, 2n), which have relevance to nuclear technology applications. Results are compared to CHUQ, a global calibration of the Chapel Hill potential. CHiRP differs from CHUQ particularly in the energy and radial dependence of its imaginary components. For elastic scattering, the uncertainties on the observables using CHiRP are smaller than those using CHUQ. CHiRP also provides an improvement over its global counterpart in the agreement with data around and above 10 MeV, while the regional and global approaches offer similar levels of agreement to the higher energy and heavier target calibration data. The relative performance of CHiRP and CHUQ when propagating their uncertainty through compound nucleus reaction channels is also reported. The regional potential approach is a viable alternative to global optical model parameterizations for applications that require precise information within a sub-region of the nuclear chart.

Light-by-light scattering: asymptotic expansions, Coulomb resummation and NLO corrections

Light-by-light (LbL) scattering is one of the earliest predictions of quantum electrodynamics (QED). Interest in this process has been renewed following its experimental observation at the LHC and the prospects of future measurements at free-electron laser facilities. In this paper, we refine theoretical predictions for LbL scattering by improving the full fermion-mass-dependent two-loop QCD and QED helicity amplitudes using high- and low-energy asymptotic expansions, and by performing Coulomb resummation in the threshold region. The $W$-boson contribution, relevant when the di-photon invariant mass exceeds twice the $W$-boson mass, is included at one-loop accuracy. We present state-of-the-art predictions for LbL cross sections in the Standard Model and provide a new event generator, LbLatNLO, for Monte Carlo simulations of LbL scattering.

Harder, Better, Faster, Stronger: The $\Delta(1232)$ Resonance in Supernova QCD Axion Emission

The high-energy tail of supernova axion emission controls searches whose response grows with axion energy. We identify a novel production channel, in which the axion is emitted through an intermediate $\Delta(1232)$ resonance, which adds a hard component to the spectrum. We calculate the axion production rate from nucleon-nucleon Bremsstrahlung in chiral effective field theory through next-to-leading order. Including the $\Delta(1232)$ roughly doubles the luminosity at one second after bounce and increases the number of escaping axions above $200$ MeV by a factor of about $3$ over the first nine seconds, relative to the nucleon-only calculation. Adding this new production channel roughly doubles $\gamma$-ray counts from conversion in the progenitor's magnetic field and increases water-Cherenkov counts by about an order of magnitude through its combined effects on emission and absorption. The same hard component can mimic a higher core temperature or thermal-pion emission in $\gamma$-ray spectral fits. The $\Delta(1232)$ resonance is therefore crucial for predicting supernova axion signals and interpreting their spectra.

Microscopic Approach to Neutron Elastic and Inelastic Cross Sections

Depending on the nuclei involved and the beam energies, reaction timescales differ tremendously. Today, distinct models are often used to describe each timescale, relying on phenomenological inputs, such as level densities or partial-widths, which limits their predictive power. This work aims to address this issue by providing a unified description of low-energy neutron elastic and inelastic reaction channels starting from microscopic predictions of the structure of mid-mass nuclei. The goal is to build a framework that is general enough to span the different reaction regimes while remaining anchored in microscopic theory. For illustration purposes, this work focuses on reactions $n+^{20}$Ne to the elastic and inelastic channels. The S-matrix is build from shell model predictions of resonances states and their spectroscopic amplitudes for $^{21}$Ne, using two different approximations of the single-particle asymptotics. Then, the shell-model predictions for the level density and single-particle partial widths are analyzed and the final cross sections are compared with evaluated ones. This method qualitatively reproduces the ENDF evaluation for the $^{20}$Ne$(n,el)$ and $^{20}$Ne$(n,n')$ cross sections. The present study also compares the Shannon entropy obtained from this model to the Gaussian Orthogonal Ensemble (GOE). In the future, the framework will be extended to include more complex reaction channels, such as two-nucleon and gamma channels, enabling more accurate predictions of neutron-induced reactions such as those of astrophysical interest.

Lindblad驱动的X(3872)四夸克态重组

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

奇异介子X(3872)的内部结构仍是一个悬而未决的问题。我们在其为紧致四夸克态的假设下，研究其在重离子碰撞中的产生。为此，我们从Lindblad方程推导出一个聚结模型，假设未束缚的重夸克在夸克-胶子等离子体内达到热化，并且绝热近似成立。利用该模型，我们预测了LHC能量下X(3872)的核修正因子，其中质子-质子基准截面根据现有实验数据估算。我们还考虑了简化假设对模型的影响，以及一种基于化学平衡的互补方法。我们的结果表明，重组是四夸克态X(3872)的主导产生机制。它使重离子碰撞中的产额显著增强，表明核修正因子是探测该态奇异性质的有力可观测量。

查看原文标题与摘要

Lindblad-driven recombination of the X(3872) tetraquark

The internal structure of the exotic meson X(3872) remains an open question. We investigate its production in heavy-ion collisions under the hypothesis that it is a compact tetraquark. To this end, we derive a coalescence model from the Lindblad equation, assuming that unbound heavy quarks are thermalized within the quark-gluon plasma and that the adiabatic approximation holds. Using this model, we predict the nuclear modification factor of the X(3872) at LHC energies, with proton-proton baseline cross sections estimated from available experimental data. We also consider the effect of simplifying assumptions on the model, and a complementary approach based on chemical equilibration. Our results indicate that recombination is the dominant production mechanism for a tetraquark X(3872). It leads to a significant yield enhancement in heavy-ion collisions, suggesting that the nuclear modification factor is a powerful observable for probing the exotic nature of this state

在TRENTo-Sea模型中引入部分子层级涨落

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

查看原文标题与摘要

Introducing Parton-Level Fluctuations in the TRENTo-Sea Model

A variety of experimental and theoretical data suggest that initial condition models for heavy-ion collisions contain a degree of sub-nucleonic fluctuations. While current models fit the number and size of these fluctuations to data, we use nuclear parton distribution functions to constrain sub-nucleonic structure and fluctuations in TRENTo-Sea, an extension of TRENTo. Sampling the momentum fraction distributions of quarks and gluons in heavy ions, we use perturbative quantum chromodynamics to collide nuclei at the parton level, and we introduce an infrared center-of-momentum damping scale which is tuned to proton-proton cross sections. We find that our model successfully reproduces the $\varepsilon_2\{2\}$ of data-calibrated models except in central collisions, and an analysis of the power spectrum and granularity of the pre-equilibrium fireball shows that the number of low-$x$ partons in a nucleon has a significant effect on the local transverse geometry. Our model is largely insensitive to variations of the infrared parameters, as such changes are absorbed into the calibration of the infrared regulator. We expect that this work will aid future research on soft-hard correlations and constraining different nPDF sets.

强磁场对具有反常磁矩的超子中子星物质的状态方程和质径结构的影响

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

我们在相对论平均场（RMF）方法中，研究强磁场对冷的、电荷中性的、处于$\beta$平衡的超子中子星物质的状态方程（EoS）和恒星结构的影响。我们纳入了轻子和完整的重子八重态。所有带电粒子均发生Landau量子化，所有重子（包括中性重子）均通过其反常磁矩（AMM）与磁场耦合。计算采用FSU2H参数化，并使用FSU2R检验参数依赖性，同时对零磁场、恒定磁场和密度依赖的磁场构型进行比较。我们发现，在固定重子密度下，强磁场通过Landau量子化与AMM诱导的自旋劈裂之间的竞争效应改变超子组分，使粒子份额产生微小且依赖于粒子种类的变化。在固定总能量密度下，纳入AMM会在能量密度范围的大部分区间使整体EoS变硬，通常约为$0.1\%$，但在狭窄区间内会出现局域软化。相应的中子星最大支撑质量增量取决于磁场构型，且仅约为$0.002\,M_\odot$。本工作为评估AMM对磁化超子中子星物质的组分、磁化强度、状态方程和质径结构的影响提供了基准。

查看原文标题与摘要

Effects of Strong Magnetic Fields on the Equation of State and Mass-Radius Structure of Hyperonic Neutron-Star Matter with Anomalous Magnetic Moments

We investigate the effects of strong magnetic fields on the equation of state (EoS) and stellar structure of cold, charge-neutral, $\beta$-equilibrated hyperonic neutron-star matter within a relativistic mean-field (RMF) approach. We include leptons and the full baryon octet. All charged particles are Landau quantized, and all baryons, including the neutral ones, couple to the magnetic field through their anomalous magnetic moments (AMM). The calculation is performed with the FSU2H parameterization, with FSU2R used to test the parameter dependence, and is compared for zero field, a constant magnetic field, and density-dependent magnetic-field configurations. We find that, at fixed baryon density, strong magnetic fields modify the hyperonic composition through the competing effects of Landau quantization and AMM-induced spin splitting, with small, species-dependent changes in the particle fractions. At fixed total energy density, the inclusion of AMM stiffens the global EoS over most of the energy-density range, typically by about $0.1\%$, albeit with narrow windows of local softening. The associated increase in the maximum supported neutron-star mass depends on the magnetic-field configuration and is only about $0.002\,M_\odot$. The present work provides a benchmark for assessing the influence of AMM on the composition, magnetization, equation of state, and mass--radius structure of magnetized hyperonic neutron-star matter.

Proceedings of "Machine learning for trigger, reconstruction and generation" LHCP26, 20/05/2026

Machine learning (ML) is increasingly embedded in every stage of the LHC data-processing chain, from event generation to final reconstruction. This contribution reviews the latest ML developments from the ALICE, ATLAS, CMS and LHCb collaborations that were made public in the year preceding LHCP2026, following the workflow of the experiments: event generation and detector simulation, real-time trigger selection, and online/offline reconstruction. “加速 ALICE 本底模拟：针对慢中子诱发的 TPC 回旋粒子的 WGAN 方法”会议论文集，CHEP 2026，26/05/2026

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

高能物理中本底过程的模拟可能需要高昂的计算成本并耗费大量时间。为了使用蒙特卡洛模拟为 ALICE 实验提供最真实的数据描述，我们研究了生成时间投影室 (TPC) 中由慢中子引发的电磁相互作用产物的替代方案。具体而言，来自这些过程的电子和正电子可在探测器磁场内反复螺旋运动（所谓的“回旋粒子”），而通过 GEANT4 等完整输运框架对其进行跟踪需要大量计算资源。开发了一个基于 Wasserstein 生成对抗网络 (WGAN) 的快速模拟框架，以加快模拟过程。我们的方案通过直接生成所得电子-正电子对（来自中子俘获）以及来自康普顿散射的电子的运动学性质（例如动量、产生顶点），旨在绕过将中子输运至低能量所涉及的巨大计算成本。该生成模型以高保真度成功再现了本底径迹的复杂多维分布，并与完整 GEANT4 模拟保持一致。通过以 WGAN 推理取代这些特定本底组分的完整输运，实现了十倍加速，使回旋粒子的模拟在总处理时间中成为可忽略的部分。此外，该快速模拟框架采用模块化设计，未来可纳入其他本底源，例如核散裂产物。

查看原文标题与摘要

Proceedings of "Accelerating ALICE background simulations: A WGAN Approach to slow-neutron induced TPC loopers" CHEP 2026, 26/05/2026

The simulation of background processes in high-energy physics can be computationally expensive and time-consuming. To provide the most realistic data description at the ALICE experiment using Monte Carlo simulations, we investigated alternative solutions to generate the products of electromagnetic interactions initiated by slow neutrons in the Time Projection Chamber (TPC). Specifically, electrons and positrons from these processes can spiral repeatedly within the magnetic field in the detector (so-called "loopers") and tracking them via full transport frameworks, such as GEANT4, requires extensive computing resources. A fast simulation framework based on a Wasserstein Generative Adversarial Network (WGAN) was developed to speed up the simulation process. Our solution is designed to bypass the significant computational cost associated with transporting neutrons to low energies by directly generating the kinematic properties (e.g., momentum, production vertex) of the resulting electron-positron pairs (from neutron capture) and electrons from Compton scattering. The generative model successfully reproduces the complex multidimensional distributions of the background tracks with high fidelity, maintaining agreement with the full GEANT4 simulation. By replacing the full transport of these specific background components with the WGAN inference, a tenfold speedup is achieved, making the simulation of loopers a negligible component of the total processing time. Moreover, the fast simulation framework is modular, allowing for the future inclusion of other background sources, such as nuclear spallation products.

Transport due to density gradient driven TEM & UM in advanced tokamak plasma configurations: role of negative triangularity and magnetic shear reversal

Abstract Advanced tokamak configurations can modify microturbulence through changes in magnetic equilibrium, trapped-particle dynamics, resonance conditions. In this work, global gyrokinetic simulations are used to study density-gradient-driven trapped-electron turbulence in tight-aspect-ratio shaped plasmas, focusing on the role of negative triangularity and reversed magnetic shear. In the positive versus negative triangularity comparison, negative triangularity is found to stabilize high- n modes and reduce saturated particle diffusivity by approximately 40 % . Energy-exchange diagnostics show that this reduction is associated with a weaker participation of weakly trapped electrons, while zonal-flow shearing remains comparable between the two configurations. In the reversed-shear case for positive triangularity, despite larger linear growth rates, the saturated electron diffusivity is reduced by approximately 36 % as compared to positive magnetic shear case. This reduction is attributed to the diminished radial support of the perturbation in the reversed magnetic shear region. The combined effect of reversed magnetic shear and negative triangularity is further investigated, revealing that although both independently reduce saturated transport levels, their combination leads to enhanced nonlinear transport.

从面向等离子体材料中去除滞留燃料：烘烤方法与氢同位素交换的影响

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

摘要 钨 (W) 是氘–氚 (D–T) 聚变反应堆中一种重要的面向等离子体材料，但 W 中的 T 燃料滞留对安全性和运行可行性构成严峻挑战。本研究系统比较了三种烘烤方法——真空烘烤、H 2 气体气氛烘烤和氢 (H) 等离子体烘烤——在 513 K 下去除 W 中滞留的 D，以模拟反应堆中去除 T 的实际条件。实验结果表明，真空烘烤 16 h 后的 D 去除效率达到 75%，H 2 气氛烘烤 16 h 后达到 83%，H 等离子体烘烤 1 h 后达到 99%。H 2 气氛烘烤和 H 等离子体烘烤的优异性能主要由氢同位素 (HI) 交换驱动，其效率强烈依赖于引入 W 中的 H 浓度。较高的 H 浓度会促进更完全的交换，从而使 D 滞留显著减少。开发了一个多同位素填充水平模型，用于模拟端到端的 H 等离子体交换过程。模拟再现了实验观测到的 D 去除动力学以及 HD 解吸的出现，所得 D 滞留与测量结果吻合良好。H 2 气氛烘烤与 H 等离子体烘烤之间的显著差异从根本上受进入 W 的 H 数量支配：H 等离子体显著促进更多 H 进入，从而增强 HI 交换，并使 D 滞留大幅减少。本工作为将 HI 交换作为一种高效 T 去除策略提供了实验验证和建模支持。

查看原文标题与摘要

Retained fuel removal from plasma-facing materials: effects of baking methods and hydrogen isotope exchange

Abstract Tungsten (W) is a prominent plasma-facing material for deuterium–tritium (D–T) fusion reactors, yet T fuel retention in W poses critical challenges to safety and operational viability. This study systematically compares three baking methods—vacuum baking, H 2 gas atmosphere baking, and hydrogen (H) plasma baking—for removing D retained in W at 513 K, simulating realistic conditions for T removal in reactors. Experimental results demonstrate that D removal efficiencies reach 75% for vacuum baking after 16 h, 83% for H 2 atmosphere baking after 16 h, and 99% for H plasma baking after 1 h. The superior performance of H 2 atmosphere and H plasma baking is primarily driven by hydrogen isotope (HI) exchange, with efficiency strongly dependent on the concentration of H introduced into W. Higher H concentrations promote more complete exchange, leading to significantly reduced D retention. A multi-isotope Fill Level model was developed to simulate the end-to-end H plasma exchange process. The simulation reproduces the experimentally observed D removal kinetics and the emergence of HD desorption, with the D retention in good agreement with measurements. The pronounced disparity between H 2 atmosphere baking and H plasma baking is fundamentally governed by the quantity of H entering W: H plasma promotes significantly greater H ingress, thereby enhancing HI exchange and resulting in a drastic reduction in D retention. This work provides both experimental validation and modeling support for HI exchange as an efficient T removal strategy.

高能离子对动理学气球模不稳定谱和带状流生成的影响

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

Impacts of energetic ion on the unstable spectra of kinetic ballooning modes and zonal flow generation

Abstract Global gyrokinetic particle simulations are employed to investigate the interaction between energetic ions (EIs) and kinetic ballooning modes (KBMs) across different toroidal mode number ( n ) regimes, encompassing linear dispersion relations, nonlinear wave-particle interactions, and wave–wave interactions. The results demonstrate that EIs exert non-perturbative destabilizing effects on low- n KBMs, while stabilizing high- n KBMs through thermal ion dilution. These opposing EI effects arise from the different strengths of finite Larmor radius (FLR) and finite orbit width (FOW) effects in low- and high- n regimes. Single- n and multi- n nonlinear simulations reveal strong wave-particle interactions between EIs and low- n KBMs, resulting in higher KBM saturation levels and enhanced EI transport. Additionally, although low-density EIs have little direct influence on zonal flow (ZF), they can indirectly increase the spatial scale of ZF in multi- n simulations. Since ZF is generated via the beat-driven process and its structure is determined by the pump wave, the EI effects lead to the dominance of low- n KBM components, thereby increasing the spatial scale of the ZF.

Impact of helium cooling channels and flow channel inserts on magnetohydrodynamic mixed convection in a liquid metal blanket

Abstract On the pathway toward full simulations for a liquid metal blanket, this study extends the work of Chen et al (2022 Nucl. Fusion 62 026042) on mixed convection in a full-scale dual coolant lead lithium blanket by considering a more realistic case. In this study, the model incorporates the complex geometry of characteristic sub-components, including helium cooling channels and flow channel inserts (FCIs), to investigate their impacts in several key metrics: (1) Magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) pressure drop, (2) electromagnetic coupling, (3) velocity distribution in both the bulk and gap regions, (4) flow behavior in blanket components, and (5) heat transfer. Based on the MHD-UCAS platform, these multi-region, multiphysics direct numerical simulations are performed on a highly refined mesh of 5.46 × 10 8 cells under conditions relevant to the fusion reactor: the applied magnetic field of 5 T ( H a ∼ 10 4 ), the PbLi velocity of 0.1 m s –1 in poloidal ducts ( R e ∼ 10 5 ), and maximum volumetric heating of 30 MW m –3 ( G r ∼ 10 12 ). The computational results incorporate the influence of the helium cooling channels and the FCIs on electromagnetic coupling, thereby altering the MHD pressure and flow distribution. Simultaneously, the FCIs divide the flow into bulk and gap regions, where localized reverse flow is observed mainly within the gap regions of the rear poloidal ducts. The anisotropic conductivity introduced by helium channels alters the velocity and temperature distributions, further complicating the MHD flow. In addition, localized hot spots exceeding the design temperature limit are found near the top of the U-turn and in the outlet reduced activation ferritic/martensitic steel walls, suggesting that enhanced cooling is required in these locations.

高能粒子驱动的多- n 环形阿尔芬本征模非线性生成带状流的混合模拟

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

在燃烧等离子体中，由环形阿尔芬本征模 (TAEs) 激发的带状流 (ZFs) 在改善能量约束方面发挥着关键作用。在本工作中，使用 CLT-K 代码进行非线性混合模拟，分析多- n TAE 系统中强烈的自生成 ZFs，其中 ZFs 由 TAEs 的自拍频相互作用驱动。结果表明，强 ZFs 主导非线性阶段，并显著抑制 TAE 活动。ZF 模结构由 m = 0 和 m = 1 谐波组成，并与 TAEs 的空间结构直接相关。对 ZF 动能演化的进一步分析表明，由环形几何结构引起的雷诺应力与麦克斯韦应力之间的有限残差，使能量能够从 TAEs 净传递至 ZFs，同时高能粒子 (EPs) 压力响应还提供了额外贡献。此外，线性洛伦兹力根据演化阶段的不同，对 ZF 泵浦或阻尼产生贡献。高 EP 压力和低黏度范围有利于生成强 ZF。研究识别出一种自调节过程：更强的 TAEs 驱动更强的 ZFs，而后者又调节 TAE 活动。这些发现从数值和物理两方面揭示了 ZFs 在调节托卡马克等离子体中 EP 驱动不稳定性方面的作用。

Hybrid simulations of nonlinear zonal flow generation by multi- n toroidal Alfvén eigenmodes driven by energetic particles

Abstract In burning plasmas, zonal flows (ZFs) excited by toroidal Alfvén eigenmodes (TAEs) play a crucial role in improving energy confinement. In this work, strong self-generated ZFs are analyzed in a multi- n TAE system through nonlinear hybrid simulations using the CLT-K code, where the ZFs are driven by self-beat interactions of TAEs. The results show that the strong ZFs dominate the nonlinear stage and significantly suppress the TAE activity. The ZF mode structures consist of the m = 0 and m = 1 harmonics, and are directly correlated with the spatial structures of TAEs. Further analysis of the ZF kinetic energy evolution demonstrates that a finite residual between the Reynolds and Maxwell stresses, arising from toroidal geometry, enables net energy transfer from TAEs to ZFs, with an additional contribution from the energetic particles (EPs) pressure response. In addition, the linear Lorentz force contributes either to ZF pumping or damping depending on the stage of the evolution. Strong ZF generation is favored in regimes with high EP pressure and low viscosity. A self-regulating process is identified: stronger TAEs drive stronger ZFs, which in turn regulates the TAE activity. These findings provide both numerical and physical insights into the role of ZFs in regulating EP-driven instabilities in tokamak plasmas.

色玻璃凝聚态中的重味横向能量-能量关联函数

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

我们在色玻璃凝聚态框架内，以领头阶计算了质子–原子核碰撞中前向重夸克对产生的横向能量--能量关联函数 (TEEC)。该计算保留重--夸克质量、精确的末态部分子运动学以及完整的相对方位角依赖关系，而不采用背对背近似。在所采用的共线碎裂方案中，对两个重夸克碎裂分支中的强子种类进行完整求和，通过动量求和规则消除了碎裂函数。我们给出了 RHIC 能区粲产生以及 LHC 能区粲和底产生的数值结果，使用受 HERA 约束的 McLerran--Venugopalan 初始条件，并通过跑动耦合 Balitsky--Kovchegov 方程进行演化。相对于质子–质子碰撞，原子核靶抑制了每核子的积分 TEEC，并使相对角权重向更加偏离背对背的构型移动。这些修正的大小和角度依赖关系随夸克质量、碰撞运动学和原子核几何结构而变化。这些结果说明了重味能量关联对原子核小-$x$ 动力学的敏感性，同时降低了其领头共线碎裂依赖性。

Heavy-flavor transverse energy-energy correlators in the color-glass condensate

We calculate the transverse energy--energy correlator (TEEC) for forward heavy-quark pair production in proton--nucleus collisions at leading order within the Color Glass Condensate framework. The calculation retains the heavy--quark mass, exact final-state parton kinematics and full relative azimuthal-angle dependence, without imposing a back-to-back approximation. Within the adopted collinear fragmentation prescription, a complete sum over hadron species in the two heavy-quark fragmentation branches eliminates the fragmentation functions through the momentum sum rule. We present numerical results for charm production at RHIC energies and charm and bottom production at LHC energies, using HERA-constrained McLerran--Venugopalan initial conditions evolved with the running-coupling Balitsky--Kovchegov equation. Relative to proton--proton collisions, the nuclear target suppresses the integrated TEEC per nucleon and shifts the relative angular weight towards configurations farther from back-to-back. The magnitude and angular dependence of these modifications vary with the quark mass, collision kinematics and nuclear geometry. These results illustrate the sensitivity of heavy-flavor energy correlations to nuclear small-$x$ dynamics while reducing their leading collinear fragmentation dependence.

聚变相关氦等离子体辐照下晶界对钨表面改性的影响：粗晶与纳米晶微观结构的对比

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

摘要 晶粒细化被广泛认为是提高核材料抗辐照性能的有效策略，但晶界（GBs）对氦（He）等离子体辐照诱导的钨（W）表面改性的影响仍不清楚。本文比较了粗晶和纳米晶W在聚变相关He等离子体辐照下的表面形貌演化和亚表面气泡分布。样品在1173 K下同时暴露于90 eV He离子。尽管两种微观结构最终都形成了由纳米尺度卷须组成的相似绒毛，但其表面粗化的早期演化表现出明显差异。在厚度约为∼600 nm的纳米晶W薄膜中，表面粗化由晶粒交汇处的异常气泡生长引发。相比之下，粗晶W表现出显著依赖晶体学取向的粗化。随后，两种微观结构中均发生卷须形核与生长，并优先出现在高度凸起的表面区域。此外，亚表面气泡主要局限于粗晶W表面以下约100–200 nm的浅层区域，而它们则渗透了整个纳米晶薄膜。这些结果表明，在绒毛孕育阶段，由GB介导的气泡动力学主要支配表面演化，而界面曲率和毛细作用驱动的扩散主导卷须形核及随后的生长。因此，GBs对早期表面粗化具有决定性影响，但对绒毛后期发展的影响有限。这些发现有助于理解不同晶粒尺寸W中He诱导表面改性的不同机制和共同机制，并支持绒毛形成的毛细生长框架。

Grain boundary effects on surface modifications of tungsten under fusion-relevant helium plasma irradiation: coarse-grained vs nano-grained microstructures

Abstract Grain refinement is widely regarded as an effective strategy for improving radiation resistance in nuclear materials, but the influence of grain boundaries (GBs) on helium (He) plasma irradiation-induced surface modifications in tungsten (W) remains unclear. Here, we compare the surface morphology evolution and subsurface bubble distributions of coarse-grained and nano-grained W under fusion-relevant He plasma irradiation. The samples were exposed simultaneously to 90 eV He ions at 1173 K. Although both microstructures ultimately developed similar fuzz composed of nanoscale tendrils, their early-stage evolution of surface roughening exhibited distinct differences. In the nano-grained W film, ∼600 nm thick, surface roughening was initiated by anomalous bubble growth at grain junctions. In contrast, coarse-grained W exhibited pronounced crystallographic-orientation-dependent roughening. Subsequent tendril nucleation and growth occurred in both microstructures, preferentially at highly convex surface regions. Moreover, subsurface bubbles were confined mainly to a shallow region approximately 100–200 nm beneath coarse-grained W, whereas they permeated the entire nano-grained film. These results indicate that GB-mediated bubble dynamics primarily govern surface evolution during the fuzz-incubation stage, while interface curvature and capillary-driven diffusion dominate tendril nucleation and subsequent growth. Thus, GBs exert a decisive influence during early-stage surface roughening but have limited effect on the later development of fuzz. These findings provide insight into the distinct and common mechanisms governing He-induced surface modification in W with different grain sizes and support a capillary-growth framework for fuzz formation.

Effects of negative bias on turbulence behaviors and scrape-off-layer width in J-TEXT tokamak

Abstract The effects of negative bias on turbulence behaviors and scrape-off-layer (SOL) width are investigated on the J-TEXT tokamak. As the bias voltage ( U EB ) increases, edge turbulence intensity, transport and spreading are progressively suppressed, accompanied by a strengthening of the E × B shear near the last closed flux surface. Consequently, both the heat flux width (λ q ) and density decay length (λ ne ) contract, with a maximum reduction of 30%. Notably, at U EB = 0 V, λq is comparable to radial turbulence correlation length, implying that the SOL width is turbulence-dominated without bias. In contrast, for U EB = -400 V, where E × B shear is strong, λ q approaches the neoclassical heuristic drift model prediction. Correlation analysis further indicates that the SOL width is strongly tied to edge turbulence, while shows no clear dependence on SOL-localized turbulent transport, possibly due to negative-bias-induced shear that weakens cross-field coupling and may lead to partial edge-SOL transport decoupling. Over all, these findings demonstrate that bias voltage can be applied to regulate the SOL width by driving E × B shear to modulate edge turbulence behaviors.

等离子体加料和加热模式对LHD中芯部密度崩塌稳定性的影响

摘要 未来仿星器反应堆的良好性能将要求采用能够实现良好等离子体约束、避免或尽量减少不期望不稳定性的运行方案。在大型螺旋装置（LHD）上开展的实验显示，芯部密度崩塌（CDC）会发生失稳，导致等离子体约束突然丧失，这与未来仿星器反应堆的经济可行性不相容。本分析的目标是通过提高等离子体边缘气球模的稳定性，确定避免CDC失稳的技术。为此，在一组专门设计的实验中测试了不同的等离子体加热和加料模式，以寻找有效调控等离子体边缘压力剖面的方法。改变弹丸注入的实验表明，如果等离子体边缘的压力梯度增强，并与强烈尖峰化的压力剖面相关联，CDC造成的能量约束退化会加剧。增加充气也会导致显著的能量约束退化；虽然压力梯度变化很小，但等离子体边缘的等离子体电阻率会增大。采用增强垂直NBI功率注入并增加第二条NBI束线的加热模式，对等离子体剖面的影响相当小，这是由于束流加热等离子体或为其加料的效率较低；其原因可由LHD外移位形中被俘获EP粒子的不利约束来解释。离轴ECH提高了气球模稳定性，并减少了超过失稳阈值时CDC弛豫期间的等离子体能量损失。离轴ECH注入改变了等离子体温度剖面，尤其是等离子体边缘的温度剖面，降低了压力梯度和等离子体电阻率，从而使气球模增长率降低。使用代码FAR3d开展的线性模拟专门用于计算一部分位形的气球模稳定性，复现了实验中观察到的相同趋势，支持通过调控压力剖面和降低边缘区域等离子体电阻率来进行优化的技术。因此，离轴ECH可能是提高未来仿星器反应堆等离子体边缘气球模稳定性、避免CDC事件发生的一项有用技术。

Effect of the plasma fueling and heating pattern on the stability of core density collapses in LHD

Abstract The good performance of future Stellarator reactors will require operation scenarios leading to a well confined plasma, avoiding or minimizing undesired instabilities. Experiments performed in the Large Helical Device (LHD) show the destabilization of core density collapse (CDC), leading to an abrupt loss of the plasma confinement incompatible with the economical viability of future Stellarator reactors. The target of the present analysis is identifying techniques to avoid the destabilization of CDC by improving the stability of ballooning modes at the plasma edge. On that aim, different plasma heating and fueling patterns are tested in a set of dedicated experiments, searching for efficient methods to tailor the pressure profile at the plasma edge. The experiments modifying the pellet injection indicate the degradation of the energy confinement by the CDC is reinforced if the pressure gradient at the plasma edge is enhanced, connected to strongly peaked pressure profiles. Adding gas puffing also leads to a significant energy confinement degradation, weakly modifying the pressure gradient although increasing the plasma resistivity at the plasma edge. Heating patterns with enhanced perpendicular NBI power injection, adding a second NBI line, show a rather small effect on the plasma profiles due to the poor beam efficiency to heat or fuel the plasma, explained by the unfavorable confinement of the trapped EP particles in LHD outwards shifted configurations. Off-axis ECH improves the ballooning mode stability and reduces the plasma energy loss during CDC relaxations above the destabilization threshold. Off-axis ECH injection modifies the plasma temperature profile, particularly at the plasma edge, reducing the pressure gradient and plasma resistivity leading to a lower ballooning mode growth rate. Linear simulations performed with the code FAR3d, dedicated to calculate the ballooning modes stability for a sub-set of configurations, reproduce the same trends observed in the experiments supporting the optimization techniques based on tailoring the pressure profile and reducing the plasma resistivity at the edge region. Consequently, off-axis ECH could be a useful technique to improve the ballooning mode stability at the plasma edge of future Stellarator reactors, avoiding the onset of CDC events.

Shower Shapes and Data-Driven Simulation Studies using a Highly Granular Crystal ECAL

Electron shower shapes were measured using standard shower profile models for events observed with a highly granular crystal electromagnetic calorimeter prototype, with data taken from a 2025 test beam at the CERN Proton Synchrotron T9 beamline. The shower maximum, longitudinal shape parameter, and lateral variance were found to be well approximated by log-normal distributions. From these distributions, properties of the absorber were measured to be in agreement with existing literature. Energy-dependent correlations were observed between all parameters and most strongly between the shower maximum and the shower shape parameter. The distributions of energy and event-by-event pairwise log ratios of energy fractions in individual active volumes of the calorimeter conditional on the shower shape were found to be consistent with shifted and scaled Gamma and Fisher's-z distributions, respectively. Generative methods were developed to simulate the logarithm of shower shape variables using Gaussian copulas. Copula sampling for shower shape parameters was found to produce superior modeling of heavy tails observed for the shower maximum and longitudinal shape parameter distributions when compared to direct sampling from a correlated multivariate normal distribution. A copula method was also devised to sample full shower events conditional on the shower shape parameters by exploiting the observation that the vector of energy fractions follows a logistic Log-Gamma distribution, the joint relation implied by the observed Fisher's-z form of the pairwise log-ratios. The generative model was evaluated using standard manifold and classifier metrics and was found to approach the data-versus-data ceiling. The fastest generation time was found to be around 30 {\mu}s per particle shower.

Quantum Noise-Limited Terahertz Interferometry for Distinguishing Ultra-Compact Supermassive Galactic Objects

The images of the galactic centers, M87$^*$ and Sgr~A$^*$, obtained by the Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) provide hitherto unknown revelations on these supermassive objects. The key issue here is the presence or otherwise of an event horizon at these ultra-compact entities. We show here that distinguishing the central images generated by gravitational lensing of a Schwarzschild black hole and a Joshi--Malafarina--Narayan (JMN-1) central naked singularity provides important insights into the nature of these supermassive objects and would help test the cosmic censorship conjecture, central to black hole physics. For optically thin spherical accretion onto Sgr~A$^*$, their interferometric visibilities become separable, independently of the emission model, on three baseline ranges within 1.4--6.2 G$\lambda$ at errors of $\simeq36\,\mu$Jy, and at $L_\star=2.64\,$G$\lambda$ at $\simeq71\,\mu$Jy. This is about 28 times below the 2017 Event Horizon Telescope statistical uncertainty. We suggest that the widest detection window demands minutes-long coherent integrations, favoring a near-quantum-limited space interferometer.

无相互作用偏振测量

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

已有研究表明，将无相互作用测量与量子 Zeno 效应相结合，既能提高成像的信噪比，又能减少被成像物体吸收的能量。然而，迄今为止，它尚未用于定量偏振测量，即重建样品的 Mueller 矩阵。在本文中，我们将其扩展到对具有二向衰减性的样品进行偏振测量。对于被样品强烈吸收的偏振分量，该方案识别样品所需的吸收能量远少于单程测量，这对使用高频辐射探测的脆弱样品有益。对于弱吸收分量，反复穿过样品会导致更多吸收，因此这种优势仅限于强二向衰减特征。Fisher 信息分析表明，对于具有无损透射轴的样品，该方案在确定吸收轴的位置时，所需的吸收光子数远少于单程偏振测量；而在测量部分透射率时，按每个吸收光子计的效率并不更高，并且，将有损分量以相同方式分选至一个输出端口也会对相位延迟和退偏振产生响应。

Interaction-Free Polarimetry

The combination of interaction-free measurement and the quantum Zeno effect has been shown to both increase the signal-to-noise ratio of imaging, and reduce the energy absorbed by the imaged object. So far, however, it has not been used for quantitative polarimetry, the reconstruction of a sample's Mueller matrix. In this paper, we extend this to the polarimetry of diattenuating samples. For polarisation components that the sample strongly absorbs, the protocol identifies the sample with far less absorbed energy than a single-pass measurement, a benefit for delicate samples probed with high-frequency radiation. For weakly absorbing components the repeated passes through the sample absorb more, so the benefit is confined to strongly diattenuating features. A Fisher-information analysis shows that, for a sample with a lossless transmitting axis, the protocol locates the absorbing axis with far fewer absorbed photons than single-pass polarimetry, while it measures partial transmittances no more efficiently per absorbed photon, and that the same sorting of lossy components to one output port responds to retardance and depolarisation.

液体闪烁体探测器中 $\alpha$、质子和电子粒子的 Geant 4 模拟统一淬灭模型

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

液体闪烁体（LS）探测器广泛用于反应堆中微子实验。为了精确测量振荡后的中微子能谱，理解 LS 的能量响应，尤其是淬灭效应，至关重要。针对 $\alpha$ 粒子、质子和电子的淬灭效应，已经开展了大量台式测量。这些结果通常用 Birks 定律描述，但不同粒子种类需要不同的 Birks 系数。在本研究中，我们发现，如果允许在 Geant4 模拟中产生更多次级电子，则可以使用约为 $0.013~{\rm g\,cm^{-2}\,MeV^{-1}}$ 的单一 Birks 系数很好地拟合所有粒子的台式测量结果。其根本原因是，很大一部分初级能量通过产生能量低于 4~keV 的 $\delta$-电子而沉积；由于它们与初级粒子相比具有不同的淬灭行为，因此应当对其进行单独追踪。本研究为比较不同台式测量得到的 Birks 系数提供了统一框架，并帮助 JUNO 和 SNO+ 等实验更好地调校其模拟，以进行精密能量测量。

查看原文标题与摘要

A unified quenching model in Geant 4 simulation for $\alpha$, proton and electron particles in liquid scintillator detectors

The liquid scintillator (LS) detectors are widely used in reactor neutrino experiments. To precisely measure the oscillated neutrino spectrum, it is crucial to understand the LS energy response, especially the quenching effect. Numerous bench-top measurements have been conducted on quenching effects for $\alpha$ particles, protons, and electrons. These results have typically been described by Birks' law, but with different Birks' coefficients required for different particle species. In this study, we find that if more secondary electrons are allowed to be generated in Geant4 simulations, the bench-top results for all particles can be well fitted using a single Birks' coefficient of about $0.013~{\rm g\,cm^{-2}\,MeV^{-1}}$. The underlying reason is that a large fraction of the primary energy is deposited through the generation of $\delta$-electrons with energies below 4~keV, which should be tracked separately due to their different quenching behavior compared to primary particles. This study provides a unified framework for comparing Birks' coefficients from different bench-top measurements and helps experiments like JUNO and SNO+ better tune their simulations for precision energy measurements.

客观性的热力学收支

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

当一个事件存在足够多的独立记录，以致不可能再通过任何操作使所有观察者都对其一无所知时，该事件便成为事实。记录是物理的，创建、保存和销毁记录均具有热力学代价。我们在一个最小广播模型中精确计算了这些代价：一个受驱动的中心量子比特将记录写入 $N$ 个初始处于热态的寄存器，并配有功源和温度为 $T$ 的热浴。这里概述了这些操作的基本论证，完整论述见正文。以冗余度 $R$ 和信息亏缺 $\delta$ 创建一个事件记录，所需功为 $W \ge R\, k_\mathrm{B}T \ln 2\, (1-\delta) H(\varepsilon)$，其以 Landauer 系数随冗余度线性变化。当记录基与环境诱导选择相互作用存在 $\theta$ 的失准时，记录会在临界角 $\theta_c = 2\arcsin\sqrt{H_2^{-1}(\delta)}$ 处失效；超过该角度，无论做多少功都无法达到客观性，而在 $\theta_c$ 以下，最优功可在使客观性所需功最小的指针基中写成闭式形式。在只能访问少于 $R - R_{\min} + 1$ 条记录的情况下，无论做多少功都不可能降低客观性；而一旦获得访问权限，功就无法提供任何保护。记录间冗余是可提取的自由能，擦除所访问的记录会在第一份副本之后产生净功收益。热力学第二定律约束着关联的破坏：从对手控制的扇区中擦除一个事实，至少会耗散与被擦除互信息相等的量，但整合不会产生任何耗散。重新热化介质中的记录是非平衡稳态，将记录维持在 $(R,\delta)$ 所需的最小功率，是在记录质量边界处求值的已知二态驱动界。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

The thermodynamic budget of objectivity

An event becomes a fact when enough independent records of it exist such that no operation is possible that leaves all observers ignorant of it again. Records are physical, and creating, keeping, and destroying them have thermodynamic costs. We compute these costs exactly in a minimal broadcast model with a driven central qubit writing records into $N$ initially thermal registers, with work sources and baths at temperature $T$. The basic arguments for the operations are outlined here, with full treatment in the text. Creation of one event record at redundancy $R$ and information deficit $\delta$ requires work $W \ge R\, k_\mathrm{B}T \ln 2\, (1-\delta) H(\varepsilon)$, linear in redundancy at the Landauer coefficient. Recording in a basis misaligned by $\theta$ with the einselecting interaction fails at a critical angle, $\theta_c = 2\arcsin\sqrt{H_2^{-1}(\delta)}$, beyond which objectivity is unreachable at any work, and below $\theta_c$ the optimal work is a closed form in the pointer basis that minimizes the work of objectivity. Reducing objectivity is impossible at any work with access to fewer than $R - R_{\min} + 1$ records, while work protects nothing if given access. Inter-record redundancy is extractable free energy and erasing the accessed records yields a net gain of work beyond the first copy. The second law constrains the destruction of the correlation: erasing a fact from a sector an adversary controls dissipates at least the mutual information erased, but a consolidation dissipates nothing. Records in re-thermalizing media are nonequilibrium steady states, and the minimal power to maintain a record at $(R,\delta)$ is the known two-state driving bound evaluated at the record-quality boundary.

查看原文标题与摘要

Quartz Crystal Microbalance impedance of discrete adsorbates with lubricated contact

We present theoretical investigation of the shear force exerted on a horizontal plate undergoing fast (MHz range) longitudinal oscillations in presence of a spherical particle suspended in the liquid at close proximity to the plate. This hydrodynamic problem is relevant toward the analysis of frequency response (i.e., complex impedance) of Quartz Crystal Microbalance (QCM) to discrete (particulate) adsorbates. For low areal density of adsorbates, the contributions of individual particles remain additive, rendering the theory applicable towards quantitative interpretation of the experimental measurements. For adsorbates much smaller than the viscous penetration depth, the closed-form analytical expressions for the complex impedance were derived for particles of arbitrary density, assuming a lubricated contact (i.e., non-localized adsorption). The theoretical predictions were shown to be in an excellent agreement with accurate Finite Element numerical computations. The validity of the theoretical approach was confirmed by QCM measurements conducted for polystyrene nanoparticles in a broad range of sizes, from 32 nm to 510 nm, adsorbing onto gold/silica sensor upon varying the oscillation frequency.

多阈值采样：信号空间、采样算子与首次记录的越阈时间

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

多阈值采样通过信号在规定阈值处的越阈时间对信号进行编码，为均匀时域采样提供了一种替代方案。其降低硬件要求和功耗的潜力促进了它在高速脉冲采集中的应用，包括闪烁探测器读出。我们通过组合可测的随机越阈算子、定时算子和选择算子，对信号统计量与记录的越阈统计量之间的关系进行建模。各阈值的阈值噪声过程相互独立。采样器在量化之前，对每次模拟越阈施加独立的定时抖动，并在记录窗口内分别对每个阈值实施死时间。当每个阈值处的各次越阈共享相同的定时抖动时，我们使用路径下确界和上确界表示首次记录的越阈时间的联合累积分布函数。将该公式专用于单个阈值可得到边缘分布，并由此获得条件标准差。我们使用这些公式近似每次越阈具有独立定时抖动时相应的精确量，并对每个近似误差给出界限。在一个闪烁探测示例中，对于联合分布、边缘分布和条件标准差，共享抖动公式与独立抖动波形模拟的结果在蒙特卡洛采样不确定度范围内一致。工程师可以使用这些结果选择阈值，并评估采样器的变化如何影响定时精度。

Multi-Threshold Sampling: Signal Space, Sampling Operators, and First Recorded Crossing Time

Multi-threshold sampling encodes signals through crossing times at prescribed thresholds, offering an alternative to uniform time-domain sampling. Its potential to reduce hardware requirements and power consumption motivates its use in high-speed pulse acquisition, including scintillation detector readout. We model the relation between signal statistics and recorded-crossing statistics by composing measurable stochastic crossing, timing, and selection operators. The threshold noise processes are independent across thresholds. The sampler applies independent timing jitter at each analog crossing before quantization and enforces dead time separately at each threshold in the recording window. We express the joint cumulative distribution function of first recorded crossing times using path infima and suprema when crossings at each threshold share the same timing jitter. Specializing this formula to a single threshold gives the marginal distribution, from which we obtain the conditional standard deviation. We use these formulas to approximate the corresponding exact quantities under independent timing jitter at each crossing and bound each approximation error. In a scintillation example, the shared-jitter formulas and independent-jitter waveform simulations agreed within Monte Carlo sampling uncertainty for the joint and marginal distributions and for the conditional standard deviation. Engineers can use these results to choose thresholds and assess how sampler changes affect timing precision.

MuSTRNG：基于缪子的可同步真随机数生成系统

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

宇宙射线缪子提供了可由空间上分离的设备探测到的自然随机事件，为同步随机比特生成提供了一种潜在的物理源。这之所以可行，是因为同一物理缪子可由两个设备各自独立探测，并用于导出相应的随机比特段。因此，识别共同的缪子事件并正确对齐事件序列对于同步随机比特生成至关重要。本文介绍 MuSTRNG，这是一种基于缪子的可同步真随机数生成系统，具有两种同步方法：径迹追踪--径迹追踪和径迹追踪--非径迹追踪。对于径迹追踪--径迹追踪同步，基于径迹的筛选会移除重建径迹不与另一设备的探测器区域相交的事件；基于角度的筛选将重建方向划分到多个角度区间中，并对齐事件序列以移除不一致的事件对；基于时间戳的筛选比较时间戳的高位比特，以进一步抑制残余失配。径迹追踪--非径迹追踪同步仅在具备径迹追踪能力的设备上应用基于径迹的筛选，随后在两个设备之间进行基于时间戳的筛选。我们评估了一个由两个具备径迹追踪能力的设备组成的原型。对于径迹追踪--径迹追踪同步，使用 15-bit 随机段时，三阶段过程将无效事件比率降低至 0.28%，同时生成的比特序列在适用的 NIST SP~800-22 测试中总体表现良好。在不使用其中一个设备的径迹信息的情况下重新处理数据，为径迹追踪--非径迹追踪同步提供了初步支持；使用 5-bit 随机段时，无效事件比率为 0.30%。这些结果证明了在不同设备配置下基于缪子的同步随机比特生成的可行性，并具有应用于安全通信的潜力。

MuSTRNG: A Muon-Based Synchronizable True Random Number Generation System

Cosmic-ray muons provide naturally occurring stochastic events detectable by spatially separated devices, offering a potential physical source for synchronized random-bit generation. This is possible because the same physical muons can be independently detected by both devices and used to derive corresponding random-bit segments. Therefore, identifying common muon events and correctly aligning the event sequences are essential for synchronized random-bit generation. This article presents MuSTRNG, a muon-based synchronizable true random number generation system with two synchronization methods: Tracking--Tracking and Tracking--Nontracking. For Tracking--Tracking synchronization, track-based screening removes events whose reconstructed tracks do not intersect the detector area of the other device; angle-based screening partitions reconstructed directions into angular bins and aligns the event sequences to remove inconsistent pairs; and timestamp-based screening compares higher-order timestamp bits to further suppress residual mismatches. Tracking--Nontracking synchronization applies track-based screening only at the tracking-capable device, followed by timestamp-based screening between the two devices. A prototype with two tracking-capable devices was evaluated. For Tracking--Tracking synchronization, the three-stage process reduced the invalid-event ratio to 0.28% using 15-bit random segments, while the generated bit sequences showed generally favorable results in the applicable NIST SP~800-22 tests. Reprocessing the data without track information from one device provided preliminary support for Tracking--Nontracking synchronization, yielding an invalid-event ratio of 0.30% using 5-bit random segments. These results demonstrate the feasibility of muon-based synchronized random-bit generation under different device configurations, with potential application to secure communications.

First Order Real Resonant Digital Filters and A Novel Orthogonal State Generator

We describe FOREST, a real, first order digital resonant filter and orthogonal state generator. We show that in spite of the imaginary nature of the roots of the transfer function of a resonator, appropriate interpretation of this transfer function nonetheless leads to a practical first order filter with real coefficients. We use a discrete state space model of FOREST to predict the resonator properties, and implement the filter in digital signal processing slices on a digital signal processing platform. Applications are in real time digital signal processing, engineering fields such as mains electricity monitoring and motor control, any application requiring a phase locked loop, and physics applications utilising high-quality-factor resonators.

Diagnosing internal leakage currents through magnetometry of lithium-ion cells

Internal leakage currents are difficult to detect and to localize through measurements at the terminals of lithium-ion cells. We measured the magnetic fields of pouch cells non-invasively with a magnetometer while a battery cycler set the current and recorded the voltage. A three-axis magnetoresistive sensor remains fixed inside a three-layer magnetic shield, and two linear stages move the cell in two dimensions relative to the sensor to acquire a map of the magnetic field above the cell. The field of an intact cell was linear in current from 10 to 420 mA, and its maps were symmetric between charge and discharge. To create internal leakage currents, two 600 mAh cells were damaged by mechanical indentation. Both cells developed a magnetic field at zero applied current that decayed over hours, while the cell voltage showed a loss of charge by self-discharge. This field breaks the symmetry between the charge and discharge maps, which allows leakage to be detected while the cell is being cycled. At rest, the setup detects leakage currents down to about 1--3 mA. The results highlight opportunities for battery diagnostics and safety, and may help in designing techniques for characterizing soft shorts.

Precision Measurements of Kaonic Boron Isotopes withSIDDHARTA-2 at DAΦNE‡

Abstract A precision measurement of X-ray transitions in kaonic boron, performed by the SIDDHARTA-2 collaboration at the DAΦNE collider, is reported. The energies and yields of the 5g→4 f and 4 f →3d transitions were determined for both boron isotopes, kaonic 10B and kaonic 11B. For the 5g → 4 f transition, the measured energies are 7064.62 ± 16.93 (stat.) ± 2.00 (sys.) eV for kaonic 11B and 6920.96±58.23 (stat.)±2.00 (sys.) eV for kaonic 10B. For the 4 f →3d transition, the corresponding values are 15293.33±4.80 (stat.)±5.30 (sys.) eV and 15180.11±20.86 (stat.)±5.30 (sys.) eV, respectively. The yields for the 5g → 4 f transition are 0.076 ± 0.013 (stat.)+0.012 −0.011 (sys.) for kaonic 11B and 0.079 ± 0.014 (stat.) +0.013 −0.011 (sys.) for kaonic 10B. For the 4 f → 3d transition, the corresponding yields are 0.115 ± 0.006 (stat.) +0.002 −0.005 (sys.) and 0.107 ± 0.007 (stat.) +0.002 −0.005 (sys.), respectively. All measured transition energies are consistent with electromagnetic (QED) calculations within the experimental uncertainties. These results provide precise experimental data on the transition energies and absolute X-ray yields of kaonic boron, which may contribute to future theoretical investigations of kaon–nucleus optical potentials and, within specific theoretical models, the related scattering amplitudes. The measurements also demonstrate the feasibility of precision X-ray spectroscopy of light kaonic atoms using solid targets.

古老GD-1恒星流中的双星质量转移化石

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

我们报告了J0930+2902的发现，这是在GD-1恒星流中识别出的第一颗具有s过程增丰的碳增强贫金属星（CEMP-s）；该恒星流被广泛认为起源于一个古老贫金属球状星团的潮汐瓦解。利用高分辨率Subaru/HDS光谱，我们对这颗恒星进行了全面的化学丰度分析。结合Gaia测光和视差以及对Fe谱线的迭代非局部热力学平衡（NLTE）分析，重新推导了恒星大气参数。我们确定了22种元素的丰度，其中包括对C、Na、Mg、Ca、Ti、Cr、Mn、Co、Sr、Y、Zr、Ba和Eu的NLTE分析。J0930+2902表现出显著的C、N、Na和s过程元素增强，同时具有较低的$^{12}$C/$^{13}$C比值7.4，这与通过昔日渐近巨星分支（AGB）伴星的双星质量转移而增丰相符。较大的径向速度变化和显著的近紫外超量分别独立表明存在一颗白矮星伴星，为这一情景提供了有力支持。与FRUITY AGB核合成模型的比较表明，初始质量约为3.0 $M_{\odot}$的低质量AGB伴星能够最佳再现残余中子俘获丰度模式。其C、N、Na和s过程元素的增强使其在化学性质上有别于其他已确认的GD-1成员。这些结果将J0930+2902确认为保存在古老GD-1恒星流中的双星质量转移化石记录，表明双星相互作用能够在吸积恒星群体中留下长期存在的化学印记。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Binary mass transfer fossil in the ancient GD-1 stellar stream

We report the discovery of J0930+2902, the first carbon-enhanced metal-poor star with s-process enrichment (CEMP-s) identified in the GD-1 stellar stream, which is widely believed to originate from the tidal disruption of an old, metal-poor globular cluster. Using high-resolution Subaru/HDS spectroscopy, we present a comprehensive chemical abundance analysis of this star. Stellar atmospheric parameters were re-derived using Gaia photometry and parallaxes, combined with an iterative non-local thermodynamic equilibrium (NLTE) analysis of Fe lines. We determine abundances for 22 elements, including NLTE analyses of C, Na, Mg, Ca, Ti, Cr, Mn, Co, Sr, Y, Zr, Ba, and Eu. J0930+2902 exhibits pronounced enhancements of C, N, Na, and s-process elements, together with a low $^{12}$C/$^{13}$C ratio of 7.4, consistent with enrichment through binary mass transfer from a former asymptotic giant branch (AGB) companion. Large radial-velocity variations and a significant near-ultraviolet excess independently indicate the presence of a white dwarf companion, providing strong support for this scenario. Comparisons with FRUITY AGB nucleosynthesis models show that the residual neutron-capture abundance pattern is best reproduced by a low-mass AGB companion with an initial mass of approximately 3.0 $M_{\odot}$. Its enhancements of C, N, Na, and s-process elements make it chemically distinct from the other confirmed GD-1 members. These results identify J0930+2902 as a fossil record of binary mass transfer preserved in the ancient GD-1 stellar stream, demonstrating that binary interaction can leave long-lived chemical signatures in accreted stellar populations.
