A probable supernova remnant and a possible neutron star in the main body of 30 Doradus

We report a probable supernova remnant (SNR) within Shell 1 in the main body of the 30 Doradus giant region, using multiwavelength data from the Hubble Space Telescope (HST), Cerro Tololo Inter-American Observatory (CTIO), Spitzer Space Telescope, Chandra X-ray Observatory, South African MeerKAT Radio Telescope, and Australian Square Kilometre Array Pathfinder (ASKAP). While the optical shell of Shell 1 exhibits properties consistent with a photoionized superbubble in a dense medium, its interior shows compelling evidence of SNR signatures. These include diffuse X-ray emission, nonthermal radio emission with a spectral index of approximately -0.64, localized enhancements of the H ii S ii /Hα ratio, and high-velocity features in the Hα line. From the X-ray morphology and characteristics of high-velocity features, we find it plausible that more than one supernova explosion is responsible for the observed SNR signatures. We further use the initial masses and ages of the underlying massive star population to search for clues to the supernova progenitor's properties. Several X-ray point sources are detected within Shell 1, and their nature is discussed. The bright source CX15 may be a neutron star candidate.

数据反演：从问题表述到结果评估

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

粒子与核物理、粒子天体物理以及辐射防护剂量学中的实验数据，是使用由复杂的传感器、电子设备和软件系统组成的实验设施收集的。测得的能谱或截面被视为概率密度函数（PDFs），由于分辨率、偏差和效率效应，它们会偏离真实的概率密度函数。反演被视为一种估计未知真实概率密度函数的过程。对真实概率密度函数的可靠估计是检验理论模型、比较不同实验的结果以及合并各种研究工作的结果所必需的。为此，既可以采用外部质量评估方法，也可以采用内部质量评估方法。在某些情况下，存在用于评价反卷积质量的外部判据。一个典型示例是模糊图像的反卷积，其中复原图像的清晰度可作为质量指标。然而，定义此类外部判据可能颇具挑战性，尤其是在此前尚未进行过某项测量时。本文讨论了在不依赖外部信息的情况下评估结果质量的各种内部判据，以及影响反演分布质量的因素。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Data Unfolding: From Problem Formulation to Result Assessment

Experimental data in particle and nuclear physics, particle astrophysics, and radiation protection dosimetry are collected using experimental facilities that consist of a complex system of sensors, electronics, and software. Measured spectra or cross sections are considered as Probability Density Functions (PDFs) that deviate from true PDFs due to resolution, bias, and efficiency effects. Unfolding is viewed as a procedure for estimating an unknown true PDF. Reliable estimates of the true PDF are necessary for testing theoretical models, comparing results from different experiments, and combining results from various research endeavors. Both external and internal quality assessment methods can be applied for this purpose. In some cases, external criteria exist to evaluate deconvolution quality. A typical example is the deconvolution of a blurred image, where the sharpness of the restored image serves as an indicator of quality. However, defining such external criteria can be challenging, particularly when a measurement has not been performed previously. This paper discusses various internal criteria for assessing the quality of the results independently of external information, as well as factors that influence the quality of the unfolded distribution.

Yukawa 等离子体中的上坡扩散

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

在由多种组分组成的流体混合物中，由于某一组分的通量与其他组分的梯度相耦合，该组分可以在没有任何外部驱动力的情况下向更高浓度处扩散。这种“上坡扩散”在化学和冶金学文献中广为人知，但据我们所知，尚未针对中子星和白矮星内部的强耦合等离子体进行研究。我们此前开发了一种半解析扩散模型，可准确预测平衡混合物中各组分的扩散系数。上坡扩散使我们能够在非平衡条件下检验该模型，并检验用于预测扩散率的广义 Darken 关系。我们推导了解析理论，并将其与三组分分子动力学模拟进行比较，发现二者吻合得非常好。这为检验我们的扩散模型向任意多组分混合物的推广提供了一种新颖的非平衡检验方法。

查看原文标题与摘要

Uphill Diffusion in Yukawa Plasmas

In a fluid mixture of many species an individual species can diffuse toward higher concentration without any external driving force because its flux is coupled to the gradients of other components. Such `uphill diffusion' is well known in the chemical and metallurgy literature but to our knowledge has not been studied for strongly coupled plasmas in neutron star and white dwarf interiors. We have previously developed a semi-analytic diffusion model that accurately predicts diffusion coefficients of individual species in a mixture in equilibrium. Uphill diffusion allows us to test this model out of equilibrium, and also test a generalized Darken relation for predicting diffusivities. We derive an analytic theory and compare to three-component molecular dynamics simulations and find very good agreement. This provides a novel out-of-equilibrium test of the generalization of our diffusion model to arbitrary multi-component mixtures.

新 Abelian 长程力的屏蔽

查看原文标题与摘要

Screening of New Abelian Long-Range Forces

We study how bounds on new $U(1)$ long-range forces, resulting from gauging the Standard Model symmetries $B-L$ and $L_a-L_b$, $a,b=e,\mu,\tau$, are modified if the mediator mass is generated by the Higgs mechanism. Large baryonic and leptonic charges of astronomical bodies displace the Higgs field from its vacuum value, resulting in charge screening. Depending on the $U(1)$ Higgs mass, the screening relaxes the bounds on the new force strength and mediator mass from equivalence principle violation and neutrino oscillations experiments, and eliminates the bounds on the muonic $L_\mu-L_\tau$ force from neutron star binaries.

We report evidence for undulator radiation generated by ultrarelativistic positrons in a large-amplitude, long-period crystalline undulator. Development of a low-level radio frequency system for remote in situ superconducting radio frequency cavity measurements and calibrations in conduction-cooling systems

We developed a low-level radio frequency (LLRF) system for superconducting radio frequency (SRF) cavity measurements in a conduction-cooling system. A voltage-controlled oscillator (VCO) was used in a phase-locked loop (PLL) to track the frequencies of SRF cavity modes. {\tt{Python}}-based software developed in-house controlled and monitored the system. By introducing an additional circulator and associated components, we developed an LLRF system enabling fully remote in situ SRF cavity measurements for the first time. This design also enables remote in situ calibrations. Hence, our LLRF system improves safety through fully remote operation and enhances measurement precision through in situ calibrations, with safety always taking priority. These calibrations are important for measurements in conduction-cooling systems because temperature stability and uniformity in these systems are poorer than those in convection-cooling systems using costly liquid helium and can vary over time and with environmental conditions.

重新审视重夸克在各向异性夸克-胶子等离子体中的碰撞能量损失

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

我们重新审视了重夸克在具有小动量空间各向异性的夸克-胶子等离子体中的碰撞能量损失。在Braaten-Thoma框架内，我们得到了硬贡献的闭合形式，并将软贡献化简为一维积分。我们的结果从解析上证明，在各向异性的一阶近似下，两种贡献中对人为分离尺度的依赖相互抵消。通过同时考察未归一化的各向异性分布以及分别将部分子数密度或能量密度保持在其各向同性值的两种变体，我们发现，在所有三种情形中，各向异性修正的角度依赖部分完全相同，并且与四极项$P_2(\cos\alpha)$成正比，其中$\alpha$是重夸克速度与各向异性方向之间的夹角。此外，各向异性修正的符号决定了相对于各向同性结果，能量损失是增强还是受到抑制，而该符号共同取决于夸克速度、耦合和传播角。特别是，在能量密度固定时，仅四极项保留下来。当$P_2(\cos\alpha)$的系数为正时，修正在$\cos^2\alpha>1/3$时增强能量损失，在$\cos^2\alpha<1/3$时抑制能量损失；系数为负时，情况相反。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Revisiting the collisional energy loss of a heavy quark in an anisotropic quark-gluon plasma

We revisit the collisional energy loss of a heavy quark in a quark-gluon plasma with a small momentum-space anisotropy. Within the Braaten-Thoma framework, we obtain the hard contribution in closed form and reduce the soft contribution to one-dimensional integrals. Our result demonstrates analytically that, at linear order in the anisotropy, the dependence on the artificial separation scale cancels between the two contributions. By considering the unnormalized anisotropic distribution together with two variants that hold either the parton number density or the energy density at its isotropic value, we find that the angle-dependent part of the anisotropy correction is identical in all three cases and is proportional to the quadrupole $P_2(\cos\alpha)$, where $\alpha$ is the angle between the heavy-quark velocity and the anisotropy direction. In addition, the sign of the anisotropy correction, which determines whether the energy loss is enhanced or suppressed relative to the isotropic result, depends jointly on the quark speed, coupling, and propagation angle. In particular, at fixed energy density, only the quadrupole term survives. For a positive coefficient of $P_2(\cos\alpha)$, the correction enhances the energy loss for $\cos^2\alpha>1/3$ and suppresses it for $\cos^2\alpha<1/3$, with the opposite behavior for a negative coefficient.

Non-perturbative charm production from color ropes in relativistic heavy ion collisions

由温度梯度驱动的临界涨落模式和各向异性关联

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

QCD相变信号的研究通常在空间均匀的温度条件下进行。然而，空间温度梯度对重离子碰撞所产生火球中相界面处出现的信号的影响尚未得到充分探索。基于类Ising有效势，我们研究了具有温度梯度的局域平衡系统。在2D圆盘几何中，低能涨落谱被明确分解为径向模和角动量模。奇异本征模的非局域关联表现出很强的各向异性：沿等温线具有长程性，但由于系统的热几何结构，在径向上受到抑制。不同于零动量模占主导地位的均匀系统，这种非均匀系统中的关联源于一系列贡献相当的零角动量模和非零角动量模的叠加。我们提取了奇异角动量模，并建立了它们与实验可观测的各向异性流之间的联系。我们发现，具有方位角敏感性的可观测量可能为探测QCD相变提供一条此前未被探索的途径。

Critical fluctuation patterns and anisotropic correlations driven by temperature gradients

Studies of QCD phase transition signals are often conducted under spatially uniform temperature conditions. However, the influence of spatial temperature gradients on the signals emerging at the phase interface in the fireball generated by heavy-ion collisions has not yet been fully explored. Based on an Ising-like effective potential, we study the locally equilibrated systems with temperature gradients. In a 2D disk geometry, the low-energy fluctuation spectrum is explicitly resolved into radial and angular momentum modes. The nonlocal correaltions of singular eigen-mode exhibits strong anisotropy, which are long-ranged along isotherms but suppressed radially due to the thermal geometry of the system. Unlike homogeneous systems where the zero-momentum mode dominates, correlations in such inhomogeneous system result from the superposition of a series of zero and non-zero angular momentum modes with comparable contributions. We extract the singular angular momentum modes and establish their connection to experimentally observable anisotropic flow. We find azimuthally sensitive observables may offer a previously unexplored avenue for detecting the QCD phase transition.

通过振幅调制的周期和准周期脉冲序列产生电子-正电子对

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

摘要 我们采用量子动力学方法，研究由符号交替的电场脉冲列驱动的非微扰电子--正电子对产生。我们比较了严格周期序列与准周期（斐波那契排序）序列：在严格周期序列中，脉冲振幅以重复的 \((E_1,E_2)\) 模式交替；在准周期序列中，振幅遵循一种确定性但非周期的替换规则，具有长程有序性但不存在精确重复。对于 \(N=12\) 个脉冲，周期脉冲列产生具有多缝（Ramsey 型）干涉特征的规则调制谱，而斐波那契序列产生具有部分动量空间局域化的碎片化谱。将脉冲数增加到 \(N=20\) 会放大这些差异：周期驱动产生对比度更高的干涉条纹，而准周期排序则产生更强的局域化和更不规则的谱特征。积分产额强烈依赖于场强比 \(\xi_{12}=E_1/E_2\)，但对时间排序的依赖很弱。对于 \(\xi_{12}\lesssim 1\)，两种排序给出几乎相同的产额；而对于 \(\xi_{12}>1\)，则出现幅度不大的交叉，其中斐波那契序列产生的粒子对略多。总体而言，时间排序主要重新分配动量空间中的谱权重，而粒子产额主要由有效场强决定。这些结果确立了长程时间排序作为多脉冲 Schwinger 对产生中的一种有效控制参数，并为未来高强度激光实验中设计定制脉冲序列提供指导。

查看原文标题与摘要

Electron-positron pair creation via amplitude-modulated periodic and quasiperiodic pulse sequences

Abstract We study nonperturbative electron--positron pair production driven by alternating-sign electric field pulse trains, using a quantum kinetic approach. We compare strictly periodic sequences, in which the pulse amplitudes alternate in a repeating \((E_1,E_2)\) pattern, with quasiperiodic (Fibonacci-ordered) sequences, in which the amplitudes follow a deterministic but aperiodic substitution rule with long-range order but no exact repetition. For \(N=12\) pulses, periodic trains produce regularly modulated spectra characteristic of multi-slit (Ramsey-type) interference, whereas Fibonacci sequences yield fragmented spectra with partial momentum-space localization. Increasing the pulse number to \(N=20\) amplifies these differences: periodic driving gives higher-contrast interference fringes, while quasiperiodic ordering produces stronger localization and more irregular spectral features. The integrated yield depends strongly on the field-strength ratio \(\xi_{12}=E_1/E_2\), but only weakly on the temporal ordering. For \(\xi_{12}\lesssim 1\), the two orderings give nearly identical yields, while for \(\xi_{12}>1\) a modest crossover occurs, with the Fibonacci sequence yielding slightly more pairs. Overall, temporal ordering primarily redistributes spectral weight in momentum space, whereas the particle yield is governed predominantly by the effective field strength. These results establish long-range temporal ordering as an effective control parameter in multipulse Schwinger pair production and provide guidance for designing tailored pulse sequences in future high-intensity laser experiments.

查看原文标题与摘要

Divergence-type hydrodynamics of radial oscillations in viscous neutron stars

Based on a simplified model of a viscous neutron star in the Newtonian limit coupled to a quadratic dissipative fluid theory of divergence type with bulk viscosity only, we explore the impact of nonlinear effects on the evolution of radial perturbations, and compare it with the results of linear hydrodynamics. For high values of the bulk viscosity $\zeta_B \gtrsim 5\times 10^{31}$ g/cm/s, the linear theory develops growing oscillations, which do appear in the quadratic theory but at much higher values of $\zeta_B$. The quadratic theory shows stronger damping of oscillations, longer decay rates of non-hydrodynamic modes and also a ``stiffer'' evolution of radial perturbations that results in less deformation. These results suggest that nonlinear effects may be relevant to capture the dynamics of the long-lived non-hydrodynamic modes present at high $\zeta_B$.

查看原文标题与摘要

Constraining the Inner Accretion Geometry of GX 9+9 with Broadband Spectral and Timing Studies

We present a broadband spectral and timing study of the bright atoll neutron star low-mass X-ray binary GX~9+9, using simultaneous NICER, NuSTAR, and MAXI observations from 2019 and 2022. The broadband spectra are well described by a combination of multicolor disk emission, thermal Comptonization of blackbody seed photons from the neutron star surface or boundary layer, and associated disk reflection. We report a clear detection of reflection features in this source, including a moderately broad Fe K$\alpha$ complex and a reflection hump, confirming an earlier detection from a different observation. These features likely arise from the illumination of the moderately ionized disk by thermally Comptonized emission from the boundary layer or warm corona. While the source remained in the soft spectral state during both observations, the 2022 observation exhibits a relatively larger thermal contribution, together with a steeper Comptonized component and lower coronal optical depth. Both reflection and disk-continuum modeling favor a truncated disk, with the latter indicating a smaller inner disk radius in 2022. The inferred radial extent of the boundary layer of $\sim8~r_{\rm g}$ may play a role in the observed disk truncation. Assuming that the disk is truncated at the magnetospheric radius, we obtain an upper limit on the magnetic field strength of $\lesssim3\times10^{10}$ G. Timing analysis reveals a relatively stable disk component and a highly variable Comptonized component, consistent with the typical characteristics of the soft state.

在允许未知相关性和模型系统误差的情况下合并 PSR J0030+0451 的质量--半径后验分布

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

NASA 中子星内部组成探测器（\emph{NICER}）任务测量毫秒脉冲星的 X 射线脉冲轮廓，并采用脉冲轮廓建模（PPM）技术约束其质量（$M$）和半径（$R$），以探测其内部物质的状态。作为研究最为广泛的脉冲星之一，PSR J0030+0451 已由多个研究组采用不同的热点模型选择进行了分析。采用不同的热点设定拟合同一观测数据，产生了彼此并不完全一致的不同 $M$--$R$ 后验分布，从而为致密物质状态方程（EoS）推断造成了实际瓶颈。在本文中，我们将一种贝叶斯合并框架应用于已发表的 PSR J0030+0451 的 $M$--$R$ 后验分布，同时允许存在未知相关性和系统不确定性。利用该技术，我们将现有的八个 $M$--$R$ 后验分布合并为对源参数的单一估计。所得约束为 $M=1.53^{+0.31}_{-0.27}\,M_\odot$、$R=13.44^{+2.95}_{-2.38}\,\mathrm{km}$，紧致度为 $C=0.169^{+0.018}_{-0.021}$（68\% 可信区间）。合并后的分布保留了采用不同热点模型得到的较低质量解和较高质量解。结合 PSR~J0437--4715 进行条件插值得到 $R_{1.4}=11.84^{+0.88}_{-0.83}\,\mathrm{km}$。我们的结果为 J0030+0451 提供了一个综合的 $M$--$R$ 约束，其中考虑了共享观测和跨模型不确定性，并提供了一个用于合并同一源的相关测量结果的框架。

查看原文标题与摘要

Combining the Mass--Radius Posteriors of PSR J0030+0451 Allowing for Unknown Correlations and Model Systematics

The NASA Neutron star Interior Composition Explorer (\emph{NICER}) mission measures the X-ray pulse profiles of millisecond pulsars and uses pulse profile modeling (PPM) techniques to constrain their masses ($M$) and radii ($R$), in order to probe the state of matter in their interiors. One of the most studied pulsars, PSR J0030+0451, has been analyzed by multiple groups using different choices of hotspot models. The different choices of hotspot prescriptions to fit the same observational data led to different $M$--$R$ posteriors that do not completely agree with one another, resulting in a practical bottleneck for dense-matter equation-of-state (EoS) inference. In this paper, we adapt a Bayesian combination framework to the published $M$--$R$ posteriors of PSR J0030+0451 while allowing for unknown correlations and systematic uncertainties. Using this technique, we combine eight existing $M$--$R$ posteriors into a single estimate of the source parameters. The resulting constraint is $M=1.53^{+0.31}_{-0.27}\,M_\odot$, $R=13.44^{+2.95}_{-2.38}\,\mathrm{km}$, and compactness $C=0.169^{+0.018}_{-0.021}$ (68\% credible intervals). The combined distribution retains the lower- and higher-mass solutions obtained with different hotspot models. Conditional interpolation with PSR~J0437--4715 gives $R_{1.4}=11.84^{+0.88}_{-0.83}\,\mathrm{km}$. Our results provide a combined $M$--$R$ constraint for J0030+0451 that accounts for shared observations and cross-model uncertainty, and a framework for combining correlated measurements of the same source.

A Tale of Two Type IIb Supernovae: Radio-loud and Radio-silent Candidate Counterparts to Sub-solar Mass Gravitational-wave Triggers

On 2025 November 12, the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA collaboration reported S251112cm, a gravitational-wave (GW) candidate from a compact binary merger with at least one sub-solar-mass component. Optical follow-up identified SN 2025adtq, a Type IIb supernova (SN) within the localization volume that exploded a few days before the merger. SN 2025adtq is the second Type IIb SN found to be potentially coincident with a sub-solar-mass GW candidate, after SN 2025ulz (S250818k). A key question is whether such SNe harbor fast, possibly collimated ejecta from a central engine, as invoked in ``superkilonova'' scenarios for forming sub-solar-mass neutron stars. We present deep Karl G. Jansky Very Large Array observations of SN 2025adtq from the JAGWAR program, spanning 139-216 days after the GW trigger at 6-10 GHz. The radio properties of SN 2025adtq differ substantially from those of SN 2025ulz: whereas SN 2025ulz has a significant 6-10 GHz counterpart, SN 2025adtq remains undetected to 3sigma limits of ~7.8 uJy, implying a radio luminosity density one order of magnitude below that of SN 2025ulz at a comparable epoch, excluding jet models that remained viable for SN 2025ulz. This diversity in radio properties calls for systematic multi-wavelength follow up observations of candidate sub-solar events in future runs of the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA detectors.

红背双星PSR J2215+5135的光学光变曲线展宽

原始发布日期：2026-11-26

蜘蛛型毫秒脉冲星通过对受辐照伴星进行光学光变曲线建模，为约束中子星质量提供了一条直接途径，其中包括质量高于两倍太阳质量的候选天体。这一方法假定辐照稳定。我们报告了伊朗国家天文台于2024年获得的红背脉冲星PSR~J2215+5135的新$g'r'i'$测光数据，并将其与\citet{linares2018peering}的2014年数据进行了比较。峰值亮度和轨道周期在不确定度范围内未发生变化，但2024年的光变曲线翼部显著更宽，因为所有滤光片上的局部偏差在上升支均超过$3\sigma$，且下降支还存在额外的$i'$波段超出，得到的全局置换检验显著性为$4.26\sigma$。单热斑模型对2024年数据的拟合优于无斑模型，但仍存在系统性残差。只有通过热斑、倾角、填充因子和伴星温度的相关偏移才能再现更宽的翼部，我们认为这是模型补偿，而非物理效应。一个延展且可变的加热组分也可能实现拟合。最佳拟合热斑温度在两个历元之间相差约$1060$\,K，与其模型依赖性相当（对于2014年数据，两个代码之间相差约$900$\,K），因此我们不将其解释为物理效应。两个历元无法表明这种变化是渐进的、突然的还是短暂的。推断的中子星质量取决于热斑的处理方式：带热斑的2014年数据给出$2.13^{+0.16}_{-0.13}\,M_\odot$，2024年数据给出$M_\mathrm{NS}=1.95^{+0.11}_{-0.07}\,M_\odot$。二者在约$1\sigma$范围内一致，但来自形状不同的光变曲线，并且统计不确定度不包括加热方案的系统误差。

查看原文标题与摘要

Optical Light-Curve Broadening in the Redback Binary PSR J2215+5135

Spider millisecond pulsars offer a direct route to constrain neutron-star masses, including candidates above two solar masses, via optical light-curve modeling of the irradiated companion. This assumes stable irradiation. We report new $g'r'i'$ photometry of the redback PSR~J2215+5135 from the Iranian National Observatory in 2024 and compare it with the 2014 data of \citet{linares2018peering}. Peak brightness and orbital period are unchanged within uncertainties, but the light-curve wings are significantly broader in 2024, as the local deviations exceed $3\sigma$ on the rising branch in all filters, with an additional $i'$-band excess on the falling branch, giving a global permutation-test significance of $4.26\sigma$. A single-hot-spot model fits the 2024 data better than a spot-free one, but systematic residuals remain. The broader wings are reproduced only through correlated shifts in the spot, inclination, filling factor and companion temperature, which we regard as model compensation, not physics. An extended, variable heating component may also fit. The best-fit spot temperature differs between epochs by about $1060$\,K, comparable to its model dependence (about $900$\,K between two codes for 2014), so we do not interpret this as physical. Two epochs cannot show whether the change is gradual, sudden or short-lived. The inferred neutron-star mass depends on the spot treatment: $2.13^{+0.16}_{-0.13}\,M_\odot$ for 2014 with a hot spot, and $M_\mathrm{NS}=1.95^{+0.11}_{-0.07}\,M_\odot$ for 2024. These agree within about $1\sigma$, but come from differently shaped light curves, and the statistical uncertainties exclude heating-prescription systematics.

Probing long-lived particle production in muon decays at the SNS with a highly capable hydrocarbon detector

The spallation neutron source (SNS) at Oak Ridge National Laboratory (ORNL) is a prolific muon producer, making it an ideal location for studying dark sector particles produced in muon decays at rest. In this paper, we explore sub-GeV dark particle detection possibilities in a tons-scale, highly capable hydrocarbon scintillator ( HC 2 ) detector at the SNS. We consider a search for e + e − final states produced by decays of long-lived, O ( 10 – 100 ) MeV axionlike particles and heavy neutral leptons. The HC 2 technology space, exemplified by the PROSPECT and mobile antineutrino demonstrator detectors, offers strong rejection capabilities for the cosmic ray backgrounds that would normally dominate this search. By benchmarking on-surface cosmic ray signatures with data from PROSPECT at ORNL, we generate robust predictions for a multiyear SNS deployment of a range of HC 2 detector implementations. Results indicate the potential for order-of-magnitude improvements in sensitivity to axionlike particles and heavy neutral leptons in the 10–100 MeV mass regime compared to current global limits. We also comment on the neutrino detection possibilities of a HC 2 deployment at the SNS.

D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 μ + ν μ 衰变的首次测量、动力学研究以及利用 D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 ℓ + ν ℓ 衰变对轻子普适性的检验

我们报告了对半轻子衰变 D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 μ + ν μ 的首次测量，以及对衰变 D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 e + ν e 的改进测量，所用数据样本为北京正负电子对撞机 (BEPCII) 上的 BESIII 探测器在 4.128 至 4.226 GeV 之间的质心能量下收集的 7.33 fb − 1 e + e − 湮灭数据。我们测得分支分数为 B ( D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 μ + ν μ ) = ( 2.07 ± 0.2 2 stat ± 0.1 0 syst ) × 10 − 3 和 B ( D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 e + ν e ) = ( 2.14 ± 0.1 8 stat ± 0.1 0 syst ) × 10 − 3。基于对两个半轻子衰变动力学的同步研究，确定 D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 跃迁中的强子形状因子参数为 r V = V ( 0 ) / A 1 ( 0 ) = 1.63 ± 0.1 4 stat ± 0.0 8 syst、r 2 = A 2 ( 0 ) / A 1 ( 0 ) = 0.60 ± 0.1 3 stat ± 0.0 6 syst，以及 A 1 ( 0 ) = 0.56 ± 0.0 2 stat ± 0.0 1 syst，其中 V ( 0 ) 是矢量形状因子，A 1 , 2 ( 0 ) 是在 q 2 = 0 处计算的轴矢量形状因子。r V 和 r 2 的精度提高到原来的两倍，并首次测量了 A 1 ( 0 )。我们还报告了 D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 ℓ + ν ℓ 衰变的微分衰变率和轻子前后不对称性的首次模型无关测量。基于这些测量，我们利用 D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 ℓ + ν ℓ 衰变，在完整和分立的 q 2 区间内检验了轻子味普适性。在不确定度范围内未发现破坏。我们的结果首次给出了对 D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 跃迁动力学的完整研究，并对多种非微扰理论计算提供了严格检验。

First Measurement of the D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 μ + ν μ Decay, Study of Dynamics and Test of Lepton Universality with D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 ℓ + ν ℓ Decays

We report the first measurement of the semileptonic decay D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 μ + ν μ and an improved measurement of the decay D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 e + ν e using a sample of 7.33 fb − 1 of e + e − annihilation data collected at center-of-mass energies between 4.128 and 4.226 GeV with the BESIII detector at the Beijing Electron Positron Collider (BEPCII). We measure the branching fractions to be B ( D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 μ + ν μ ) = ( 2.07 ± 0.2 2 stat ± 0.1 0 syst ) × 10 − 3 and B ( D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 e + ν e ) = ( 2.14 ± 0.1 8 stat ± 0.1 0 syst ) × 10 − 3 . Based on a simultaneous study of the dynamics in two semileptonic decays, the hadronic form factor parameters in the D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 transition are determined to be r V = V ( 0 ) / A 1 ( 0 ) = 1.63 ± 0.1 4 stat ± 0.0 8 syst , r 2 = A 2 ( 0 ) / A 1 ( 0 ) = 0.60 ± 0.1 3 stat ± 0.0 6 syst , and A 1 ( 0 ) = 0.56 ± 0.0 2 stat ± 0.0 1 syst , where V ( 0 ) is the vector form factor and A 1 , 2 ( 0 ) are the axial-vector form factors evaluated at q 2 = 0 . The precision of r V and r 2 is improved by twofold and A 1 ( 0 ) is measured for the first time. We also report the first model-independent measurements of the differential decay rates and the lepton forward-backward asymmetries for D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 ℓ + ν ℓ decays. Based on these measurements, we perform a test of lepton flavor universality in full and separate q 2 intervals with D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 ℓ + ν ℓ decays. No violation is found within uncertainties. Our results present for the first time a complete study of the dynamics in the D s + → K * ( 892 ) 0 transition, and provide stringent tests of various nonperturbative theoretical calculations.

Charged-current neutrino-nucleus scattering off Xe 129 and Xe 131 including the meson-exchange contributions

The scattering cross sections of (anti)neutrinos off the detector material in liquid xenon detectors is of great interest in both dark matter searches and neutrino physics. Here it is necessary to use calculated cross sections because directly observed experimental ones do not exist. In this work we provide the scattering cross sections for charged-current (anti)neutrino scattering off the even–odd xenon nuclei 129 , 131 Xe. To do this we use G-matrix-based two-nucleon interactions in large single-particle valence spaces. We model the nuclei involved using the Microscopic Quasiparticle-Phonon Model, constructing the states of the even–odd and odd–even nuclei from BCS (Bardeen–Cooper–Schrieffer) quasiparticles and QRPA (Quasiparticle Random Phase Approximation) phonons of the neighbouring even–even nuclei. Such calculations have been done before for 129 , 131 Xe, but here we go beyond earlier works by including the effect of the meson-exchange two-body currents (2BC) that can naturally explain a part of the quenching of the weak axial-vector coupling g A . A comparison to a phenomenologically quenched one-body-currents-only (1BC-only) model shows that the inclusion of the 2BC can account for a majority of the quenching of g A .

J / ψ → K * ( 892 ) K ¯ 中出乎意料的巨大相对强相位及同位旋破缺与 C P 不对称性的搜寻

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

查看原文标题与摘要

Unexpected Large Relative Strong Phase and Search for Isospin Breaking and C P Asymmetries in J / ψ → K * ( 892 ) K ¯

Using a direct scan of 26 energy points near the J / ψ resonance, we perform the first measurement of the relative phase ϕ γ , 3 g between the strong and electromagnetic amplitudes in J / ψ → K ¯ 0 K * ( 892 ) 0 + c . c . and J / ψ → K + K * ( 892 ) − + c . c . Unexpectedly, the phase in the neutral channel is found to be ∼ 15 0 ° , deviating from orthogonality (90°) by 4.8 σ and from a relative real amplitude (0° or 180°) by 12.6 σ or 2.8 σ , respectively. By contrast, the charged channel phase is consistent with ∼ 180 ° within 1 σ , exhibiting channel-dependent behavior. The corresponding branching fractions are consistent with the world averages but achieve better than twofold improvement in precision. The ratios between the branching fractions of J / ψ → K ¯ 0 K * ( 892 ) 0 + c . c . and J / ψ → K + K * ( 892 ) − + c . c . are also measured. After subtracting the electromagnetic contribution, the corresponding strong contribution ratios obey isospin symmetry within 1.4 σ . A search for direct C P violation yields asymmetries consistent with zero.

SN 2020hvp：由 56Ni 衰变和磁星自旋减速驱动的正常光度 Ib 型超新星

摘要 本文给出了 Ib 型超新星 SN 2020hvp 的光学观测结果。其多波段光变曲线的下降甚至比一些缓慢下降的超新星还要慢，而早期光谱则表明其光谱演化更快。由 Fe ii 5169 Å 推断的峰值附近光球速度约为 8500 km s−1。总辐射光变曲线在 Lpeak = 1.93 × 1042 erg s−1 处达到峰值，与正常光度 Ib 型超新星一致。我们使用 56Ni 模型、磁星模型以及 56Ni 加磁星模型对光变曲线进行拟合。拟合结果和 Katz 模型表明，SN 2020hvp 主要由磁星驱动，这与剥离包层超新星主要由 56Ni 衰变驱动的传统观点相反。导出的磁星模型参数为 Mej = 2.89 M⊙、P = 43.16 ms、B = 1.96 × 1015 G 以及 v = 3.51 × 109 cm s−1。组合模型得到相似的参数，但给出 MNi = 0.05 M⊙。AIC/BIC 检验和 Katz 积分法表明，56Ni 加磁星模型给出了更好的解释。SN 2020hvp 的能源似乎是 56Ni 衰变与磁星自旋减速的结合：早期辐射主要由磁星驱动，而晚期光变曲线则由 56Co 衰变主导。这些结果支持了先前的研究，即除放射性衰变外，一些正常光度剥离包层超新星还可以至少部分地由磁星自旋减速驱动。

查看原文标题与摘要

SN 2020hvp: A Normal-luminosity Type Ib Supernova Powered by 56Ni Decay and Magnetar Spin-down

Abstract This paper presents optical observations of the Type Ib supernova SN 2020hvp. Its multiband light curves decline more slowly than even some slowly declining supernovae, while early-time spectroscopy indicates more rapid spectral evolution. The photospheric velocity near peak, inferred from Fe ii 5169 Å, is approximately 8500 km s−1. The bolometric light curve peaks at Lpeak = 1.93 × 1042 erg s−1, consistent with normal-luminosity Type Ib supernovae. We fit the light curve using the 56Ni model, the magnetar model, and the 56Ni plus magnetar model. Fitting results and the Katz model suggest that SN 2020hvp is mainly magnetar-powered, contrary to the traditional view that stripped-envelope supernovae are primarily powered by 56Ni decay. Derived magnetar model parameters are Mej = 2.89 M⊙, P = 43.16 ms, B = 1.96 × 1015 G, and v = 3.51 × 109 cm s−1. The combined model yields similar parameters but gives MNi = 0.05 M⊙. AIC/BIC tests and the Katz integral method indicate that the 56Ni plus magnetar model provides a better explanation. The energy source of SN 2020hvp appears to be a combination of 56Ni decay and magnetar spin-down: early-time radiation is primarily powered by the magnetar, while the late-time light curve is dominated by 56Co decay. These results support previous studies suggesting that some normal-luminosity stripped-envelope supernovae can be powered, at least in part, by magnetar spin-down in addition to radioactive decay.

Role of neutron skin thickness in α-decay dynamics and implications for multimessenger astrophysics

Abstract We establish neutron skin thickness as a central structural descriptor of $\alpha$-decay systematics in even even heavy nuclei ($Z=74$ to $94$), revealing a highly constrained structural trajectory in neutron skin space that connects nuclear surface structure to decay dynamics. The neutron skin $\Delta_n(A,Z)$ and its evolution during $\alpha$ decay, $\zeta_{\alpha n}(A,Z)=\Delta_n(A-4,Z-2)-\Delta_n(A,Z)$, exhibit systematic correlations with decay energies and half-lives, establishing a connection between surface isospin structure and decay systematics. Ground state properties from the self consistent Skyrme Hartree--Fock--Bogoliubov framework with UNEDF1, benchmarked against FRDM, DRHBc, and AME2020 data, capture the established shell effects at $N=82$ and $126$, together with the predicted closure at $N=184$. Three principal structural and phenomenological results emerge. First, a global parameterization in $\ln Q_\alpha/Z$ organizes the calculated isotopic chains into a common linear relation with Pearson correlation coefficient $r=-0.9932$, reducing the residual sum of squares by $45.8\%$ relative to the two-coefficient generalized Geiger--Nuttall form. Second, we show analytically that the parent and daughter skin correlation follows naturally from the approximately linear dependence on isospin asymmetry and its kinematic transformation under $\alpha$ emission. The resulting trajectory identifies the regular surface evolution and resolves its structural departures. Third, the skin dependence on isospin asymmetry supplies a finite nucleus diagnostic of the isovector sector. A calibration across relativistic mean field models connects the UNEDF1 $^{208}$Pb skin to $R_{1.4}=12.58\pm1.19$ km and $\Lambda_{1.4}=518^{+385}_{-221}$, with propagated descriptive 95\% prediction bounds, and to the gravitational wave tidal response of binary neutron stars. These results provide a unified structural framework connecting microscopic nuclear structure, $\alpha$-decay systematics, and phenomenologically inferred multimessenger astrophysical observables.

通过 SMEFT 将核深度非弹性散射用作 BSM 探针

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

能量前沿的新物理搜寻推动了模型无关方法的理论发展，以约束标准模型之外（BSM）的相互作用，例如标准模型有效场论（SMEFT），并推动了相关的唯象分析。尽管这些方法十分强大，但它们也受到高维算符空间庞大以及这些算符之间可能存在简并这一难题的困扰。为帮助缓解这些困难，强子对撞机过程之外的过程，包括深度非弹性散射（DIS），已因其能够提供额外约束能力而得到确认和研究。在本工作中，我们将此类研究扩展到涉及重原子核的带电轻子 DIS，并针对一系列代表性情形探究 SMEFT 效应的系统学。具体而言，我们指出，核效应可以重新排列支配进入经 SMEFT 修正的硬相互作用之部分子的亮度，使原子核能够作为一个扩展的实验室，用于探索与自由核子 DIS 互补的 SMEFT 效应。具体而言，我们发现核 DIS 对味道等矢量 SMEFT 算符组合具有显著的约束能力，且对 nPDF 的依赖减弱；类似地，改善对 nPDFs 的掌控将进一步拓展核 DIS 观测量对 SMEFT 的探测范围。我们探讨了 EIC、LHeC 和 FCC-eh 等未来（拟建）设施的预期结果，发现对核部分子分布函数（nPDFs）的改进能够进一步提高核 DIS 的探测能力。

Nuclear deep-inelastic scattering as a BSM probe via SMEFT

Searches for New Physics at the energy frontier have motivated theoretical development of model-independent methods to constrain interactions beyond the Standard Model (BSM) like the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT), as well as associated phenomenological analyses. While these approaches are powerful, they are also afflicted by the large space of higher-dimensional operators and the challenge of possible degeneracies among these operators. To help ameliorate these difficulties, processes beyond those at hadron colliders, including deep-inelastic scattering (DIS), have been identified and studied for their ability to contribute added constraining power. In this work, we extend such investigations to charged-lepton DIS involving heavy nuclei, for which we explore the systematics of SMEFT effects for a series of representative cases. In particular, we point out that nuclear effects can reshuffle the luminosities governing partons entering SMEFT-corrected hard interactions, such that the nucleus can serve as an expanded laboratory to explore SMEFT effects complementary to free-nucleon DIS. In particular, we find that nuclear DIS possesses significant constraining power over flavor-isovector SMEFT operator combinations, with reduced nPDF dependence; similarly, improved control over nPDFs would further enhance the SMEFT reach of nuclear DIS observables. We explore projections for future (proposed) facilities like the EIC, LHeC, and FCC-eh, finding that improvements to nuclear parton distribution functions (nPDFs) can further sharpen the reach of nuclear DIS.

A Multiwavelength View of SPLUS J102139.31-282247.5: An X-Ray Luminous and Eclipsing Dwarf Nova

Abstract We report the independent discovery of the cataclysmic variable (CV) SPLUS J102139.31-282247.5 using Southern Photometric Local Universe Survey 12 band photometry, following its initial identification via ASAS-SN variability. Here, we investigate the system using optical photometry alongside optical and X-ray spectroscopy. High-cadence optical light curves from publicly available Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS) data and a dedicated campaign at the Pico dos Dias Observatory in Brazil revealed a highly inclined system exhibiting pronounced eclipses with an orbital period of 1.81 hr. We found no evidence of a white dwarf (WD) spin period. Despite seasonal gaps, the long-term ASAS-SN light curve revealed several outbursts (15 with Δmag ∼ 1.5 brighter than the mean magnitude) and one superoutburst over the past decade. During Swift X-ray telescope observations, the system exhibited X-ray luminosity of 8.5 × 10 31 erg s −1 in the 0.3–10 keV band, originating from thermal plasma emission at a temperature consistent with accretion shocks. Optical spectroscopy from the Gemini Multi-Object Spectrographs on the Gemini South telescope confirms the CV nature of the system, showing strong, double-peaked H and He I emission lines alongside a weak He II 4686 line. Altogether, these features strongly support the classification of SPLUS J102139.31-282247.5 as a quiescent, eclipsing dwarf nova with an unusually high X-ray luminosity.

利用Fermi/GBM及后续Swift/BAT观测对新银河系磁星开展相干搜索：七个候选体和一个大型暴发星表

摘要 银河系磁星是一类具有极强磁场的罕见中子星，目前仅有约30个已知源。它们短暂的硬X射线和软伽马射线暴发为发现磁星活动提供了一条重要途径。Fermi伽马射线暴监视器（GBM）等宽视场仪器非常适合探测这些暴发，但其较大的定位不确定性使得难以将单次暴发与特定源关联起来。相比之下，编码孔径望远镜可提供角分尺度的定位，但视场更窄。我们将2013–2025年间由相干GBM探测流程获得的大量短时、软谱触发事件样本与对Swift/BAT历史图像的定向搜索相结合，搜索此前未识别的磁星并构建磁星暴发星表。我们建立了一个统计关联框架，利用BAT探测将GBM暴发定位至角分精度，同时还建立了一个时空聚类框架，将定位较差的暴发与共同源相关联。这些方法共识别出1720次磁星暴发，其中1170次被可靠地关联到11颗已知磁星。其余550次暴发无法归属于特定源。我们通过三种互补的发现途径识别出七个新磁星候选体。该星表由已关联的暴发组成，覆盖至GBM所观测到的最低流量。我们还提供了该星表的光谱和时间分析。这些新候选源为X射线后续观测提供了目标，此类观测可能揭示此前未识别的银河系磁星。更广泛地说，这项工作表明，将能力互补的仪器结合起来可以最大限度地提高高能暂现源观测的科学产出。

A Coherent Search for New Galactic Magnetars Using Fermi/GBM and Follow-up Swift/BAT: Seven Candidates and a Large Burst Catalog

Abstract Galactic magnetars are a rare class of neutron stars with extreme magnetic fields, with only about 30 known sources. Their short, hard X-ray and soft gamma-ray bursts provide an important channel for discovering magnetar activity. Wide-field instruments such as the Fermi Gamma-ray Burst Monitor (GBM) are well suited to detecting these bursts, but their broad localization uncertainties make it difficult to associate individual bursts with specific sources. Coded-aperture telescopes, in contrast, provide arcminute-scale localizations but have narrower fields of view. We search for previously unidentified magnetars and construct a magnetar burst catalog by combining a broad sample of short, soft triggers from our coherent GBM detection pipeline over 2013–2025 with targeted searches of archival Swift/BAT images. We develop a statistical association framework that uses BAT detections to localize GBM bursts to arcminute precision, together with a spatiotemporal clustering framework that associates poorly localized bursts with common sources. Together, these methods identify 1720 magnetar bursts, of which 1170 are confidently associated with 11 known magnetars. The remaining 550 bursts could not be assigned to specific sources. We identify seven candidate new magnetars through three complementary discovery channels. The catalog, composed of the associated bursts, extends to the lowest fluences observed by GBM. We also provide spectral and temporal analyses of the catalog. The new candidate sources provide targets for X-ray follow-up that could reveal previously unidentified Galactic magnetars. More broadly, this work demonstrates how combining instruments with complementary capabilities can maximize the scientific return of high-energy transient observations.

跨越交叉过渡区的QCD物质强子和部分子组成

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

我们构建了一个化学势为零的强相互作用物质唯象状态方程，在温度范围$T=100$-$510$ MeV内，以强子和部分子自由度统一描述格点QCD热力学。强子相由量子范德瓦耳斯强子共振气体描述，并扩展纳入介子之间的排除体积斥力；夸克-胶子等离子体则被建模为夸克和胶子的理想气体，并辅以一个与$T^3$成正比的唯象相互作用项。这两种区域通过一个平滑交叉的切换函数连接。我们通过拟合迹反常的格点QCD结果，确定了四个模型参数——介子硬核半径、部分子相互作用项的强度以及切换函数的两个参数。在该模型中，强子贡献在最高约$200$ MeV时仍然显著，远高于QCD手征交叉过渡温度。我们还量化了由切换函数的温度依赖性产生的交叉贡献。切换温度以及强子和部分子贡献取决于所选择的状态方程和切换函数。格点热力学数据不能唯一确定QCD物质的微观组成。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Hadronic and partonic composition of QCD matter across the crossover

We construct a phenomenological equation of state of strongly interacting matter at zero chemical potentials that provides a unified description of lattice QCD thermodynamics in terms of hadronic and partonic degrees of freedom in the temperature range $T=100$-$510$ MeV. The hadronic phase is described by the quantum van der Waals hadron resonance gas, extended by excluded-volume repulsion between mesons, while the quark-gluon plasma is modeled as an ideal gas of quarks and gluons supplemented with a phenomenological interaction term proportional to $T^3$. The two regimes are connected by a smooth crossover switching function. We determine the four model parameters - the meson hard-core radius, the strength of the partonic interaction term, and two parameters of the switching function - by fitting to lattice QCD results for the trace anomaly. In this model, hadronic contributions remain significant up to about $200$ MeV, well above the QCD chiral crossover. We also quantify the crossing contributions generated by the temperature dependence of the switching function. The switching temperature and the hadronic and partonic contributions depend on the chosen equations of state and switching function. The lattice thermodynamic data do not uniquely determine the microscopic composition of QCD matter.

含时密度泛函理论中的裂变碎片总动能：粒子数投影和集体形变的作用

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

对裂变碎片总动能（TKE）的含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）计算，在仅限于轴对称轨迹和单个破坏对称性的平均场态时，会在偏离最概然质量分裂处将TKE低估10-20 MeV。我们在相对论TDDFT+BCS框架内针对$^{240}$Pu和$^{252}$Cf重新审视这一问题，将粒子数投影（PNP）与包含十六极形变和非轴形变的扩展集体空间相结合。改变配对强度基本不改变TKE，但PNP将每条轨迹转换为随碎片质量分辨的TKE分布，而要达到正确的断裂构型，则需要扩大的集体空间。两者结合后，对经验TKE质量依赖关系的再现显著优于标准计算，这表明该偏差反映的是对称性恢复和构型空间的缺失，而非能量泛函存在缺陷。

查看原文标题与摘要

Total kinetic energy of fission fragments within time-dependent density functional theory: role of particle-number projection and collective deformations

Time-dependent density functional theory (TDDFT) calculations of fission fragment total kinetic energy (TKE), restricted to axially symmetric trajectories and a single symmetry-violating mean-field state, underestimate TKE by 10-20 MeV away from the most probable mass split. We reexamine this for $^{240}$Pu and $^{252}$Cf within relativistic TDDFT+BCS, combining particle-number projection (PNP) with an extended collective space including hexadecapole and non-axial deformations. Varying the pairing strength leaves the TKE essentially unchanged, but PNP converts each trajectory into a resolved TKE distribution over fragment mass, and the enlarged collective space is needed to reach the correct scission configurations. Together, these reproduce the empirical TKE mass dependence substantially better than standard calculations, indicating the discrepancy reflects missing symmetry restoration and configuration space rather than a deficient energy functional.

The impact of phase transitions on dilepton production

We calculate dielectron yields at beam energies relevant for the CBM experiment at FAIR and the STAR Beam Energy Scan. The production of intermediate mass dielectrons is expected to increase in presence of a phase transition due to the latent heat. In this work, we use the SMASH+vHLLE hybrid model to quantify this enhancement in two scenarios: with a toy equation of state consisting of the Bag Model and Hadron Resonance Gas, separated by a Maxwell-constructed transition; and with a more realistic equation of state containing a critical endpoint, based on the 3D Ising universality class. We confirm that the dilepton yield increases when the system passes through a first-order phase transition, by about 7% in the realistic scenario.

在相同多重数下，$\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}} = 5.36$ TeV 的 Ne+Ne 碰撞中带电强子谱比 O+O 碰撞中更软

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

将在 $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}} = 5.36$ TeV 下由 ATLAS 合作组测量的 O+O 和 Ne+Ne 碰撞带电强子谱按相同带电粒子多重数而非相同中心度进行比较，以检验给定多重数下横动量谱的形状是否依赖于碰撞系统的大小。在多重数相等时，Ne+Ne 碰撞的平均横动量 $\langle p_{\rm T}\rangle$ 低于 O+O 碰撞。在不进行任何谱拟合的情况下，已发表的 $\langle p_{\rm T}\rangle$ 值给出在 3-4% 中心度下 Ne+Ne/O+O 比值为 0.9822；即使将 O+O 的系统不确定度视为在各中心度区间之间不相关，该值仍比一低 6.6 个标准差。在 $0.4 < p_{\rm T} < 2$ GeV 范围内，通过用单一伸缩因子压缩横动量轴后的 O+O 谱，可以描述 Ne+Ne 谱，在 3-4% 区间内 $\lambda_{\rm Ne/O} = 0.969$。根据 O+O 系统不确定度的共同部分是否在各中心度区间之间抵消，该值比一低 9.5 或 3.3 个标准差。如果 Ne+Ne 碰撞在相同多重数下具有更大的横向面积，并且更大的面积会产生更软的谱，则这一排序符合预期。谱的软化朝外周碰撞方向减弱，并且在前向赝快度处相同。在中心碰撞中，谱形状与中心度区间的对应关系比与多重数的对应关系更密切。还在 Xe+Xe 和 Pb+Pb 碰撞中检验了相同的程序。

查看原文标题与摘要

Softer charged-hadron spectra in Ne+Ne than in O+O collisions at the same multiplicity at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}} = 5.36$ TeV

Charged-hadron spectra measured by the ATLAS Collaboration in O+O and Ne+Ne collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}} = 5.36$ TeV are compared at the same charged-particle multiplicity instead of at the same centrality, to test whether the shape of the transverse-momentum spectrum at a given multiplicity depends on the size of the colliding system. At equal multiplicity, Ne+Ne collisions have a lower mean transverse momentum $\langle p_{\rm T}\rangle$ than O+O collisions. Without any spectral fit, the published $\langle p_{\rm T}\rangle$ values give a Ne+Ne/O+O ratio of 0.9822 at 3-4% centrality, 6.6 standard deviations below unity even if the O+O systematic uncertainties are taken as uncorrelated between centrality classes. At $0.4 < p_{\rm T} < 2$ GeV the Ne+Ne spectrum is described by the O+O spectrum with the transverse-momentum axis compressed by a single stretch factor, $\lambda_{\rm Ne/O} = 0.969$ in the 3-4% class. It lies 9.5 or 3.3 standard deviations below unity, depending on whether the common part of the O+O systematic uncertainties cancels between centrality classes. This ordering is expected if Ne+Ne collisions have a larger transverse area at the same multiplicity and a larger area gives a softer spectrum. The softening decreases towards peripheral collisions and is the same at forward pseudorapidity. In central collisions the spectral shape follows the centrality class more closely than the multiplicity. The same procedure is tested in Xe+Xe and Pb+Pb collisions.

用于束缚态模拟的基于机器学习的量子误差缓解

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

量子设备中的噪声仍然是实现可靠量子模拟的一项重大挑战，因此量子误差缓解成为提高其准确性的重要工具。基于机器学习的量子误差缓解（ML-QEM）提供了一种有用的方法，但其性能取决于训练数据集，并且可能产生物理上无效的重构量子态。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种 ML-QEM 框架，该框架采用带凸投影的残差学习，以确保重构的密度矩阵满足物理有效性条件。我们使用 Haar 随机态和变分量子本征求解器（VQE）拟设态，研究不同训练数据集和回归模型的影响。我们将所提出的方法与传统 ML-QEM、读出误差缓解和零噪声外推进行比较。该方法在 IBM 量子硬件上进行了测试，分别用于束缚 $\Lambda$-氘核和 $\Lambda$-$\alpha$ 超核系统的三量子比特和四量子比特模拟。对于三量子比特系统，在 VQE 拟设态上训练的岭回归给出的基态能量为 -0.134 MeV，接近 -0.135 MeV 的理想值，态保真度为 0.999。对于四量子比特系统，核岭回归给出的基态能量为 -3.014 和 -3.045 MeV，对应的态保真度分别为 0.995 和 0.998。这些结果表明，物理约束重构与训练数据集和回归模型的选择相结合，可以提高 ML-QEM 在本工作所研究的量子模拟中的性能。

查看原文标题与摘要

Machine Learning-Based Quantum Error Mitigation for Bound-State Simulations

Noise in quantum devices remains a major challenge for reliable quantum simulations, making quantum error mitigation an important tool for improving their accuracy. Machine learning-based quantum error mitigation (ML-QEM) provides a useful approach, but its performance depends on the training datasets and may produce reconstructed quantum states that are not physically valid. In this work, we introduce an ML-QEM framework that uses residual learning with convex projection to ensure that reconstructed density matrices satisfy physically valid density matrices. We study the effect of different training datasets and regression models using Haar-random and variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) ansatz states. We compare the proposed approach with conventional ML-QEM, readout error mitigation, and zero-noise extrapolation. The method is tested on IBM quantum hardware for three- and four-qubit simulations of the bound $\Lambda$-deuteron and $\Lambda$-$\alpha$ hypernuclear systems, respectively. For the three-qubit system, ridge regression trained on VQE-ansatz states gives a ground-state energy of -0.134 MeV, close to the ideal value of -0.135 MeV, with a state fidelity of 0.999. For four-qubit system, kernel ridge regression gives ground-state energies of -3.014 and -3.045 MeV, with corresponding state fidelities of 0.995 and 0.998. These results show that physically constrained reconstruction, along with the choice of training datasets and regression models, can improve ML-QEM performance for the quantum simulations studied in this work.

Analyzing Fermionic Dark Matter Scenarios with Anomalous Compact Objects

In this paper, we consider three compact objects (HESS J1731-347, PSR J1231-1411, XTE J1814-338) with anomalous mass-radius relations to analyze the possibility of their being dark matter admixed neutron stars. We try to infer the properties of the dark matter particles, under the assumption of behaving as a free Fermi gas. The main novelty relies on the use of a baryonic equation of state obtained from first principles over the whole density range, which allows the model dependence of the baryonic part of the calculation to be eliminated. Once the possible dark matter admixed neutron star configurations are obtained, we check their stability and whether it is feasible for a neutron star to capture the necessary dark matter fraction. Our analysis shows that our dark matter model favours the dark matter admixed neutron star hypothesis over the pure baryonic neutron star one, with the most favoured dark matter mass of $m_{DM} = 1$~GeV, although the necessary dark matter content cannot be explained in a standard galactic environment.

Transverse Single-Spin Asymmetry Extraction, using both Binned and Unbinned Analysis, including Background Subtraction and Unfolding

The determination of transverse single-spin asymmetries in experiments involving polarized targets and/or beams may encounter challenges when (1) the magnitude of the polarization varies greatly with time, (2) the polarization magnitude is not the same for each spin state, (3) different integrated luminosities occur for different spin states or different target materials, and/or (4) some kinematic variables require unfolding; these are just a few examples. We present general methods of determining the asymmetry based on both binned analysis and unbinned maximum likelihood optimization, incorporating the unfolding of kinematic variables that are smeared by detector effects, and also including the possibility of background subtraction.

Z boson production in lead-lead collisions: An investigation Utilizing MRW nuclear TMDs

We investigate $Z$-boson production in Pb--Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=5.02~\mathrm{TeV}$ within the $k_t$-factorization framework, using four nuclear transverse-momentum-dependent parton distribution (TMD) prescriptions, namely LO-MRW, VO-MRW, MVO-MRW, and MKMRW. The aim of this study is to examine how different ordering conditions and normalization treatments influence the theoretical description of this process. We calculate the rapidity and transverse-momentum distributions using the parton-level KaTie event generator and compare the predictions with CMS measurements and collinear NLO calculations. This comparison allows us to assess the sensitivity of the predicted distributions to the choice of UPDF prescription.

78 · arXiv nucl-th · 源文更新（相对 11 月 25 日）

QDMin：参数化多体哈密顿量上的相干量子最小值查找

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

关于相互作用量子系统的许多问题所问的是，哈密顿量族 $H(\mathbf{q})$ 中哪个成员具有最低的基态能量，而不是求某一个哈密顿量的基态。逐点扫描通过对每个候选者进行一次多体求解来回答这一问题。相比之下，QDMin（量子形变最小值查找）将全部 $N_q$ 个候选者保存在一个相干寄存器中，并使用基态能量阈值预言机搜索它们，从而以 $O(\sqrt{N_q})$ 次相干预言机调用取代 $N_q$ 次求解。我们指出，静态场（Hubbard-Stratonovich）族是一个广泛的类别，在这类体系中，该最小值是母体系基态无法揭示的严格变分界；我们还通过定点振幅放大，使预言机对不完美的基态制备具有稳健性。测得的搜索常数表明，相对于逐点量子扫描的交叉点位于 $N_q \approx 220\xi^2$，其中 $\xi$ 是一次相干调用的开销。我们在具有多达 $10^6$ 个网格点的现实 sd 壳层（USDB）静态四极矩景观以及具有竞争序的 Hubbard 环上测试了该搜索。它在竞争极小值会困住局部下降法时具有优势：对于 $^{28}$Si 中的扁球形—长球形共存，其所需能量查询次数约为多起点下降法的四分之一；而在平滑的单势阱景观上则不具优势。电路级模拟在一个示意模型上验证了完整流程。增益涉及能量查询次数；量子求解器本身的任何优势则是另一回事。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

QDMin: Coherent Quantum Minimum Finding over Parametrized Many-Body Hamiltonians

Many questions about interacting quantum systems ask which member of a Hamiltonian family $H(\mathbf{q})$ has the lowest ground-state energy, rather than for the ground state of one Hamiltonian. A pointwise scan answers this with one many-body solve per candidate. QDMin (Quantum Deformation Minimum Finding) instead holds all $N_q$ candidates in a coherent register and searches them with a ground-energy threshold oracle, replacing $N_q$ solves by $O(\sqrt{N_q})$ coherent oracle calls. We identify static-field (Hubbard-Stratonovich) families as a broad class in which this minimum is a rigorous variational bound that the parent ground state does not reveal, and we make the oracle robust to imperfect ground-state preparation by fixed-point amplitude amplification. The measured search constant places the crossover against a pointwise quantum scan at $N_q \approx 220\xi^2$, where $\xi$ is the overhead of a coherent call. We test the search on realistic sd-shell (USDB) static quadrupole landscapes with up to $10^6$ grid points and on a Hubbard ring with competing orders. It pays where competing minima trap local descent, needing about four times fewer energy queries than multistart descent for oblate-prolate coexistence in $^{28}$Si, and not on smooth single-well landscapes. Circuit-level simulations validate the complete pipeline on a schematic model. The gain concerns the number of energy queries; any advantage of the quantum solver itself is separate.

Neural Network Potential Reconstruction from Phase Shifts in Resonant $n+{}^{16}\mathrm{O}$ Scattering

We reconstruct a local, energy-independent effective potential for the narrow $D_{3/2}$ resonance of $n+{}^{16}\mathrm{O}$ from elastic phase shifts, using a Gaussian-enveloped neural parametrization coupled to a differentiable variable-phase calculation and checked by independent radial Schr\"odinger solvers. For the first resonance (below $2.5$ MeV LAB), the reconstruction gives a $90^\circ$ crossing at $1.001$ MeV LAB and a derivative width of $85$ keV LAB. The published Dubovichenko potential gives similar values with its original kinetic coefficient. Four reconstructions with adjustable range place the crossing within $5$ keV of one another while their central potentials span $47$ MeV. This spread illustrates the radial ambiguity of the inversion for the measured $D_{3/2}$ phases. The broad $P_{3/2}$ resonance of $n+\alpha$ serves as a benchmark for the same method.

Studies of dual ions arriving time reconstruction in N$\nu$DEx time projection chamber

General Relativistic Simulations of the Nonlinear Evolution of r -modes in Rapidly Rotating Neutron Stars

Abstract The r -modes in rotating neutron stars are a promising source of continuous gravitational waves, and may grow or be excited to amplitudes α ≲ 1. We perform general relativistic simulations of nonlinear l = ∣ m ∣ = 2 r -modes in a rapidly rotating neutron star with spin frequency f spin ≈ 792 Hz, relaxing the Cowling and small- α approximations. The star is idealized as an unmagnetized, one-component, perfect fluid with a polytropic equation of state. The r -mode frequency is measured to be f r-mode = 1014 Hz for initial amplitude α 0 = 0.2. The dimensionless frequency κ ≈ 0.7197 agrees with an estimate from linear perturbation theory to within 1.7%. The f r-mode increases quadratically with amplitude, and κ decreases by 3.4% across simulations where α 0 is varied through the range 0.2 ≤ α 0 ≤ 1.2. The gravitational-wave strain calculated from the Newman–Penrose scalar implies h r-mode ≈ 9.7 × 10 −22 at 10 kpc for α 0 = 0.2, which agrees with Einstein’s quadrupole formula to within 35%. We also report on nonlinear phenomena observed in our simulations, e.g., differential rotation, amplitude decay, and signatures of nonlinear mode coupling in the spacetime oscillations.

Background characterization in the PIKACHU Phase 1 search for two-neutrino double-beta decay of $^{160}$Gd

We report a background characterization for the search for two-neutrino double-beta ($2\nu2\beta$) decay of $^{160}$Gd using Ce:Gd$_3$Al$_2$Ga$_3$O$_{12}$ scintillators in the PIKACHU experiment Phase 1 at the Kamioka underground laboratory. 闭合形式的相对论性 BCS-BEC 渡越：作为有效力程的截断

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

在零温下，具有配对能隙的 Dirac 费米子的平均场势包含一个动量积分，即其两个准粒子能级之和的积分。我们通过一种几何构造以闭合形式对其求值：这两个能级是平面中的距离，而动量线是一个固定椭圆的有限弧段；沿该弧段，对各模式求和后的电荷、标量密度和配对振幅是 Legendre 三类椭圆积分的组合。随着动量上限增大，该积分增加一个四次项、一个二次项以及一个系数已知的对数项。这两个幂次项分别被吸收到一个常数和耦合中，而在对所有动量积分后，余下的是 Legendre 积分与一个标度 $\lambda$ 的对数所构成的有限组合。相对论性 BCS-BEC 渡越的能隙方程和密度由此以闭合形式给出。一旦用散射长度替代耦合，$\lambda$ 就取代了文献中保留为第三个参数的截断。我们证明，它确定了配对通道的有效力程：对于质量为 $m$、配对顶角为 $\gamma_5$ 的一对 Dirac 费米子，$r_e=-(4/\pi m)[\ln(2\lambda/m)-1]$。在低密度和固定 $k_Fr_e$ 下，该渡越与具有相同散射长度和有效力程的 Feshbach 共振双通道模型的渡越相同。在 Nambu-Jona-Lasinio 模型中，π 介子衰变常数确定 $\lambda$，π 介子通道的有效力程为 $-8\pi f_\pi^{2}/(N_cM^{3})$，而夸克的组分质量 $M$ 是余下的一个自由参数。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

The relativistic BCS-BEC crossover in closed form: the cutoff as an effective range

At zero temperature the mean-field potential of a Dirac fermion with a pairing gap contains one momentum integral, that of the sum of its two quasiparticle levels. We evaluate it in closed form from a geometric construction: the two levels are distances in a plane, and the momentum line is a finite arc of a fixed ellipse, along which the charge, the scalar density and the pair amplitude, summed over the modes, are combinations of Legendre's three elliptic integrals. As the upper limit of the momentum grows, the integral grows by a quartic term, a quadratic term and a logarithm with a known coefficient. The two powers are absorbed into a constant and into the coupling, and with all momenta integrated what remains is a finite combination of Legendre's integrals and the logarithm of one scale $\lambda$. The gap equation and the density of the relativistic BCS-BEC crossover follow in closed form. Once the coupling has been traded for the scattering length, $\lambda$ takes the place of the cutoff that the literature keeps as a third parameter. We show that it fixes the effective range of the channel of the pair: for a pair of Dirac fermions of mass $m$ with the pairing vertex $\gamma_5$, $r_e=-(4/\pi m)[\ln(2\lambda/m)-1]$. At low density and a fixed $k_Fr_e$ the crossover is that of the two-channel model of a Feshbach resonance with the same scattering length and effective range. In the Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model the pion decay constant fixes $\lambda$, the effective range of the pion channel is $-8\pi f_\pi^{2}/(N_cM^{3})$, and the constituent mass $M$ of the quark is the one parameter that remains free.

Nuclear Excitation by Inelastic Positron Scattering in Hydrogenlike $^{229}\mathrm{Th}^{89+}$ Ions

We develop a relativistic theory of nuclear excitation by inelastic positron scattering (NEIPS) with nuclear hyperfine mixing (NHM) explicitly included and apply it to hydrogenlike $^{229}\mathrm{Th}^{89+}$ ions. The target ion is described by dressed hyperfine states, while the incident and outgoing positron continuum states are treated within a relativistic distorted-wave approach. We calculate hyperfine-resolved excitation cross sections for incident positron energies from $10^{2}$ to $10^{5}~\mathrm{eV}$. The cross sections increase strongly with the positron energy, with the $F_g=2\rightarrow F_e=2$ channel exhibiting the largest value. Comparison with NEIPS of the bare $^{229}\mathrm{Th}^{90+}$ nucleus shows that NHM enhances the excitation cross sections by about four orders of magnitude. These results demonstrate that NHM provides an efficient mechanism for enhancing NEIPS and broaden the range of nuclear excitation processes in which NHM plays a significant role.

Lightest $\Omega$-baryon systems with strangeness $-3$ and $-6$ within the framework of Faddeev and Faddeev--Yakubovsky equations

We investigate the binding energies and spatial properties of few-body $\Omega$-baryon systems with strangeness $S=-3$ and $S=-6$ using the configuration-space Faddeev and Faddeev--Yakubovsky equations. For the first time, microscopic Faddeev--Yakubovsky calculations are performed for the four- and five-body $\Omega NNN$, $\Omega NNNN$, and $\Omega\Omega NN$ systems. 利用张量网络研究 Schwinger 模型中矢量和标量束缚态的光锥分布

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

光锥分布编码了强子的部分子结构，但它们涉及欧几里得格点方法无法直接获取的实时关联。我们利用张量网络计算了有质量 Schwinger 模型中矢量和标量介子束缚态的部分子分布函数（PDFs）和光锥分布振幅（LCDAs）。我们采用通过 Gauss 定律消去规范场的 Kogut-Susskind 格点 Hamiltonian，将真空和介子制备为矩阵乘积态，并通过其电荷共轭和动量识别矢量和标量介子。定义这两种分布的规范不变光锥关联函数通过实时演化直接在 Minkowski 空间中求值。由电荷共轭决定，这两种介子呈现出定性不同的光锥结构。矢量介子具有单峰 PDF 和对称 LCDA，而标量介子具有双峰 PDF 和反对称 LCDA；后者为零的零阶矩要求通过其一阶矩进行归一化。由局域矩阵元和 LCDAs 的矩得到的衰变常数彼此一致，并随着费米子质量增加而趋近于非相对论模型的预言。对于本工作所考虑的相对较大质量，PDFs 与 LCDAs 的平方高度吻合，表明介子由其价组分主导。我们的结果表明，张量网络是获得低维规范理论中基态以及激发态强子光锥分布的一条实用途径。

查看原文标题与摘要

Light-cone distributions of vector and scalar bound states in the Schwinger model from tensor networks

Light-cone distributions encode the partonic structure of hadrons, but they involve real-time correlations that Euclidean lattice methods cannot access directly. We compute the parton distribution functions (PDFs) and light-cone distribution amplitudes (LCDAs) of the vector and scalar meson bound states of the massive Schwinger model with tensor networks. Using the Kogut-Susskind lattice Hamiltonian with the gauge field eliminated through Gauss's law, we prepare the vacuum and the mesons as matrix product states and identify the vector and scalar mesons through their charge conjugation and momentum. The gauge-invariant light-cone correlators that define both distributions are evaluated directly in Minkowski space by real-time evolution. The two mesons exhibit qualitatively different light-cone structure, dictated by charge conjugation. The vector meson has a single-peaked PDF and a symmetric LCDA, while the scalar meson has a double-peaked PDF and an antisymmetric LCDA, whose vanishing zeroth moment requires a normalization through its first moment. The decay constants obtained from local matrix elements and from the moments of the LCDAs agree, and approach predictions from nonrelativistic model as the fermion mass increases. For the relatively large masses considered in this work, the PDFs closely follow the squared LCDAs, showing that the mesons are dominated by their valence component. Our results demonstrate tensor networks as a practical route to light-cone distributions of ground-state as well as excited hadrons in low-dimensional gauge theories.

库仑相互作用诱导的 $K^- pp$ 分子态

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

我们研究了由一个反 kaon 和两个质子组成的三体系统中的一种新束缚态。该态主要由 $K^-$ 与两个质子之间的库仑相互作用诱导，并受到强 $K^- p$ 和 $pp$ 相互作用的修正。与主要由强相互作用引起、已得到广泛研究的准束缚 $K^- pp$ 态所具有的数十 MeV 量级的结合能和宽度相比，这种空间上延展的分子态的结合能和宽度为 keV 量级。

Coulomb-Induced $K^- pp$ Molecular State

We study a new bound state in the three-body system consisting of an antikaon and two protons. The state is primarily induced by Coulomb interactions between the $K^-$ and the two protons, and is modified by strong $K^- p$ and $pp$ interactions. This spatially extended molecular state has binding energy and width that are of the order of keV, compared to the tens of MeV binding energy and width of the much-studied quasi-bound $K^- pp$ state caused primarily by strong interactions.

与电动力学最小耦合的手征微扰理论中的 BPS 重子管及一种新颖的交叉转变

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

我们在 $(3+1)$ 维手征微扰理论（CPT）中推导出一种新颖的一阶 BPS 系统（其解自动也是二阶场方程的解）。这些 BPS 位形描述了沿管本身携带手征流的重子管。这类重子管沿 $z$ 轴既可以是均匀的，也可以是调制的。我们分析了 CPT 中的领头项，即与电动力学最小耦合的 $SU(2)$ 非线性 sigma 模型。这些精确解与现有的重子管解析描述在定性上不同，因为由于其 BPS 性质，能量密度峰的位置可以是任意的。相比之下（对于文献中已知的解），能量密度峰的位置是固定的。我们比较了这两类重子位形；决定哪一种在能量上更有利的相关参数是重子电荷密度：在低密度下，旧解更有利，而在高密度下，这些新颖的 BPS 管在能量上更有利。电磁场的纳入（以及其对重子管的相应反作用）也可以通过解析方法处理。我们讨论了两个区域（低重子密度和高重子密度）之间电磁场分布的差异。附录 B 详细分析了手征场的势能项。

查看原文标题与摘要

BPS baryonic tubes in chiral perturbation theory minimally coupled with electrodynamics and a novel crossover

We derive a novel first-order BPS system (whose solutions are automatically solutions of the second-order field equations) in chiral perturbation theory (CPT) in $(3+1)$ dimensions. These BPS configurations describe baryonic tubes carrying a chiral current along the tubes themselves. Such baryonic tubes can be either homogeneous or modulated along the $z$-axis. We analyze the leading term in CPT, which is the $SU(2)$ non-linear sigma model minimally coupled to electrodynamics. These exact solutions are qualitatively different from the available analytic description of baryonic tubes since, due to their BPS nature, the positions of the energy-density peaks can be arbitrary. In contrast (in the case of the known solutions in the literature), the energy-density peaks' positions are fixed. We compare these two types of baryonic configurations; the relevant parameter that determines which one is energetically favored is the baryonic charge density: for low densities, the old solutions are favorable, while for high densities these novel BPS tubes are energetically favored. The inclusion of the electromagnetic field (together with the corresponding back-reaction on the baryonic tubes) can also be treated analytically. We discuss the differences in the electromagnetic field distribution between the two regions (low and high baryonic density). The analysis of potential terms for the chiral field is detailed in Appendix B.

$QQ\bar Q\bar Q$ Quark System and Gauge/String Duality II: String Breaking

This is the second of two companion papers in which we continue developing the construction of the fully heavy tetraquark system using the gauge/string duality. Here, we extend this construction to model $SU(3)$ gauge theory with two light flavors of equal mass. As an application, we analyze the two lowest Born-Oppenheimer potentials, both analytically and numerically, for rectangular and collinear geometries. Our analysis reveals several critical scales associated with string reconnection and breaking, as well as junction annihilation and pinching. In addition, we find that the string tension is universal, whereas the string breaking distance is not.

相关短脉冲反射计理论

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

摘要 考虑了一种解析的 1D 模型，用于研究短脉冲反射计诊断，以评估测量电子密度涨落径向相关长度的可行性。得到了与不同探测频率相对应的脉冲延迟和宽度互相关函数的表达式。结果表明，与常规反射计相反，小尺度涨落在两种信号中均占主导地位。 解析结果通过 2D 全波模拟得到证实，表明 1D 结果在真实探测几何构型中的稳健性，但也揭示了其对非线性效应的敏感性。此外，还展示了与常规相关反射计一致的宽脉冲振幅相关函数，从而在同一测量中给出径向相关长度的上限。 基于这些解析结果，提出了一种基于单一探测频率测量径向相关长度的方法，并对其进行了数值测试。尽管结果表明该方法同时受到非线性效应和探测几何构型的影响，但在类 1D 几何构型中，它仍可给出湍流径向相关长度的数量级估计。

查看原文标题与摘要

Theory of correlation short pulse reflectometry

Abstract An analytical 1D model is considered for studying the short pulse reflectometry diagnostic for evaluating the feasibility of measuring the radial correlation length of the electron density fluctuations. Expressions are obtained for the cross-correlation function of pulse delays and widths corresponding to different probing frequencies. The results demonstrate the dominance of the small-scale fluctuations in both signals, contrary to conventional reflectometry. Analytical results are confirmed with 2D full-wave simulations, demonstrating the robustness of the 1D results in realistic probing geometry, but also revealing their sensitivity to nonlinear effects. Additionally, a wide correlation function of the pulse amplitude consistent with conventional correlation reflectometry is demonstrated, providing an upper bound for the radial correlation length within the same measurement. Based on these analytical results, a method for measuring the radial correlation length based on a single probing frequency is proposed and tested numerically. Although it is shown to be affected by both nonlinear effects and probing geometry, it nonetheless provides an order of magnitude estimate for the radial correlation length of the turbulence in 1D-like geometries.

受功率排出约束的 SPARC 分离界面运行空间

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

摘要 在本工作中，我们将分离界面运行空间（SepOS）预测外推至 SPARC，并引入脱靶准入判据，从而构建受功率排出约束的组合 SepOS（即 PE-SepOS），以评估全尺度下的综合功率排出方案。通过解读 SPARC SOLPS-ITER 数据集以及已在实验中得到验证的基础性工作，我们构建了耗散状态下 SOL 净功率和动量损失尽可能简单的描述，以将主要功率排出量（q ⊥ , T e,t , ⟨c Ne ⟩ sep ）与 SepOS 参数（n e,sep , T e,sep , Z eff , α t ）联系起来。通过该框架，我们证明了归一化 PE-SepOS 框架在针对给定的可排出 P sep 要求识别可达运行点方面的实用性。在应用 PE-SepOS 框架预测 SPARC 运行空间时，我们发现了内在的权衡，具体而言：i）进入高杂质辐射情景会导致 n e,sep 显著降低（例如，在 ⟨c Ne ⟩ sep =2% 时降低 50%），这是功率限制的结果；以及 ii）根据目前对准连续排出状态（QCE）准入判据的理解，为了在足够高密度、高 α t 条件下进入 QCE，需要在高辐射份额与高密度/中性粒子压强之间作出折中，同时偏滤器耗散状态向显著脱靶跃迁。我们进一步表明，通过在执行器 Γ D,core , Γ Ne , P in 与物理参数 T e,t 和 ⟨c Ne ⟩ sep 之间建立稳健的相关关系，PE-SepOS 框架可用于指导从低加热功率扩大至反应堆尺度 P sep /R 值的实验策略。利用能够对密度和杂质注入扫描进行预测的普适趋势，PE-SepOS 因而提供了一个以可扩展方式绘制边缘等离子体运行空间的框架，但须在 SPARC 早期运行期间使用适当的偏滤器和边缘等离子体可观测量进行验证。

The power exhaust constrained SPARC separatrix operational space

Abstract In this work we extrapolate the separatrix operational space (SepOS) projections to SPARC and introduce detachment access criteria, thus formulating the combined power exhaust constrained SepOS (i.e., PE-SepOS) to evaluate integrated power exhaust solutions at scale. Through the interpretation of SPARC SOLPS-ITER datasets and foundational work already demonstrated in experiments we formulate an as simple as possible description of the SOL net power and momentum losses in dissipative regimes to link the main power exhaust quantities (q ⊥ , T e,t , ⟨c Ne ⟩ sep ) with the SepOS parameters (n e,sep , T e,sep , Z eff , α t ). Through this framework, we demonstrate the utility of a normalized PE-SepOS framework in identifying accessible operational points for given exhaustible P sep requirements. In applying the PE-SepOS framework to project the SPARC operational space, we find inherent trade-offs, namely: i) accessing high impurity radiation scenarios leads to pronounced reductions in n e,sep (e.g., 50% reductions at ⟨c Ne ⟩ sep =2%) as a consequence of power limitation, and ii) given present understanding of access criteria to the quasi-continuous exhaust regime (QCE), a compromise between high radiative fraction and high density/neutral pressure is required for QCE access at sufficiently high density, high α t conditions, with the divertor dissipative regime transitioning to pronounced detachment. We further show that by establishing robust correlations between actuators Γ D,core , Γ Ne , P in , and physics parameters T e,t and ⟨c Ne ⟩ sep , the PE-SepOS framework can be used to inform experimental strategies in scaling up from low heating power to reactor-scale values of P sep /R. Taking advantage of universal trends enabling projections of density and impurity seeding scans, the PE-SepOS thus provides a framework for mapping out the edge plasma operational space in a scalable manner, subject to validation during early SPARC operations with suitable divertor and edge plasma observables.

通过氢成像研究氘在钨中的温度和几何构型依赖晶界输运

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

摘要 氚渗透钨等离子体面对部件是未来聚变反应堆中燃料滞留、库存控制和安全方面的一个关键问题。然而，晶界对多晶钨中氢同位素输运的贡献程度在实验上仍未得到充分厘清。在本工作中，采用图案化氢成像方法，以 10 µm 的空间分辨率研究氘在钨中的渗透。再结晶钨箔的一侧暴露于低温氘等离子体，而由钇或氧化钨薄膜组成的氢敏感指示点沉积在另一侧，以可视化局部渗透通量。使用几何构型有利于晶界主导渗透的钨箔，在 300 K 至 700 K 之间对晶界介导输运的温度依赖性进行了系统研究。结果揭示，随温度升高，具有渗透活性的晶界数量增加。在 630 K 及以上温度，若干晶界周围形成明显的氘通量密度晕环，表明氘解吸开始，并逐渐向体相主导的输运跃迁。进一步在 600 K 下使用平均横向晶粒尺寸与厚度纵横比分别为 (0.2 ± 0.1)、(1.1 ± 0.7) 和 (1.6 ± 0.8) 的钨箔研究了微观结构几何构型的影响。对于两个较低的纵横比，氘输运由晶界渗透主导，而最高的纵横比则促进体相输运，并伴随若干晶界附近渗透的局部耗减。这些发现为空间分辨的钨中晶界介导氢输运提供了实验证据，并为旨在预测聚变反应堆材料中氚渗透的模型提供了约束。

Temperature- and Geometry-dependent grain boundary transport of deuterium in tungsten by hydrogenography

Abstract Tritium permeation through tungsten plasma-facing components is a key concern for fuel retention, inventory control, and safety in future fusion reactors. However, the extent to which grain boundaries contribute to hydrogen isotope transport in polycrystalline tungsten remains insufficiently resolved experimentally. In this work, deuterium permeation through tungsten is investigated with spatial resolution of 10 µm, using a patterned hydrogenography approach. Recrystallized tungsten foils were exposed to low-temperature deuterium plasma on one side, while hydrogen-sensitive indicator dots, consisting of either yttrium or tungsten oxide thin films, were deposited on the opposite side to visualize the local permeation flux. A systematic study of the temperature dependence of grain-boundary-mediated transport was conducted between 300 K and 700 K using tungsten foils with geometries favoring grain-boundary-dominated permeation. The results reveal an increasing number of permeation-active grain boundaries with increasing temperature. At temperatures of 630 K and above, distinct halos of deuterium flux density form around several grain boundaries, indicating the onset of deuterium desorption and a gradual transition toward bulk-dominated transport. The influence of microstructural geometry was further investigated at 600 K using tungsten foils with average lateral grain size to thickness aspect ratios of (0.2 ± 0.1), (1.1 ± 0.7), and (1.6 ± 0.8). For the two lower aspect ratios, deuterium transport is dominated by grain boundary permeation, whereas the highest aspect ratio promotes bulk transport accompanied by local depletion of permeation near several grain boundaries. These findings provide spatially resolved experimental evidence of grain-boundary mediated hydrogen transport in tungsten and offer constraints for models aimed at predicting tritium permeation in fusion reactor materials

损失聚变 alpha 粒子对 SPARC 等离子体面对部件的热负荷和粒子负荷

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

摘要 SPARC 托卡马克旨在 Q > 10 的燃烧等离子体中产生高达 140 MW 的聚变功率。在外中平面处等离子体与限制器间隙可能小于 1 cm 的情况下，在这些高功率脉冲中，未受约束的聚变 alpha 粒子在某些等离子体面对部件（PFCs）上引起的热负荷可能十分显著；环向场（TF）线圈的失准会导致 TF 波纹和快离子约束损失，从而增大这些负荷，PFCs 的失准也会增大这些负荷。在本工作中，基于 TF 线圈和 PFCs 的实际 CAD 模型，使用 Monte Carlo 轨道跟踪程序 ASCOT5 对 SPARC 中的 alpha 粒子损失和 PFC 负荷进行了模拟，并允许存在当前 SPARC 装配公差范围内的失准。采用一种渐近方法来近似空间尺度小于三角形壁面网格的 PFC 边缘峰值热负荷，预测由 alpha 粒子引起的峰值热负荷介于 2.5 − 22 MW/m 2 之间，具体取决于 TF 和 PFC 的对准情况。研究了外侧间隙尺寸、等离子体电流和磁场强度的影响，并计算了预测的 alpha 粒子负荷所引起的钨 PFCs 温升、再结晶和溅射；结果表明，即使在可能的最恶劣 alpha 粒子负荷下，SPARC 仍可运行持续 5−10 s 的高性能脉冲，但在对准不良时会受到一些限制。随着 SPARC 进入燃烧等离子体状态，这些结果将用于指导其安全运行。

Heat and particle loading of SPARC PFCs by lost fusion alphas

Abstract The SPARC tokamak aims to produce up to 140 MW of fusion power in a Q > 10 burning plasma. With the potential for plasma-limiter gaps of less than 1 cm at the outer midplane, heat loads induced by unconfined fusion alphas on certain plasma-facing components (PFCs) may be significant in these high-power pulses, and these loads can be increased by misalignments of the toroidal field (TF) coils, which leads to TF ripple and loss of fast ion confinement, as well as by misalignments of the PFCs. In this work, simulations of alpha losses and PFC loads in SPARC are performed with the Monte Carlo orbit-following code ASCOT5 based on actual CAD models of the TF coils and PFCs, allowing misalignments within current SPARC assembly tolerances. An asymptotic method is used to approximate peak heat loads to PFC edges on spatial scales smaller than the triangular wall mesh, and the peak alpha-induced heat loads are predicted to be between 2.5 − 22 MW/m 2 depending on TF and PFC alignment. The impact of outer gap size, plasma current, and magnetic field strength are investigated, and temperature rise, recrystallization, and sputtering of tungsten PFCs induced by the predicted alpha loads are calculated, showing that even with the worst possible alpha loads, it will be possible to run high performance pulses in SPARC for 5−10 s, with some restrictions if alignment is poor. These results will be used to inform safe operation of SPARC as it moves into the burning plasma regime.

First divertor exposure experiments of a renewable boron pebble aggregate in DIII-D

Abstract Boron pebble aggregate was tested for the first time as a high-heat-flux granular plasma facing material in a tokamak divertor. Exposures of up to q∥ = 80 MW/m² incident heat flux were conducted in the DIII-D tokamak. Single protruding rods of pebble aggregate composed of sintered amorphous boron pebbles bound with carbon binder were mounted in the Divertor Material Evaluation System (DiMES) sample holders and exposed to L-mode lower single null (LSN) plasmas. Under these heat loads, significant boron dust emission from the boron spheres was observed, and this dust dominates the divertor boron ionization source. Only about half of the released boron was recovered locally as mm-sized particles, with the rest presumably lost mainly as dust into the plasma and vacuum chamber. Preliminary estimates suggest that the rate of surface recession of ~1 cm/s in the pebble conglomerate within the plasma divertor is consistent with the recession rates observed in laser bench tests subjected to normal incidence heat loads. Although core performance was not adversely affected by the high boron dust emission, future work will need to improve the boron pebble aggregate design to reduce boron dust emission at high heat loads.

类时电磁形状因子中的奇异双光子交换

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

尽管类空核子电磁形状因子中的双光子交换（TPE）效应在二十多年间已得到广泛研究，但其在类时区域中的表现仍有待确证。我们提出将同位旋破缺衰变 J / ψ → Σ ¯ 0 Λ 及其电荷共轭过程作为探测 TPE 对类时超子电磁形状因子贡献的灵敏探针。该道通过灵敏且稳健的极化和自旋关联观测量，为分离 TPE 效应提供了一个纯净的研究平台，而 Λ − Σ 0 质量劈裂尤其增强了这些观测量。通过将单光子交换贡献的色散关系分析与 TPE 振幅的显式圈图计算相结合，我们对阈值区域中的 TPE 效应进行了定量研究。我们的结果表明，所考虑的观测量能够提供 TPE 贡献的明确特征，并给出了其大小及相关理论不确定度的预测。

Strange two-photon exchange in timelike electromagnetic form factors

While two-photon exchange (TPE) effects in spacelike nucleon electromagnetic form factors have been extensively studied for over two decades, their manifestations in the timelike region have yet to be definitively established. We propose the isospin-violating decay J / ψ → Σ ¯ 0 Λ and its charge-conjugate process as a sensitive probe of TPE contributions to timelike hyperon electromagnetic form factors. This channel provides a clean laboratory for isolating TPE effects through sensitive and robust polarization and spin-correlation observables, which are particularly enhanced by the Λ − Σ 0 mass splitting. Combining a dispersion-relation analysis of the one-photon-exchange contribution with explicit loop calculations of the TPE amplitudes, we perform a quantitative study of TPE effects in the threshold region. Our results demonstrate that the considered observables can provide unambiguous signatures of TPE contributions and present predictions for their size together with the associated theoretical uncertainties.

Multispectral representation of Distributed Acoustic Sensing data: a framework for physically interpretable feature extraction and visualization

Distributed Acoustic Sensing (DAS) enables continuous monitoring of dynamic strain along tens of kilometres of optical fibre, generating massive datasets whose interpretation and automated analysis remain challenging. DAS data can be understood as a time-distance image array. However, the absence of standardised representation methods limits both the physical interpretability and the development of automated image-based analysis pipelines. This work introduces a systematic framework for visualisation and feature extraction of DAS data based on a multispectral signal representation. The approach decomposes strain-rate measurements into predefined frequency bands and produces band-limited energy images that describe the spatial and temporal distribution of acoustic energy across distinct spectral regimes. The framework is evaluated using DAS recordings containing fin whale and blue whale vocalisations. Three experiments are conducted to assess the approach: enhanced visualisation of bioacoustic signals, unsupervised clustering of acoustic patterns, and event detection using a convolutional neural network. The visualisation experiment shows that the multispectral representation allows for the simultaneous visualisation of sets of events that cannot be visualised using the strain amplitude representation. The unsupervised clustering experiment confirms that the multispectral representation yields class-discriminative feature spaces without requiring labelled data, enabling the automatic grouping of acoustically distinct event types. Finally, the use of multispectral composites as input of a ResNet-18 achieves an accuracy of 95.9% in whale vocalisation detection, demonstrating that the proposed representation provides an effective feature space for automated analysis.
