Design, Testing and Calibration of the GAPS Experiment Si(Li) Tracker Readout ASIC: From the First Flight Campaign Toward the Second Mission Upgrade

The General Antiparticle Spectrometer (GAPS) is a balloon-borne experiment designed to perform three long-duration flights from Antarctica to search for low-energy cosmic antinuclei, in particular antideuterons, as an indirect signature of dark matter. Its detection method relies on the formation and decay of exotic atoms, identified through the emission of characteristic x-rays and the subsequent annihilation into secondary particles. At the core of the instrument is the silicon tracker, composed of lithium-drifted silicon detectors operated at -40{\deg}C and read out by a custom application-specific integrated circuit, SLIDER32, implemented in 180 nm CMOS technology. This thesis reports on the integration, calibration, and validation of the SLIDER32-based tracker readout electronics. These activities were part of the integration and testing phase for the first flight, culminating in the complete instrument assembly and validation during the 2024 Antarctic campaign. Building on this experience, the work presents the design and characterization of ANTARES4, a prototype integrated circuit in 65 nm CMOS technology conceived as an upgrade to SLIDER32. The chip integrates eight analog channels up to the shaping stage and implements dynamic signal compression based on dynamic-threshold MOS transistors, together with an improved detector leakage current compensation circuit at the preamplifier input. Its characterization provides a first validation of these design choices, laying the foundations for the next-generation tracker readout to be adopted in the second flight of the experiment.

TWIN-1: A new 185 to 350 MHz Two-Element Radio Interferometer for Solar Observation at Gauribidanur

We present details of a new two-element radio interferometer, namely the TWIN, developed for routine monitoring of solar events at the Gauribidanur Observatory (77.428{\deg}E, 13.603{\deg}N) between 185 MHz and 350 MHz. This interferometer is built using custom-built antennas, an indigenous RF front-end consisting of LNAs, power combiners, and high-gain amplifiers, and RF over fiber signal transport modules. A data capture system was developed using a commercial digital storage oscilloscope and controlled with custom-designed Python-based tools. The oscilloscope digitises the RF signals with a time resolution of 0.8 ns, bursts of 9952 time samples, with a nominal duty cycle of 3 seconds, and the resultant data is collected on a remote computer. Specialised tools were developed to analyse the data and detect solar events. The interferometer has been operational since July 2023. In our analysis, we have detected several solar events from the ongoing Solar Cycle 25. This paper outlines the design philosophy, signal flow, commissioning and validation, results from a solar event, and future plans for data capture, data analysis, and archival.

Noncontact Chemical and Morphological Profiling in Liquid via Fiber-Based Micro-FTIR

Liquid-phase mid-infrared (MIR) imaging is challenging due to the strong MIR light absorption of most liquid media. Existing liquid-phase MIR microscopy approaches overcame the background absorption using predominantly either ultrathin liquid cells or attenuated total reflection (ATR) setups. However, these designs introduce additional challenges, such as sample confinement, limited probe volume, probe perturbation, and/or lack of quantification capabilities. In this work, a fiber-based Fourier-transform infrared (FTIR) microspectroscopy method was developed to overcome the technical barriers of liquid-phase MIR characterization. By integrating a tapered fiber microprobe, a transmission-based optical setup, and a scanning stage, we achieved noncontact chemical and morphological profiling in liquid. The micro-FTIR capabilities were benchmarked by measuring a micropatterned photoresist substrate in water, obtaining quantitative spatial chemical distribution and morphological profiles with ~35 {\mu}m lateral resolution and nanometer-scale vertical detection limit. This noncontact, quantitative, liquid-phase micro-FTIR technique is promising for non-perturbative characterization of heterogeneous structures in liquids.

Longitudinal-dither wavefront sensing: Calibration-free measurement of mode mismatch

Mode mismatch between a gravitational-wave detector and the beams injected into it lowers the power stored in the arms and the level of squeezing. Correcting it requires the complex amplitudes of the three second-order modes, measured on a calibrated scale with the cavity on resonance at its operating power. Here we present a method that obtains a signal proportional to the mismatch by moving the beam on the wavefront sensors already used for alignment control. A longitudinal dither of the cavity adds to the resonant mode a reference field, which reaches the sensors through the same signal chains and so calibrates both the scale of this signal and the Gouy phases of the sensors and actuators, with no external calibration. We demonstrate the method on an optical cavity held on resonance in its operating state at input powers of 2 W and 62 W. At 2 W it yields an optical model that agrees with independent measurements in the mode content, and with the layout model in the actuator Gouy phase within its uncertainty of about $1^\circ$. At 62 W the thermally induced change of the eigenmode is decomposed into waist-size and waist-position changes. The method requires only components that every controlled cavity already possesses and enables continuous in situ monitoring of the mode matching in the operating state.

A source-based decomposition of thermal noise for mechanical system design

Thermal noise limits the sensitivity of precision mechanical instruments. We present a thermal- noise formulation for lumped-element mechanical systems that writes the total displacement thermal noise at the readout as the sum of contributions from all dissipative elements. For each contribution, we separate the thermal force generated by the element from its transfer to the readout. This decomposition provides simple design rules for comparing contributions from elements in series and in parallel and identifying the dominant noise source. It applies to frequency-dependent dissipation and to elements at different steady temperatures under local thermal equilibrium. We use it to identify the frequency regimes in which parallel and series dissipation dilution reduce displacement thermal noise. We then use examples of a pendulum on a lossy support, a rotational flexure, and rigidly mounted lunar optics to show how support noise can dominate even when the support has little effect on the system dynamics. Finally, a feedback-controlled suspension illustrates how sensing and actuation noise can be included in the same displacement-noise budget.

基于广义液滴模型的对称能四阶项研究

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

核物质的四阶对称能及有限原子核中相应的 $$I^4$$ 项仍然知之甚少。在液滴质量公式中，该项与 Wigner 项相竞争，因此提取出的对称能系数取决于为 Wigner 能所假定的形式。在本研究中，通过加入四阶体对称项和 Wigner 项扩展液滴质量公式，并通过拟合 AME2020 质量比较 Wigner 项的三种常见形式。采用选定的形式，通过自助重采样将同一公式分别拟合到 AME2020 数据以及 FRDM2012、WS4、WS4+RBF、HFB24 和 HFB31 质量表。该统一分析提取每个质量表中编码的二阶、四阶和 Wigner 同位旋贡献，并在相同基础上对其进行比较；实验质量表得到负的四阶系数，其符号与所有理论质量表相反。仅在已测量原子核区域内进行拟合时，理论质量表也给出相同的负系数，因此，这种符号差异源于它们在远离稳定线区域的外推。随后，将体对称系数和表面对称系数转换为核物质对称能的斜率 L 和曲率 $$K_{\text {sym}}$$，并评估四阶项对这些约束的影响。

Study of the fourth-order term of the symmetry energy based on the generalized liquid drop model

The fourth-order symmetry energy of nuclear matter and the corresponding $$I^4$$ term of finite nuclei are still poorly known. In the liquid drop mass formula this term competes with the Wigner term, so the extracted symmetry energy coefficients depend on the form assumed for the Wigner energy. In this work the liquid drop mass formula is extended by a fourth-order volume symmetry term and a Wigner term, and three common forms of the Wigner term are compared by fitting the AME2020 masses. With the selected form, the same formula is fitted by bootstrap resampling to the AME2020 data and to the FRDM2012, WS4, WS4+RBF, HFB24 and HFB31 mass tables. This common analysis reads out the second-order, fourth-order and Wigner isospin contributions encoded in each mass table and compares them on the same footing; the experimental table yields a negative fourth-order coefficient, opposite in sign to all theoretical tables. Fitted only in the region of measured nuclei, the theoretical tables give the same negative coefficient, so the sign difference arises from their extrapolation far from stability. The volume and surface symmetry coefficients are then converted into the slope L and curvature $$K_{\text {sym}}$$ of the nuclear matter symmetry energy, and the effect of the fourth-order term on these constraints is evaluated.

节点角色发现揭示复杂网络中新颖的结构分解和拓扑转变

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

大多数复杂系统都可以用图或网络来表征。网络通过边（航班）连接节点（例如机场），从而概括系统的结构。探究图的一种常用方法是社区检测，它可以揭示由相互邻近且连接密集的节点构成的集合。另一种做法是，我们可以考察一个节点在网络中占据何种位置，并根据拓扑或结构相似性对它们进行聚类，而不考虑它们之间连接的距离和密度。我们利用这一方法研究了一系列表征相关复杂系统的网络。在我们的框架中，每个图都按照显著性顺序将我们的注意力引向其关键特征。得益于此，我们揭示了可为基于社区的分析提供补充的、信息丰富的网络分解。我们以全球机场网络为例阐释该框架，并为其发现了一种基于机场功能的非平凡分解。我们还将该方法应用于美国众议院法案共同提案图，从中发现了合作模式的剧烈重组，这可能是由极化驱动的。对更多网络的其他简要分析凸显了该框架的通用性。

Node role discovery reveals novel structural decompositions and topological shifts in complex networks

Most complex systems can be captured by graphs or networks. Networks connect nodes (e.g., airports) through edges (flights), thus summarizing the system's structure. A popular way of interrogating graphs is community detection, which uncovers sets of close and densely connected nodes. Alternatively, we may ask what a node occupies within a network, and cluster them according to topological or structural similarity, irrespective of the distance and density of connections between them. We exploit to study a series of networks that capture relevant complex systems. In our framework, each graph guides our attention to its key features, in order of salience. Thanks to this, we uncover informative network decompositions that complement community-based analyses. We illustrate this framework for the global airport network, for which we discover a nontrivial decomposition based on airport functionality. We also apply the approach to the U.S. House bill cosponsorship graph, in which we discover a drastic reorganization of cooperation patterns likely driven by polarization. Other brief analyses on more networks highlight the framework's versatility.

两核子有效场论的自举分析

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

手征 EFT 会产生奇异势，在将其用于 Lippmann–Schwinger 方程等动力学方程时，需要进行正规化和重整化。我们采用两种不同的方法：使用接触项进行重整化——这是手征 EFT 中最常用的做法——以及采用多次减除的精确 N/D 方法。我们从一个玩具模型入手，在该模型中，我们可以控制势的有限力程展开，并将完整势视为“精确”理论。为了评估这些方法与完整理论在统计上的一致性，我们采用自举技术。我们将同一框架用于研究 LO 和 NLO 阶手征 EFT 与 Granada 相移分析在 $$^1S_0$$ 两核子分波中的一致性。我们的结果表明，NLO 势显著扩展了理论保持有效的能量范围。

查看原文标题与摘要

Bootstrapping two-nucleon effective field theories

Chiral EFT yields singular potentials that require regularization and renormalization when implemented in a dynamical equation such as the Lippmann–Schwinger equation. We employ two different approaches, renormalization with contact terms—as is most commonly done in chiral EFT—and the exact N/D method with multiple subtractions. We start with a toy model in which we can control the finite-range expansion of the potential, treating the full potential as the ‘exact’ theory. To assess the statistical consistency of the approaches with the full theory, we use the bootstrap technique. We apply the same framework to study the consistency of chiral EFT at LO and NLO with the Granada phase-shift analysis in the $$^1S_0$$ two-nucleon partial wave. Our results show that the NLO potential significantly extends the energy range over which the theory remains valid.

Toward light-controlled transverse Wigner ordering in attosecond electron beams

Kinetic fluctuations and space-charge expansion hinder Wigner ordering in free-electron beams. Here we propose a laser-driven route toward transient, substrate-free Wigner ordering that combines photon-induced near-field electron microscopy (PINEM) attosecond bunching, synchronized magnetic focusing, and spatial preconditioning. Classical relaxation and zero-temperature fixed-node diffusion Monte Carlo trap benchmarks show that sixfold order survives quantum fluctuations from 1 T to sub-Tesla fields, defining ultrafast preparation targets.

仿星器中的逃逸电子：不太可能还是不可避免？

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

历史上，相对论性逃逸电子的产生一直未被认为是仿星器中可能出现的问题，但尽管不存在外部驱动的等离子体电流，这一判断对于反应堆尺度的仿星器可能并不成立。等离子体电流的大小支配着逃逸电子的指数产生，即使不存在外部驱动的等离子体电流，自举电流在与反应堆相关的仿星器中也可能相当可观。在本文中，我们对仿星器温度坍缩情景下逃逸电子的产生进行了先导研究。为了可靠地研究仿星器中的逃逸电子，我们在逃逸电子模拟工具中实现了一个仿星器等离子体模型。该模型用于探索在初始等离子体电流、温度衰减时间尺度和衰减后温度的不同组合下，何时会产生逃逸电子。我们特别考察了仿星器中通过雪崩倍增产生逃逸电子的过程，以及它与托卡马克中相应过程的比较。我们发现，仿星器中也可能产生数量显著的逃逸电子，并说明了在哪些条件下它可能成为值得关注的问题。然而，我们的研究结果支持这样一种观点：与托卡马克相比，逃逸电子在反应堆尺度的仿星器中将不那么值得关注。

查看原文标题与摘要

Runaway electrons in stellarators: Unlikely or unavoidable?

Generation of relativistic runaway electrons has historically not been considered a possible problem in stellarators, but this may not hold in reactor-scale stellarators despite the lack of an externally driven plasma current. The magnitude of the plasma current governs the exponential generation of runaways, and even if there is no externally driven plasma current, the bootstrap current could be considerable in reactor-relevant stellarators. In this paper, we present a pilot study on the generation of runaway electrons in stellarator temperature collapse scenarios. To reliably study runaway electrons in stellarators, we implemented a stellarator plasma model in the runaway electron simulation tool . The model is used to explore when runaway electrons can be generated with regard to combinations of initial plasma current, temperature decay timescale, and postdecay temperature. Special consideration is given to runaway generation through avalanche multiplication in stellarators, and how it compares to tokamaks. We find that significant runaway electron generation is possible also in stellarators, and demonstrate under which conditions it could be a concern. However, our findings support the conception that runaway electrons will be less of a concern in reactor-scale stellarators compared to tokamaks.

On the potential of widening the band of X-ray polarimetry

The Imaging X-ray Polarimetry Explorer (IXPE) has made X-ray polarimetry a routine astrophysical diagnostic, but its sensitivity is confined to the 2-8 keV band, where the photoelectric effect in a gas is most efficiently exploited. Several of the questions raised by its results, as well as some classical goals of high-energy astrophysics, require polarimetry below 2~keV, to reach the thermal emission of neutron stars, black-hole discs and blazars, and above 8~keV, where reflection, cyclotron lines and the non-thermal emission of supernova remnants, pulsar wind nebulae and magnetars can be isolated. Following a recent review of the subject, we discuss the scientific case for extending the band over roughly 0.1-80 keV and beyond, and the techniques that can deliver it: Bragg diffraction on graded multilayers, photoelectric gas imagers read out by pixel ASICs with time-of-arrival capability, time projection chambers and focal-plane Compton polarimeters, including their combination in stacked focal planes. We then summarise how these techniques are being implemented, or could be, in REDSoX and GOSoX, eXTP, XPP, WXPT and in EXPO and PHEMTO, proposed to the ESA M8 call, as well as in the wide-field transient polarimeter POLAR-2, and we discuss the trade-offs that will drive the design of a wide-band polarimetry mission.

具有大角度扰动的高能电子-正电子束碰撞

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

我们证明，高能电子-正电子碰撞中的束流和场动力学受一个新的无量纲参数$\varepsilon$支配。在高扰动状态下，粒子以$\varepsilon$量级的角度发生横向偏转，同时经历按$\varepsilon^2$标度变化的纵向减速，并伴随出现振幅与$\varepsilon$成正比的纵向电场。我们将大角度扰动（$\varepsilon \ge 1$）确定为一种新的极端状态，其中横向运动变得具有强相对论性，制动效应使束流传播完全停止并随后反向。我们的理论模型与三维电磁粒子网格模拟高度一致。现有束束研究仅在$\varepsilon \ll 1$极限下有效。对于拟议中在量子状态下运行的等离子体线性对撞机，显著的束束效应会产生低能次级粒子，其$\varepsilon$可接近一；因此，为了准确建模，这些粒子的碰撞动力学和行为可能需要全电磁粒子网格模拟。

查看原文标题与摘要

High-energy electron-positron beam collisions with large-angle disruptions

We demonstrate that the beam and field dynamics in high-energy electron-positron collisions are governed by a new dimensionless parameter $\varepsilon$. In the high-disruption regime, particles are deflected transversely at angles of order $\varepsilon$ while simultaneously undergoing longitudinal deceleration which scales as $\varepsilon^2$, accompanied by the emergence of a longitudinal electric field with amplitude proportional to $\varepsilon$. We identify the large-angle disruptions ($\varepsilon \ge 1$) as a novel extreme regime in which transverse motion becomes strongly relativistic and the braking effect fully arrests, and subsequently reverses, beam propagation. Our theoretical model is in good agreement with three-dimensional electromagnetic particle-in-cell simulations. Existing beam-beam studies are valid only in the $\varepsilon \ll 1$ limit. For proposed plasma-based linear colliders operating in the quantum regime, notable beam-beam effects generate low-energy secondary particles whose $\varepsilon$ can approach unity; the collision dynamics and behavior of these particles may therefore require fully electromagnetic particle-in-cell simulations for accurate modeling.

在下一代暗物质探测器中发现翘曲额外维度

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

未来几年可能会见证搜寻WIMP暗物质（DM）的最后冲刺，直接探测将深入中微子雾区，间接探测将触及TeV尺度的热遗迹粒子。我们研究了一类可能被这些实验发现的模型：源自翘曲额外维度的DM。在IR中，这些情景一般表现为一个$Z'$型门户。我们表明，在这种自下而上的背景下，具有电弱耦合和TeV尺度质量的DM仍然可行且可被发现。然而，这些模型要求DM与$Z'$质量之间存在20\%的微调；如果DM与$Z'$为强耦合且质量更大（$\sim$$10$ TeV），这种微调要求便会放宽。事实上，正是强耦合的$Z'$型门户由翘曲额外维度大统一理论（GUT）框架提供UV完备化，其中DM的稳定性是抑制质子衰变的衍生结果，从而产生$\sim$$10$ TeV的Kaluza-Klein（KK）尺度。我们确定了这一框架的一种变体，其中除重子数规范玻色子之外的所有规范场与DM的耦合均受到抑制；该模型显著抑制了直接探测率，但仍可通过间接探测被发现。此外，为了解决与$\sim$$10$ TeV KK尺度相关的小层级问题，受超复合Higgs的启发，我们在额外维度GUT设定中加入了超对称性。我们表明，该模型也具有一个DM候选者，此时其$Z'$媒介子为弱耦合且质量为$\sim$${\rm TeV}$。在所有情况下，近期内仍有可能取得发现，例如利用现已投入运行的Cherenkov望远镜阵列天文台（CTAO）；这表明，我们关于额外维度的首个线索可能来自高能天空。

Discovering Warped Extra Dimensions at Next Generation Dark Matter Detectors

The coming years will see what could be the final push in the search for WIMP dark matter (DM), with direct detection reaching into the neutrino fog and indirect detection reaching TeV-scale thermal relics. We study a class of models that these experiments could discover: DM emerging from a warped extra dimension. In the IR, these scenarios appear generically as a $Z'$-type portal. We show that DM with electroweak couplings and TeV scale masses remains viable and discoverable within such a bottom-up context. However, the models require a 20\% tuning between the DM and $Z'$ masses, a tuning that is relaxed if the DM and $Z'$ are strongly coupled and heavier ($\sim$$10$ TeV). In fact, it is the strongly-coupled $Z'$-type portal which is UV-completed by the framework of a warped extra-dimensional Grand Unified Theory (GUT), where DM stability is a spin-off of suppressing proton decay, resulting in a $\sim$$10$ TeV Kaluza-Klein (KK) scale. We identify a variant of this framework where all gauge fields except a baryon-number gauge boson have suppressed couplings to the DM; this model significantly suppresses the direct detection rate, but it remains discoverable through indirect detection. Further, to address the little hierarchy problem associated with $\sim$$10$ TeV KK scale, inspired by the Super-Composite Higgs, we augment our extra-dimensional GUT setup with supersymmetry. We show that this model also possesses a DM candidate, now with a weakly-coupled and $\sim$${\rm TeV}$ mass $Z'$ mediator. In all cases, a near-term discovery remains possible, for instance with the now online Cherenkov Telescope Array Observatory (CTAO), suggesting that our first hint of extra dimensions could emerge from the high-energy sky.

Slipstream：PyTorch中的非线性束流动力学

Slipstream是一个用于储存环、直线加速器和光阴极注入器中非线性束流动力学的PyTorch软件包。它提供CPU和GPU粒子跟踪、包含空间电荷的场图注入器模型，以及沿所支持跟踪路径的自动微分。通过与elegant、ASTRA、GPT及其他代码进行比较，评估非线性环跟踪、光学匹配和光阴极注入器计算的准确性与计算成本。环调谐与elegant的一致程度达到$10^{-9}$。对于所测试的包含数万粒子的环计算任务，采用float32的GPU跟踪比此处比较的单线程CPU实现快数百倍。与有限差分相比，自动微分将24参数光学匹配所需的时间缩短了一个数量级。以高亮度光阴极注入器为例，在20和100pC时，最终水平发射度与ASTRA的结果相差约1%，在250pC时则相差$\approx$4%。对于$2^{18}$个宏粒子，Slipstream较快的默认设置在RTX 3050 GPU上耗时14s，所得发射度与ASTRA结果的差异在2%以内。其他测试考察了相干同步辐射、动态孔径和阻性壁阻抗。该软件包主要由AI编码助手在人类指导下开发。这些比较明确了其模型、GPU执行和导数在扫描、拟合与优化的重复模拟中适用的情形。

Slipstream: nonlinear beam dynamics in PyTorch

Slipstream is a PyTorch package for nonlinear beam dynamics in storage rings, linacs and photoinjectors. It provides CPU and GPU particle tracking, field-map injector models with space charge, and automatic differentiation along supported tracking paths. Comparisons with elegant, ASTRA, GPT and other codes assess accuracy and computational cost for nonlinear ring tracking, optics matching and photoinjectors. Ring tunes agree with elegant to $10^{-9}$. For the tested ring workloads with tens of thousands of particles, GPU tracking in float32 is hundreds of times faster than the single-threaded CPU implementations compared here. Automatic differentiation reduces the time for 24-parameter optics matching by an order of magnitude compared to finite differences. Using a high-brightness photo-injector as an example, final horizontal emittances agree with ASTRA to about 1% at 20 and 100pC and differ by $\approx$4% at 250pC. For $2^{18}$ macroparticles, Slipstream's faster default settings take 14s on an RTX 3050 GPU and give emittances within 2% of that of ASTRA. Additional tests examine coherent synchrotron radiation, dynamic aperture and resistive-wall impedance. The package was developed predominantly by AI coding assistants under human direction. These comparisons identify where its models, GPU execution and derivatives are useful for repeated simulations in scans, fits and optimisation.

通过引力拉曼散射研究中子星中的潮汐加热

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

我们提出一种散射振幅形式体系，结合世界线有效场论和相对论恒星微扰理论，研究无自旋中子星的潮汐加热效应。在中子星中，潮汐加热源于内部各种散射过程所导致的流体黏性。它还充当中子星与其环境之间交换能量和角动量的通道。在中子星内部，我们首先推导出两个主微扰方程，它们能够描述在频率线性阶精确的流体微扰。值得注意的是，由于恒星流体中非正压微扰所产生的一种特殊绝热不可压缩性，这些方程没有来自体黏性的贡献。在外部，度规微扰约化为 Regge-Wheeler (RW) 方程，我们使用解析的 Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi (MST) 方法求解该方程。我们计算引力波被中子星散射的振幅，这也称为引力拉曼散射。根据该振幅，我们得到电四极静态 Love 数和领先耗散数在致密度至所有阶的表达式。随后，我们针对各种现实的状态方程计算领先耗散数，并估算在 LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) 频段内旋期间潮汐加热导致的引力波周期数变化。我们发现，剪切黏性贡献很小。即使对于冷的（$T\sim 10^5$K）且弱致密的恒星，它引起的周期数变化也小于 $10^{-2}$，比其他保守的 4PN 贡献低约一个数量级，并且对于温度更高的恒星，它随 $T^{-2}$ 减小。

查看原文标题与摘要

Investigating tidal heating in neutron stars via gravitational Raman scattering

We present a scattering amplitude formalism to study the tidal heating effects of nonspinning neutron stars incorporating both worldline effective field theory and relativistic stellar perturbation theory. In neutron stars, tidal heating arises from fluid viscosity due to various scattering processes in the interior. It also serves as a channel for the exchange of energy and angular momentum between the neutron star and its environment. In the interior of the neutron star, we first derive two master perturbation equations that capture fluid perturbations accurate to linear order in frequency. Remarkably, these equations receive no contribution from bulk viscosity due to a peculiar adiabatic incompressibility which arises in stellar fluid for non-barotropic perturbations. In the exterior, the metric perturbations reduce to the Regge-Wheeler (RW) equation which we solve using the analytical Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi (MST) method. We compute the amplitude for gravitational waves scattering off a neutron star, also known as gravitational Raman scattering. From the amplitude, we obtain expressions for the electric quadrupolar static Love number and the leading dissipation number to all orders in compactness. We then compute the leading dissipation number for various realistic equation-of-state(s) and estimate the change in the number of gravitational wave cycles due to tidal heating during inspiral in the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) band. We find that the shear-viscous contribution is small. Even for cold ($T\sim 10^5$K) and weakly compact stars it changes the number of cycles by less than $10^{-2}$, about an order of magnitude below the other conservative 4PN contributions, and it decreases as $T^{-2}$ for hotter stars.

弹性极向振荡与核参数

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

由于中子星的弹性，除基频模、压力模和重力模之外，剪切模和界面模也会被激发。尽管直接观测振荡谱仍然具有挑战性，但这些模式的潜在共振特征可能会显现在双中子星并合产生的短伽马射线暴的前兆中。如果观测到振荡谱，这将是通过星震学技术提取物理性质的良好机会。我们尤其关注地壳弹性，并考察 $s$-模和 $i$-模频率。我们发现，对于由核饱和参数指定的给定 EOS，至少当较高密度区域中的 EOS 仅能由特定的恒定声速表征时，与半径成比例缩放的 $s$-模频率和与质量成比例缩放的 $i$-模频率可以表示为恒星致密度的函数，而与核心 EOS 的刚度无关。随后，我们可以推导出 $i=1-4$ 时 $s_i$-模频率和 $i_1$-模频率的拟合公式，将其表示为恒星致密度以及对称核物质不可压缩系数 $K_0$ 与核对称能斜率参数 $L$ 的组合的函数。如果观测到 $s$-模和/或 $i$-模振荡频率，则可以利用我们的拟合公式，通过将观测频率识别为特定模式，得到这些性质之间的关系，从而为核饱和参数和恒星性质提供有用见解。通过结合其他观测数据，可能可以缩小 EOS 参数和恒星性质的参数空间。我们强调，本文总结了我们先前研究中给出的结果，并讨论了它们对中子星星震学的意义。

查看原文标题与摘要

Elastic polar oscillations and nuclear parameters

Owing to the elasticity of a neutron star, the shear and interface modes are excited, in addition to the fundamental, pressure, and gravity modes. While direct observations of the oscillation spectrum remain challenging, potential resonance signatures from these modes could be manifested in the precursors of short gamma-ray bursts from binary neutron star mergers. If the oscillation spectrum is observed, this is a good opportunity to extract the physical properties through the technique of asteroseismology. In particular, we focus on the crustal elasticity and examine the $s$- and $i$-mode frequencies. We find that, for a given EOS specified by the nuclear saturation parameters, the radius-scaled $s$-mode frequencies and the mass-scaled $i$-mode frequencies can be expressed as a function of the stellar compactness, independently of the stiffness of the core EOS, at least when the EOS in a higher-density region can be characterized only by the specific constant sound velocity. Then, we could derive the fitting formula for the $s_i$-mode for $i=1-4$ and $i_1$-mode frequencies as a function of the stellar compactness and the combination of the incompressibility of symmetric nuclear matter, $K_0$, and the slope parameter for the nuclear symmetry energy, $L$. If the $s$- and/or $i$-mode oscillation frequencies were observed, one may get the relation between these properties by identifying the observed frequencies with specific modes, using our fitting formula, which offers useful insights into the nuclear saturation parameters and stellar properties. By combining the other observational data, it may be possible to narrow the parameter space of EOS parameters and stellar properties. We emphasize that this article summarizes the results presented in our previous studies and discusses their implications for neutron star seismology.

查看原文标题与摘要

A multiscale composition method for tracking electron bunches from periodically nanostructured photocathodes

Nanoholes and other periodic structures on a photocathode can enhance emission by increasing optical absorption and concentrating light. The curvature of a nanohole also alters electron emission directions and the local accelerating field. For the injector considered here, resolving holes of 200 nm nominal width across a laser spot 0.87 mm in diameter is computationally not feasible. We propose a multiscale composition method for tracking electron bunches from nanostructured photocathodes through rf injectors using conventional beam dynamics codes. For a cathode patterned with a 41x41 array of nanoholes, the method reproduces the transverse phase space obtained by resolving every hole in WarpX. The projected emittances agree to within 0.1% in both planes. We then apply this method to calculate the dynamics of a 100 pC bunch in an rf photoinjector with solenoid focusing. For a flat cathode with smooth emission across the same laser spot, the central-slice emittances at 5 m are about 15% above the nominal thermal emittance. The predicted central-slice emittances for the nanostructured cathode exceed those for the flat cathode by 13% horizontally and 19% vertically. The vertical emittance in this slice is modestly larger than the horizontal. No periodic modulation is visible in the transverse phase-space projections of the whole bunch.

Saturation effects in two and three jet production processes at LH$\mu$C

Motivated by recent proposal of high energy $\mu^+$-hadron collider at CERN, LH$\mu$C, which could be a natural extension of the LH$e$C at the end of the HL-LHC stage, we have studied expected gluon saturation effects in two and three jet production processes at DIS; these processes are sensitive to the most basic non-perturbative structure parametrizing hadronic content of small-$x$ gluons, the Weizs\"acker-Williams (WW) TMD gluon distribution. For the nominal center-of-mass energy of LH$\mu$C $\sqrt{s}=5.3$~TeV we have computed a large selection of observables using the ITMD framework in Mote Carlo setting, both for proton and lead target. In the context of future data-driven precise restrictions of the WW TMD gluon distribution, we have computed several azimuthal angle correlations, including the correlations between pairs of jets as well as the outgoing $\mu^+$ and jet plane. We have also computed nuclear modification factors, that show a significant suppression, ranging locally between 30%-40%, depending on the observable.

来自相对论流体动力学精确解的Lévy形飞米尺度关联源

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

高能物理的一个重要目标是更好地理解重离子碰撞中产生的粒子发射源的时空结构。在不足一秒的时间内，会产生一种特殊形式的物质，即所谓的夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP），可使用飞米尺度关联学工具对其进行研究。通过测量关联函数，已经确定L\'evy分布能够最准确地描述冻结时强子（π介子）发射源的形状；然而，流体动力学的解析解通常给出高斯源，而幂律尾通常被归因于强子阶段，而非流体动力学本身。在本文中，我们展示了相对论流体动力学方程的一个精确解，旨在通过直接从流体动力学给出L\'evy分布来解决这一难题。详细分析揭示了该解的预言是否与观测相符；特别是，分析了由角度平均横质量分布得到的有效温度，以及由双粒子动量关联函数得到的飞米尺度关联半径。

查看原文标题与摘要

Levy-shaped femtoscopic sources from an exact solution of relativistic hydrodynamics

An important goal of high-energy physics is to better understand the spacetime structure of particle emitting sources created in heavy-ion collisions. For a fraction of a second, a special form of matter, the so-called quark-gluon plasma (QGP), is created, which can be examined with the toolkit of femtoscopy. With the measurement of correlation functions it was determined that L\'evy distributions describe the shape of hadron (pion) emission sources at freeze-out the most accurately; however, analytic solutions of hydrodynamics typically yield Gaussian sources, and power-law tails are usually attributed to the hadronic stage rather than to the fluid dynamics itself. In this article we showcase an exact solution of the equations of relativistic hydrodynamics, which aims to resolve this puzzle by providing L\'evy distributions directly from hydrodynamics. A detailed analysis reveals whether the predictions of this solution are in accordance with observations; in particular, the effective temperatures from angle-averaged transverse mass distributions and the femtoscopic radii from two-particle momentum correlation functions are analyzed.

查看原文标题与摘要

Azimuthal Correlations of Heavy-Quark Pairs in Ultraperipheral Collisions

We study azimuthal correlations of $D\bar D$ and $B\bar B$ pairs in Xn0n-selected Pb--Pb ultraperipheral collisions at $\sqrt{s}=5.36~\mathrm{TeV}$. The selection requires neutron emission from the target nucleus and no neutron emission from the photon source. The unpolarized and linearly polarized Weizs\"acker--Williams gluon transverse-momentum-dependent distributions are modeled in the Color Glass Condensate (CGC) framework and evolved to the hard scale. We keep the full mass dependence of the Born coefficients and the one-gluon soft current. The photon flux includes the neutron selection and is averaged over the target area. We present the transverse-momentum and rapidity dependence of the $\cos(2\phi_h)$ and $\cos(4\phi_h)$ asymmetries. Over the ranges studied, both moments increase with the pair total transverse momentum and decrease with its relative transverse momentum. For the lowest relative transverse momentum interval considered, the $D\bar D$ moments are larger than those for $B\bar B$. In the kinematic region considered, the $\cos(2\phi_h)$ asymmetry is much larger than the $\cos(4\phi_h)$ asymmetry, and the contribution from gluon linear polarization is small compared with the total angular modulation.

Super-Eddington Neutron Star Accretion Columns: Comparing GRRMHD Simulations with Spectral Models

The geometry and structure of neutron star accretion columns affect the X-ray emission in super-Eddington accreting systems. We perform four axisymmetric general relativistic radiation magnetohydrodynamic simulations of super-Eddington accretion onto magnetized neutron stars with the maximum surface magnetic fields ranging from $3\times10^{10}$ to $1\times10^{11}\,\mathrm{G}$. We measure the column width and estimate the characteristic radiation shock heights from the flow velocity and velocity gradient. Across the four models, the angular width decreases from approximately $0.14$ to $0.07\,\mathrm{rad}$, while the estimated shock height increases from $1.95$ to $3.89\,\mathrm{km}$. We then compare our solutions to the best-fit values derived from spectral modeling of super-Eddington Be/X-ray binary outbursts. We calculate area-equivalent column radii that vary between 5.2 and 3.2 km, three of which fall within the upper part of the range inferred from spectral results using the \texttt{bwcycl} model.

Strange quark star II: the minimal and maximal gravitational mass and the Keplerian configuration

We employ the MIT bag model with density-dependent bag constant for the equation of state (EOS) to estimate the gravitational mass and Keplerian frequency of rapidly rotating strange quark stars (SQS). In a companion paper we discuss the structural parameters of such rotating stars under the influence of strong magnetic fields. We use the LORENE library to compute the structural parameters at different rotational frequencies in the range of 1100-1300~Hz for a non-magnetized SQS. While there is no minimum limit for the mass of slowly rotating self-bound stars, by computing the maximum rotational frequency, known as the mass-shedding limit, we show that SQS must have a minimum mass to sustain high rotational frequencies. The mass-shedding frequency in our EOS model is lower than that estimated from the MIT bag model EOS with a fixed bag constant. The Keplerian frequency in our model depends linearly on the gravitational mass at the mass-shedding limit (and similarly on the minimum mass) with the slope of 0.08~${\rm kHz}/M_\odot$. We obtain mass limits aligned with the observational data for both the heaviest and the lightest observed pulsars.

查看原文标题与摘要

Strange quark star I: the maximum gravitational mass and deformation of magnetized spinning model

We investigate the structural parameters of strange quark stars (SQS) under the influence of strong magnetic fields and varying rotational frequencies. The equation of state is computed using the MIT bag model with a density-dependent bag constant and considering the Landau quantization effect regarding the strong magnetic fields up to $5\times10^{17}\,$G in the interior of SQS. Employing the LORENE library, we calculate the structural parameters under different magnetic field strengths and rotational frequencies. Our models are compared in terms of maximum gravitational mass, deformation parameter, binding energy, and compactness. Our equation of state model demonstrates that the gravitational masses are higher than those computed using a MIT bag model with a fixed bag constant. We find the gravitational masses beyond $2.3 \,M_\odot$, which are compatible with the masses of observed compact objects, such as the ``black widow'' pulsar \emph{PSR J0952-0607}, and the \emph{GW190814} event detected by the LIGO/Virgo collaboration. The deformation parameter and maximum gravitational mass of SQS are characterized by fitted functions accounting for variations in both magnetic field strength and rotational frequency. We find the maximum deformation parameter of 1.55 and the maximum gravitational mass of $2.8\, M_\odot$ in the fast-rotating strongly magnetized model.

致密球形恒星的临界爱丁顿光度和光子冷却

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

背景。对半径大于 $3MG/c^2$ 的中子星，在 X 射线暴研究中已采用光度的相对论修正。然而，并不存在排除具有流体或固体物理表面的更致密大质量天体存在的基本限制。目的。我们旨在讨论表面位于不稳定圆形光子轨道球面（光子球）以内的球形恒星的临界“爱丁顿”光度。方法。我们采用 Schwarzschild 度规中的标准解析光线追迹公式。结果。我们发现，对于光子球以内的恒星，辐射力与引力吸引达到平衡的爱丁顿条件会因返回表面的光子而发生根本改变。这种平衡是非局域的，并且要求在光子回落的时间尺度上保持定态。因此，可能发生自发对称性破缺，导致整个光球表面的辐射不均匀。结论。对于固定的内禀光度，返回表面的光子会降低位于自身光子球以内的恒星的冷却速率。同时，返回的光子会提高能够平衡引力的临界光度值。对于恒星的任意半径，远处观测者所见的临界光度均适用同一个经红移修正的公式。在 X 射线暴文献中，对于半径小于 3 个引力半径的恒星，长期以来一直采用另一个公式，而该公式遗漏了沿逃逸锥以外方向发射的光子的关键物理。

查看原文标题与摘要

Critical Eddington luminosity and photon cooling of compact spherical stars

Context. Relativistic corrections to the luminosity have been used for neutron stars of radius greater than $3MG/c^2$ in studies of X-ray bursts. However, there is no fundamental limit that excludes the existence of more compact massive objects with a physical surface, fluid or solid. Aims. We aim to discuss critical "Eddington" luminosity for spherical stars that have a surface within the sphere of unstable circular photon orbits (photon sphere). Methods. We use standard analytical ray-tracing formulae in the Schwarzschild metric. Results. We find that for stars within the photon sphere, the Eddington condition for balance between radiative force and gravitational attraction is fundamentally altered by photons returning to the surface. The equilibrium is non-local and requires stationarity on timescales of photon fall-back. As a result, spontaneous symmetry breaking is possible, leading to non-uniformity of emission across the photospheric surface. Conclusions. For a fixed intrinsic luminosity, the photons returning to the surface decrease the cooling rate of stars inside their photon sphere. At the same time, the returning photons increase the value of the critical luminosity that would balance gravity. One and the same redshifted formula applies to the critical luminosity, as seen by a distant observer, for any radius of the star. In the X-ray burst literature, for stars with radius less than 3 gravitational radii a different formula has long been accepted that missed the essential physics of the photons emitted in directions outside the escape cone.

由 Os、Ir 和 Pt 的紫外与光学谱线推导的化学丰度的荟萃分析，以及对贫金属星中第三个 r-过程峰稳健性的重新评估

我们重新评估第三个快中子俘获过程（r-过程）峰处元素（Os、Ir、Pt）与镧系元素 Eu 之间的丰度比。我们表明，Os I、Ir I 和 Pt I 的一些弱光学谱线给出的丰度与较强的紫外（UV）和光学谱线不一致，并就这些弱谱线与更可靠谱线并用时的有限使用提出建议。我们对 35 项研究中 77 颗 r-过程增强贫金属星的 Os、Ir 和 Pt 丰度进行荟萃分析，这些恒星涵盖 -3.43 < [Fe/H] < -1.27 和 +0.22 < [Eu/Fe] < +2.45。第三峰元素与镧系元素的平均丰度比分别为 $\log\varepsilon$(Os/Eu) = 0.86 $\pm$ 0.02、$\log\varepsilon$(Ir/Eu) = 0.88 $\pm$ 0.03 和 $\log\varepsilon$(Pt/Eu) = 1.12 $\pm$ 0.04，它们与推断的太阳系 r-过程丰度比之差在 0.05 dex 以内。在现有样本中富集贫金属星的 r-过程事件里，有百分之 90 的事件中，这些丰度比的变化分别小于 0.50 dex（3.2 倍）、0.56 dex（3.6 倍）和 0.45 dex（2.8 倍）。我们使用一个有代表性的 r-过程模型表明，恒星丰度比的较小变化范围更支持富中子物质有显著贡献的模型，其中电子丰度 $Y_e$ < 0.2。我们得出结论，在现有的 r-过程增强恒星样本中，第三峰元素与镧系元素的丰度比具有稳健性。在两颗 [Eu/Fe] < 0 的恒星中，由未探测到 Pt 而推导出的上限表明，第三峰元素相对于镧系元素是亏缺的，我们得出结论，这可能是有限 r-过程的特征。

A Meta-analysis of Chemical Abundances Derived from Ultraviolet and Optical Lines of Os, Ir, and Pt, and a Reassessment of the Robustness of the Third r-process Peak in Metal-poor Stars

We reassess the abundance ratio between elements at the third rapid neutron-capture process (r-process) peak (Os, Ir, Pt) and the lanthanide element Eu. We show that some weak optical lines of Os I, Ir I, and Pt I yield abundances inconsistent with stronger ultraviolet (UV) and optical lines and provide recommendations for their limited use alongside more reliable lines. We perform a meta-analysis of Os, Ir, and Pt abundances from 35 studies of 77 r-process-enhanced metal-poor stars spanning -3.43 < [Fe/H] < -1.27 and +0.22 < [Eu/Fe] < +2.45. The mean third-peak to lanthanide abundance ratios are $\log\varepsilon$(Os/Eu) = 0.86 $\pm$ 0.02, $\log\varepsilon$(Ir/Eu) = 0.88 $\pm$ 0.03, and $\log\varepsilon$(Pt/Eu) = 1.12 $\pm$ 0.04, which agree with the inferred Solar System r-process ratios to within 0.05 dex. These abundance ratios vary by less than 0.50 dex (a factor of 3.2), 0.56 dex (a factor of 3.6), and 0.45 dex (a factor of 2.8), respectively, in 90 percent of the r-process events that enriched metal-poor stars in existing samples. We use a representative r-process model to show that the small range of stellar abundance ratios favor models with a substantial contribution from neutron-rich material, with electron fractions, $Y_e$, < 0.2. We conclude that the third-peak to lanthanide abundance ratios are robust in existing r-process-enhanced star samples. In two stars with [Eu/Fe] < 0, the upper limits derived from non-detection of Pt indicate that the third-peak elements are deficient relative to lanthanides, and we conclude that this may be a signature of the limited r-process.

Toward divertor plasma detachment in pure D-T plasma in fusion reactor with H-mode-like plasma confinement

The conditions between the separatrix plasma density and the heat flux into the scrape-off layer region allowing reaching an ultimate divertor plasma detachment with H-mode confinement in pure hydrogen (deuterium or the mixture of deuterium and tritium) plasma are obtained. Estimates for a number of present-day tokamaks show that this regime is accessible at separatrix plasma densities below or around the empirical density limit. Moreover, they are in reasonable agreement with the data from the AUG tokamak, where detachment was reached in pure deuterium plasma. However, in future reactors, such as ITER, ARC, and EU-DEMO, it would require densities exceeding this empirical density limit. Therefore, additional measures are needed for future reactors, such as operation above the density limit, which, according to recent experimental data, may not be a hard operational boundary, turbulence induced broadening of the heat flux width and reduction of the parallel heat flux in the long legs of advanced divertor configurations. Their successful implementation would open the possibility for a more efficient reactor design.

用于核反应模型高维贝叶斯校准的可微仿真器

原始发布日期：2026-11-23

耦合道反应模型的贝叶斯校准需要进行许多计算代价高昂的求解，而哈密顿蒙特卡洛所需的似然梯度在大多数生产级求解器中均不可用。我提出 DREAM（用于马尔可夫链推断的具有自动梯度的可微约化基仿真器），这是一种无需对原始求解器求导即可使约化模型可微的策略：依赖参数的算符和解快照在线下导出，通过奇异值分解压缩，并用可微运算重建，因此每次似然评估都会返回观测量以及仿真似然的精确梯度。该构造适用于离散化线性模型，其中参数依赖性可采用低秩、低维算符表示；直接求解器计算在使用约化模型的后验点处对其进行验证。将其应用于 20 MeV 下 $d$+$^{58}$Ni 弹性散射的连续态离散化耦合道计算，其中十八个中心势参数由十二个采样坐标承载，四条链在一台图形处理器上不到一小时便得到 16000 个保留样本，线下投入相当于约一千次直接求解。在所述反应模型、连续态离散化、先验和误差设定范围内，数据将实部与虚部体积分之和分别确定为 $772\pm13$ 和 $204\pm14$ MeV fm$^3$：实部积分比 Koning-Delaroche 参考值之和低 15%，且在将设定的实验误差整体重缩放为原来的两倍时保持稳定，而每个总和的质子-中子分解仍未确定。

查看原文标题与摘要

Differentiable emulation for high-dimensional Bayesian calibration of nuclear reaction models

Bayesian calibration of a coupled-channel reaction model requires many expensive solves, and the likelihood gradients needed by Hamiltonian Monte Carlo are absent from most production solvers. I present DREAM (Differentiable Reduced-basis Emulator with Automatic gradients for Markov-chain inference), a strategy that makes a reduced model differentiable without differentiating the original solver: parameter-dependent operators and solution snapshots are exported offline, compressed by singular value decomposition, and reconstructed with differentiable operations, so each likelihood evaluation returns the observables together with the exact gradient of the emulated likelihood. The construction applies to discretized linear models whose parameter dependence admits a low-rank, low-dimensional operator representation, and direct solver calculations verify the reduced model at the posterior points where it is used. Applied to continuum-discretized coupled-channels calculations of $d$+$^{58}$Ni elastic scattering at 20 MeV, with eighteen central-potential parameters carried by twelve sampled coordinates, four chains yield 16000 retained samples in under an hour on one graphics processor, with an offline investment equivalent to about one thousand direct solves. Within the stated reaction model, continuum discretization, prior, and error assignment, the data determine the summed real and imaginary volume integrals as $772\pm13$ and $204\pm14$ MeV fm$^3$: the real integral lies 15% below the summed Koning-Delaroche reference and is stable under a global factor-of-two rescaling of the assigned experimental errors, while the proton-neutron decomposition of each sum is left undetermined.

用于核反应量子模拟的复合费米子态在第一量子化映射中的反对称化

原始发布日期：2026-11-18

我提出了一种第一量子化确定性算法，用于对空间上分离且分别包含$N_T$和$N_p$个全同费米子的靶核-抛射体系统进行反对称化。该方法从两个独立反对称化的多体态的乘积构造完全反对称的波函数，其中每个多体态都可以是Slater行列式的叠加。该算法使用一个Dicke态辅助寄存器，相干编码两个子系统之间的所有单粒子交换通道；关键在于，它仅需单粒子交换即可生成完整的反对称结构。在使用逻辑或运算合并不同的控制条件后，优化电路使用$N_TN_p$次单粒子交换，其中最多$N_p$次可以并行执行。通过在$N_T$个辅助量子位上施加$Z$门来引入正确的费米子相位，随后使用紧凑的受控运算序列高效地反计算辅助寄存器。该构造为在反应和散射模拟中制备完全反对称态提供了一种非平凡且可扩展的方案，显著扩展了第一量子化量子算法可处理的系统范围。

Antisymmetrization of composite fermionic states for quantum simulations of nuclear reactions in first-quantization mapping

I present a first-quantization deterministic algorithm for antisymmetrizing a spatially separated target-projectile system containing $N_T$ and $N_p$ identical fermions, respectively. The method constructs a fully antisymmetric wavefunction from the product of two independently antisymmetrized many-body states, each of which may be a superposition of Slater determinants. The algorithm uses a Dicke-state ancilla register that coherently encodes all one-particle exchange channels between the two subsystems and, crucially, requires only single-particle swaps to generate the full antisymmetric structure. After consolidating distinct control conditions using logical or operations, the optimized circuit uses $N_TN_p$ single-particle exchanges, up to $N_p$ of which can be performed in parallel. The correct fermionic phase is incorporated through the application of $Z$ gates on $N_T$ ancillae, after which the ancilla register is efficiently uncomputed using a compact sequence of controlled operations. This construction provides a nontrivial and scalable protocol for preparing fully antisymmetric states in reaction and scattering simulations, significantly expanding the range of systems that can be addressed with first-quantized quantum algorithms.

探索微观核密度和集体激发对O 16诱发聚变截面的影响

原始发布日期：2026-11-17

背景：重离子聚变反应为研究复杂核系统中的量子隧穿提供了强有力的手段，尤其是在近垒和垒下能区。在这一区域，核结构效应以及相对运动与内禀集体自由度之间的耦合，在塑造势垒分布和聚变截面方面发挥着关键作用。准确模拟这些效应需要可靠的离子-离子相互作用势以及对集体激发的一致处理。目的：本工作的目的是在耦合道方法框架内，将近期发展的半经验和相对论离子-离子相互作用势用于描述现有的$^{16}\mathrm{O}$诱发反应实验聚变激发函数，这些反应涉及多种振动和转动靶原子核。方法：首次采用耦合道代码ccfull及其中新实现的半经验势，即S\~ao Paulo势版本2（SPP2）和相对论巴西核势（BNP）；同时还采用双折叠核-核势，该势由相对论平均场和Dirac-Hartree-Bogoliubov方法获得的基态密度构造，并与M3Y和R3Y有效核子-核子相互作用进行折叠。道耦合仅限于靶原子核的第一个低位集体激发态，形变参数取自实验数据。结果：计算表明，预测的聚变激发函数敏感地依赖于基础核密度中所包含的配对关联处理，尤其是在垒下能区。尽管所考虑的所有相互作用模型都能再现实验数据的总体趋势，但垒下聚变增强的幅度存在差异。在微观密度中纳入配对效应，可使对轻、中、重靶系统聚变截面的描述更加稳定。对振动和转动原子核观察到的增强模式与已确立的集体耦合效应一致。结论：本研究表明，要在耦合道框架内准确描述重离子聚变反应，需要通过微观核密度一致地纳入配对关联，同时采用真实的核子-核子相互作用并考虑与第一个集体激发态的耦合。近期发展的SPP2和BNP势提供了有用的参考基准，而相对论双折叠势则凸显了配对在塑造垒下聚变动力学中的作用。

Exploring the influence of microscopic nuclear densities and collective excitations on O 16 -induced fusion cross sections

Background: Heavy-ion fusion reactions offer a powerful means to investigate quantum tunneling in complex nuclear systems, especially in the near- and sub-barrier energy regions. In this regime, nuclear structure effects and the coupling between relative motion and intrinsic collective degrees of freedom play a crucial role in shaping barrier distributions and fusion cross sections. Accurate modeling of these effects requires reliable ion-ion interaction potentials and consistent treatment of collective excitations.Purpose: The purpose of this work is to apply recently developed semiempirical and relativistic ion-ion interaction potentials to the description of available experimental fusion excitation functions for $^{16}\mathrm{O}$-induced reactions on a variety of vibrational and rotational target nuclei, within the coupled-channels approach.Methods: The coupled-channels code ccfull is employed with newly implemented semiempirical potentials, for the first time, namely the S\~ao Paulo potential version 2 (SPP2) and the relativistic Brazilian nuclear potential (BNP), together with double-folding nucleus-nucleus potentials constructed from ground-state densities obtained within the relativistic mean-field and Dirac-Hartree-Bogoliubov approaches, folded with the M3Y and R3Y effective nucleon-nucleon interactions. Channel couplings are limited to the first low-lying collective excited state of the target nucleus, with deformation parameters taken from experimental data.Results: The calculations show that the predicted fusion excitation functions depend sensitively on the treatment of pairing correlations embodied in the underlying nuclear densities, particularly in the sub-barrier energy region. While all interaction models considered are able to reproduce the general trends of the experimental data, differences emerge in the magnitude of sub-barrier fusion enhancement. The inclusion of pairing effects in the microscopic densities leads to a more stable description of fusion cross sections across light, medium, and heavy target systems. The observed enhancement patterns for vibrational and rotational nuclei are consistent with well established collective coupling effects.Conclusions: The present study demonstrates that an accurate description of heavy-ion fusion reactions within the coupled-channels framework requires a consistent incorporation of pairing correlations through microscopic nuclear densities, along with realistic nucleon nucleon interactions and coupling to the first collective excited state. The recently developed SPP2 and BNP potentials provide useful reference benchmarks, while relativistic double folding potentials highlight the role of pairing in shaping sub-barrier fusion dynamics.

Half-lives of Ta 183 and Ta 185 from electron-induced reactions on natural tungsten

New high-precision measurements of the half-lives of $^{183}\mathrm{Ta}$ and $^{185}\mathrm{Ta}$ are presented. Accurate half-life data are important for testing nuclear-structure models and for providing reliable nuclear-physics input to astrophysical reaction-network calculations. The isotopes were produced via photonuclear reactions involving proton emission induced by bremsstrahlung generated with a 24-MeV electron beam from the linear accelerator at the National Centre for Nuclear Research. The decay of the reaction products was monitored using high-purity germanium detectors, and the half-lives were determined from the time evolution of $\ensuremath{\gamma}$-ray peak-area ratios relative to selected reference transitions over multiple half-life periods. The obtained half-lives are $5.0817\ifmmode\pm\else\textpm\fi{}0.0023\phantom{\rule{4pt}{0ex}}\mathrm{d}$ for $^{183}\mathrm{Ta}$ and $47.936\ifmmode\pm\else\textpm\fi{}0.016\phantom{\rule{4pt}{0ex}}\mathrm{min}$ for $^{185}\mathrm{Ta}$. These results represent the most precise half-life measurements reported to date, improving the precision of the previous measurements by approximately two orders of magnitude.

电子-离子对撞机上的极化原子核 DVCS

电子-离子对撞机（EIC）将使人们能够在前所未有的能量和亮度下开展一系列测量，为研究小 $x_B$ 区域核子和原子核的微观结构提供新机遇。深度虚康普顿散射（DVCS）等排他过程通过广义部分子分布（GPDs）为研究强子的三维结构提供了独特途径，而极化电子束和离子束还使自旋相关结构的详细研究成为可能。本文建立了极化 $^3$He 上相干 DVCS 的模型，并将其应用于 EIC 上 $9\times166$-GeV $e^3$He 碰撞的模拟。利用这一框架，估算了束流自旋不对称性测量以及康普顿形状因子（CFFs）$\mathcal H_{^3\mathrm{He}}$ 和 $\tilde{\mathcal H}_{^3\mathrm{He}}$ 提取所能达到的统计精度。研究发现，EIC 早期数据能够对非极化 CFF $\mathcal H_{^3\mathrm{He}}$ 进行精确的微分测量，并对其实部和虚部提供显著约束。相比之下，要对极化 CFF $\tilde{\mathcal H}_{^3\mathrm{He}}$ 施加有意义的约束，则需要大得多的积分亮度。本文还考察了反冲 $^3$He 原子核的运动学，并讨论了在 EIC 上标记完整原子核并对相干原子核 DVCS 进行完全排他测量所需的先进极前向探测器能力。

Polarized Nuclear DVCS at the Electron-Ion Collider

The Electron-Ion Collider (EIC) will enable a series of measurements at unprecedented energies and luminosities, providing new opportunities to investigate the microscopic structure of nucleons and nuclei at small $x_B$. Exclusive processes such as Deeply Virtual Compton Scattering (DVCS) offer unique access to the three-dimensional structure of hadrons through Generalized Parton Distributions (GPDs), while polarized electron and ion beams further enable detailed studies of spin-dependent structure. A model for coherent DVCS on polarized $^3$Heis developed and applied to simulations of for $9\times166$-GeV $e^3$He collisions at the EIC. Using this framework, the statistical precision achievable is estimated for measurements of beam-spin asymmetries and for the extraction of the Compton Form Factors (CFFs) $\mathcal H_{^3\mathrm{He}}$ and $\tilde{\mathcal H}_{^3\mathrm{He}}$. Early EIC data are found to enable precise differential measurements of the unpolarized CFF $\mathcal H_{^3\mathrm{He}}$ and to provide significant constraints on its real and imaginary components. By contrast, meaningful constraints on the polarized CFF $\tilde{\mathcal H}_{^3\mathrm{He}}$ require substantially larger integrated luminosities. The kinematics of the recoil $^3$He nucleus are also examined, and the advanced far-forward detector capabilities at the EIC required to tag the intact nucleus and perform fully exclusive measurements of coherent nuclear DVCS are discussed.

A large-area array-style cosmic-ray muon radiography detector μ Sight-1

Muon absorption radiography technology has unique advantages over conventional radiation-based techniques (e.g., X/ $$\varvec{\gamma }$$ -ray and neutron radiography), including high inherent source-free penetration capability. This method is typically used for imaging obscured internal structures within large-scale objects. However, muon radiography also encounters challenges because of low flux rates and nondirectional incidence. Advanced muon detection systems can effectively mitigate these limitations. Owing to their low cost and high environmental adaptability, plastic scintillator detectors are widely used in muon absorption radiography. In this work, a large-area planar muon radiography system, $$\varvec{\mu}$$ Sight-1, is developed on the basis of a plastic scintillator array. The detector employs three layers of position-sensitive tracking modules composed of triangular cross-section scintillator units. Scintillation photons are wavelength-shifted through wavelength-shifting fibres and subsequently converted to electrical signals by silicon photomultipliers. A custom-developed data acquisition system processes signals from all 384 scintillator channels concurrently. The effective area of the detector is 1.3 $$\text {m}^2$$ . The detection efficiency exceeds 98.5%, and the position resolution reaches 1.9 mm.

Neutron magicity at N = 8 preserved in 13 B

The 13 B ( p , p n ) 12 B reaction has been used at about 465 MeV/u to probe the neutron Fermi surface of the N = 8 , 13 B nucleus, and its magicity. The 13 B nuclei, produced by the FRS-GSI facility and transmitted to the R 3 B-LAND beam line, have been used to determine the cross sections to bound and unbound states in 12 B by means of their γ -ray detection in the Crystal Ball array and neutron detection in LAND, respectively. The contents of normally occupied 0 p 1 / 2 and valence (intruder) 1 s 1 / 2 and 0 d 5 / 2 orbitals have been determined in 13 B from the population of positive and negative parity states in 12 B, respectively, the J π values of which were previously assigned. States originating from the removal of the more deeply bound 0 p 3 / 2 orbital have also been observed up to an excitation energy of 7.5 MeV. From the small content of intruder 1 s 0 d states of 14.0(40) %, it is deduced that the magicity at N = 8 is remarkably preserved in 13 B, before suddenly collapsing in 12 Be, where the intruder content is by far dominating.

2 H( e , e ′ p ) n studies of exclusive deuteron electrodisintegration

The 2 H(e, e′p)n cross section was measured at momentum transfers Q 2 = 0.8, 2.1 and 3.5 (GeV/c) 2 covering a wide range of proton kinematics at each Q 2 setting that made it possible to study this reaction as a function of missing momentum as well as a function of the neutron laboratory recoil angle θ n q . Missing momentum distributions were determined for fixed values of θ n q up to missing momenta of 0.65 GeV/c. For the two larger momentum transfer settings, the characteristics of the experimental momentum distributions confirm the theoretical prediction that final state interactions (FSI) contribute maximally around a θ n q ∼ 70 ∘ , while for θ n q < 45 ∘ FSI are significantly reduced. The data at reduced FSI settings were best reproduced by calculations using the CD-Bonn potential wave functions.

Constraining the low- p T η / π 0 ratio for direct-photon analyses with blast-wave fits to π , K , and p spectra

We predict the $η/π^0$ ratio at low $p_T$ ($p_T \lesssim 3~\mathrm{GeV}/c$) using the measured charged $K/π$ ratio and model input from a blast-wave framework with feeddown contributions. This approach can provide improved, data-constrained background estimates for direct-photon and dilepton measurements in heavy-ion collisions. In this approach, the explicit modeling of radial flow and hadronic feeddown enables an uncertainty estimate for the low-$p_T$ extrapolation of the $η/π^0$ ratio. Using central Pb-Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=2.76$ TeV as an example, we find that the $η$-related decay-photon uncertainty at $p_T \approx 1~\mathrm{GeV}/c$ is of order 10\% of the expected direct-photon signal.

Thermodynamic and transport properties of the quark-gluon plasma at finite chemical potential with a deep neural network framework

The characteristics of a thermal system depend strongly on its response to thermal gradients and the underlying microscopic interactions among constituents. In the present study, we investigate the thermodynamic and transport properties of the quark-gluon plasma (QGP) at finite baryon chemical potential within a deep-learning-assisted quasiparticle model. The temperature- ( T ) and baryon chemical-potential- ( μ B ) dependent thermal masses of quasiparticles are estimated using neural networks trained to reproduce lattice QCD results for the equation of state, obtained via a Taylor-like expansion around vanishing baryon chemical potential. The trained model acts as an effective emulator, enabling us to estimate the thermodynamic and transport properties at finite μ B . We compute the speed of sound, specific heat, viscosity, and conductivity of the deconfined medium. Our findings are in good agreement with available lattice calculations and other phenomenological models. The present study demonstrates that a deep-neural-network-based approach provides an efficient framework for studying the properties of the QGP at finite baryon density.

查看原文标题与摘要

First evidence of neutrino absorption on argon using 8 B solar neutrinos in DEAP-3600

We report experimental evidence for electron neutrino charged-current interactions (neutrino absorption, CC~ ν e ) from 8 B solar neutrinos on 40 Ar using an exposure of (7.29 ± 0.05)~tonne ⋅ years in the DEAP-3600 detector. A region of interest (ROI) of 10.5–13.0~MeV reconstructed energy calibrated on single-peak events, corresponding to incident neutrino energy in 12.0–14.5~MeV, is used for this measurement. We observe 5 single-peak and 1 double-peak neutrino-like events consistent with the 8 B solar neutrino energy spectrum in the ROI after correcting for nonlinearities in the detector response at high energies. With an expected background of 0.48 − 0.15 + 0.16 events, the background-only hypothesis is rejected at 4.0~ σ . We report an energy-averaged cross section of ( 4.0 − 1.6 + 2.0 ( s t a t ) − 0.7 + 0.8 ( s y s ) ) × 10 − 41 c m 2 in the ROI for the CC~ ν e signal, a factor ( 2.4 − 1.0 + 1.3 ) higher than predicted by Bhattacharya, Goodman and Garc´ia (2009).

双中子星并合中的磁星形成

原始发布日期：2026-09-25

我们在日本超级计算机 FUGAKU 上，以前所未有的 6.25 ,m 空间分辨率，对一个 1.35 - 1.35 M ⊙ 双中子星系统开展了全局广义相对论中微子辐射输运理想磁流体动力学模拟。消耗的 CPU 总时间为 ≈ 530 million 核时。我们将双中子星的磁场初始化为最大 3.16 × 10 12 ~G，这与观测到的双脉冲星磁场上限相符。我们证明，当两颗中子星接触时出现的 Kelvin-Helmholtz 不稳定性，在并合后 5 ~ms 内将磁场放大至预期的 ∼ 10 50 ~erg 总电磁饱和能量。谱分析表明，磁功率谱密度和动能功率谱密度分别再现了 Kazantsev 谱和 Kolmogorov 谱。我们还发现，它使恒星尺度的磁场至少放大到原来的 ≈ 337 倍。我们得出结论，在中子星并合后的几 ms 内，可能形成一颗至少暂时存在的磁星。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Magnetar formation in binary neutron star merger

We conduct a global general relativistic neutrino-radiation-transfer ideal magnetohydrodynamics simulation of a 1.35 - 1.35 M ⊙ binary neutron star with the unprecedented spatial resolution of 6.25 ,m on the Japanese supercomputer FUGAKU. The total consumed CPU time is ≈ 530 million core hours. We initialize the binary neutron star’s magnetic field to be 3.16 × 10 12 ~G at maximum, which is compatible with the upper end of the observed binary pulsars. We demonstrate that the Kelvin-Helmholtz instability that emerges when the two neutron stars touch amplifies the magnetic field to an expected total electromagnetic saturation energy of ∼ 10 50 ~erg within 5 ~ms after the merger. The spectral analysis indicates that the Kazantsev and Kolmogorov spectra are reproduced in the magnetic and kinetic power spectral densities, respectively. We also find that it induces stellar-scale magnetic field amplification by at least a factor of ≈ 337 . We conclude that a magnetar may form at least temporarily following neutron star mergers in a few ms.

接近 $N = 126$ 壳层闭合的富中子 Au 195 – 202 同位素中高自旋态的演化

原始发布日期：2026-09-10

背景：双幻数 $^{208}\mathrm{Pb}$ 西南区域的核结构对于检验与富中子重元素形成以及质量丰度分布中 $A\ensuremath{\approx}195$ 峰起源相关的理论模型十分重要。尽管富中子 Pb、Tl 和 Hg（$Z=80\text{--}82$）同位素得到了相对充分的研究，但受实验挑战所限，富中子 Au 同位素（$Z=79$）的实验数据在很大程度上仍然有限。目的：本工作的目的是研究接近 $N=126$ 壳层闭合的富中子 Au 同位素中高自旋结构的演化，并表征其内在的壳模型组态，尤其关注高-$j$ 唯一宇称 $\ensuremath{\pi}{h}_{11/2}$ 和 $\ensuremath{\nu}{i}_{13/2}$ 轨道的作用。方法：在 7 MeV/核子的束流能量下，利用 $^{198}\mathrm{Pt}(^{136}\mathrm{Xe}, ^{x}\mathrm{I})^{y}\mathrm{Au}$ 多核子转移反应产生富中子 $^{195\text{--}202}\mathrm{Au}$ 同位素。采用一种独特的实验装置，对经同位素鉴定的反应产物进行瞬发和延迟 $\ensuremath{\gamma}$ 射线谱学测量；该装置结合了 $\mathrm{VAMOS}++$ 大接收度磁谱仪、AGATA 高纯锗 $\ensuremath{\gamma}$ 射线径迹追踪阵列和 CATLIFE 探测系统。结果：在偶-$A ^{196,198,200,202}\mathrm{Au}$ 同位素中，首次观测到建立在 ${12}^{\ensuremath{-}}$ 同核异能态之上的瞬发 $\ensuremath{\gamma}$ 射线跃迁。建立在 ${11}^{\ensuremath{-}}$ 态之上的奇-$J$ 带在 $A\ensuremath{\ge}196$ 时消失。在奇-$A ^{195,197,199,201}\mathrm{Au}$ 同位素中，鉴定出位于 $11/{2}^{\ensuremath{-}}$ 同核异能态之上的新瞬发和延迟 $\ensuremath{\gamma}$ 射线跃迁，证实并扩展了已知能级纲图。在 $^{199}\mathrm{Au}$ 和 $^{201}\mathrm{Au}$ 中确立了指认为 ($25/{2}^{+}$) 的新同核异能态。作为 $N$ 的函数，建立在 $11/{2}^{\ensuremath{-}}$ 和 ${12}^{\ensuremath{-}}$ 同核异能态之上的能带，其激发能极小值出现在 $^{198}\mathrm{Au}$（$N=119$），相对于原本平滑的演化呈现一个下凹。结论：基于 Kuo-Herling 空穴有效相互作用的大规模壳模型计算总体上与实验能级纲图符合良好。然而，目前的计算未能再现 Au 和 Hg 同位素中均观测到的 $N=119$ 激发能极小值。对 $\ensuremath{\pi}{h}_{11/2}$ 和 $\ensuremath{\nu}{i}_{13/2}$ 空穴占据数以及质子和中子的自旋贡献进行系统分析，阐明了这些高-$j$ 唯一宇称轨道在高自旋态产生过程中的作用。本工作的部分结果已在另一篇出版物中报道 [Y. Cho et al., Phys. Lett. B 878, 140558 (2026)]。

查看原文标题与摘要

Evolution of high-spin states in neutron-rich Au 195 – 202 isotopes approaching the N = 126 shell closure

Background: The nuclear structure in the region southwest of the doubly magic $^{208}\mathrm{Pb}$ is important to benchmark theoretical models relevant for neutron-rich heavy element formation and for the origin of the $A\ensuremath{\approx}195$ peak in the mass abundance distribution. Although neutron-rich Pb, Tl, and Hg ($Z=80\text{--}82$) isotopes are relatively well studied, experimental data for neutron-rich Au isotopes ($Z=79$) remain largely limited due to experimental challenges.Purpose: The purpose of this work is to investigate the evolution of the high-spin structure in neutron-rich Au isotopes near the $N=126$ shell closure and to characterize the underlying shell-model configurations, with a particular focus on the roles of the high-$j$ unique-parity $\ensuremath{\pi}{h}_{11/2}$ and $\ensuremath{\nu}{i}_{13/2}$ orbitals.Methods: Neutron-rich $^{195\text{--}202}\mathrm{Au}$ isotopes were produced using multinucleon transfer reactions of $^{198}\mathrm{Pt}(^{136}\mathrm{Xe}, ^{x}\mathrm{I})^{y}\mathrm{Au}$ at a beam energy of 7 MeV/nucleon. Prompt and delayed $\ensuremath{\gamma}$-ray spectroscopy of isotopically identified reaction products was performed with a unique experimental setup combining the $\mathrm{VAMOS}++$ large acceptance magnetic spectrometer, the AGATA high-purity germanium $\ensuremath{\gamma}$-ray tracking array, and the CATLIFE detection system.Results: In even-$A ^{196,198,200,202}\mathrm{Au}$ isotopes, prompt $\ensuremath{\gamma}$-ray transitions built on the ${12}^{\ensuremath{-}}$ isomer have been observed for the first time. The odd-$J$ band built on the ${11}^{\ensuremath{-}}$ state vanishes for $A\ensuremath{\ge}196$. In odd-$A ^{195,197,199,201}\mathrm{Au}$ isotopes, new prompt and delayed $\ensuremath{\gamma}$-ray transitions above the $11/{2}^{\ensuremath{-}}$ isomer were identified, confirming and extending the known level schemes. New isomeric states assigned to ($25/{2}^{+}$) have been established in $^{199}\mathrm{Au}$ and $^{201}\mathrm{Au}$. The minima in the excitation energies of the bands as a function of $N$ built on the $11/{2}^{\ensuremath{-}}$ and ${12}^{\ensuremath{-}}$ isomers occur at $^{198}\mathrm{Au}$ ($N=119$), showing a dip against an otherwise smooth evolution.Conclusions: Large-scale shell-model calculations based on the Kuo-Herling hole effective interaction show an overall good agreement with experimental level schemes. However, the present calculations do not reproduce the minima in excitation energies at $N=119$ observed in both Au and Hg isotopes. A systematic analysis of $\ensuremath{\pi}{h}_{11/2}$ and $\ensuremath{\nu}{i}_{13/2}$ hole occupancies, together with spin contributions of protons and neutrons, sheds light on the role of these high-$j$ unique-parity orbitals in the generation of high-spin states. Part of the results of this work have been reported in another publication [Y. Cho et al., Phys. Lett. B 878, 140558 (2026)].

在凝聚态物理学中，长程关联会引入挑战直觉的物质量子态。例如，超固体结合了表现为对称性破缺空间排列的密度有序与无摩擦的超流流动。然而，事实证明，超固体仅在非常严格的条件下存在，相关证据仅限于在弱相互作用区观察到的少数自发碎裂超流体。在此，我们通过将偶极玻色子限制在具有长程跃迁的晶格中，展示了一种在强相互作用区实现晶态超固体的框架。我们表明，偶极激子可实现这一晶格模型。在四分之一、三分之一和二分之一的分数晶格填充下，我们在约 100 个格点上观察到自发破坏晶格平移对称性的介观量子晶体。同时，相干长程跃迁诱导非对角长程序，从而使激子固体成为超流体。我们的数值方法定量证实，超固态在晶格哈密顿量的基态中逐渐形成。此前已在弱相互作用情形中观察到超固体。现在，在受限于具有长程跃迁的晶格中的偶极激子强关联系统里，已展示出若干超固相。

查看原文标题与摘要

Supersolid crystals of dipolar excitons in a lattice

In condensed-matter physics, long-range correlations introduce quantum states of matter that challenge intuition. For example, supersolids combine density order that manifests as symmetry-breaking spatial arrangement, and frictionless superfluid flow. However, supersolids have proven to exist only under very stringent conditions, with evidence limited to a few spontaneously fragmented superfluids observed in the weakly interacting regime. Here we demonstrate a framework to realize crystalline supersolids in the strong-interaction regime, by confining dipolar bosons in a lattice with long-range hopping. We show that dipolar excitons realize this lattice model. At fractional lattice fillings of one-quarter, one-third and one-half, we observe mesoscopic quantum crystals across around 100 sites that spontaneously break the lattice translational symmetry. At the same time, coherent long-range hopping induces off-diagonal long-range order such that the exciton solids are superfluids. Our numerical methods quantitatively confirm that supersolidity builds up in the ground state of the lattice Hamiltonian. Supersolids have previously been observed in weakly interacting scenarios. Now several supersolid phases are demonstrated in a strongly correlated system of dipolar excitons confined to a lattice with long-range hopping.

固态氖上相互作用电子量子比特的相干操控

Coherent manipulation of interacting electron qubits on solid neon

Electrons trapped on solid neon surfaces can serve as low-noise charge qubits with full electrical control and a compact device footprint. However, quantum information processing requires the implementation of two-qubit gates, which has been difficult to achieve in this system. Here we report the coherent manipulation of multiple interacting electron-on-solid-neon charge qubits. Using electrons that are naturally confined in close proximity on the neon surface, we demonstrate strong qubit–qubit coupling and implement the cross-resonance and bSWAP types of two-qubit operations using global microwave drives. Our results indicate that engineering electron confinement on solid neon may enhance control over the qubit–qubit interactions and yield a compact and scalable quantum computing architecture. Electrons on the surface of solid neon have been established as high-quality individual qubits. Now two-qubit gates have been demonstrated using the strong coupling between electrons confined in close proximity to each other.
